古希腊 / Ancient Greek

得墨忒耳寻找珀耳塞福涅Demeter and Persephone

珀耳塞福涅被带往冥界后，得墨忒耳离开诸神寻找女儿；本页严格以《荷马颂歌·致得墨忒耳》的见证范围叙述。

After Persephone is taken below, Demeter leaves the gods to search for her daughter; this page stays within the witness of the Homeric Hymn to Demeter.

  • 冥界
  • 厄琉息斯
  • 季节

古希腊赞歌的数字文本见证
01

女儿被带走The daughter is taken

赞歌开篇把珀耳塞福涅置于被带往冥界的事件中心，并说哈得斯在本见证中得到宙斯同意。

The hymn opens with Persephone at the center of the taking to the underworld and says that Hades acts with the consent of Zeus in this witness.

Evidence / 证据《荷马颂歌·致得墨忒耳》1–32行。claim.v050.h2.hades_participated这里只陈述本赞歌的叙事，不代表所有希腊版本。
02

得墨忒耳寻找Demeter searches

得墨忒耳离开诸神寻找女儿。赫卡忒与赫利俄斯出现在寻找段落中，承担的角色以已登记的赞歌行号为限。

Demeter leaves the gods and searches for her daughter. Hecate and Helios appear in the search passage, with their roles limited to the registered hymn lines.

Evidence / 证据赞歌1–87行及25–62行的已登记Claims。claim.v050.h2.demeter_participated未把其他作者的寻找情节混入。
03

厄琉息斯与回返框架Eleusis and the return framework

事件档案记录得墨忒耳来到厄琉息斯；赞歌继续处理珀耳塞福涅的回返安排及仪式建立。秘密仪式的内部内容没有从公开证据重构。

The event record brings Demeter to Eleusis; the hymn continues with arrangements for Persephone to return and the establishment of rites. The internal content of the mysteries is not reconstructed from public evidence.

Evidence / 证据赞歌事件索引与厄琉息斯资料层。claim.v050.h2.event_appears仪式内部内容明确保留为未知。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

原创中文概述；不复制现代译文，不把后世版本并入本见证。

古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian

奥佩特节的公开仪仗路线Public route of the Opet procession

埃及文物主管部门的卢克索神庙档案记录：阿蒙、穆特和孔苏的神像由卡纳克出发，沿仪仗联系抵达卢克索神庙。本页只叙述可公开核对的路线与纪念物层。

The Egyptian antiquities authority records the cult images of Amun, Mut, and Khonsu leaving Karnak and reaching Luxor Temple along a processional connection. This page covers only the public route and monument layer.

  • 仪仗路线

现代埃及官方遗址档案对古代祭典路线与纪念物的公开说明
Open source / 打开来源
01

从卡纳克出发Departure from Karnak

官方档案将奥佩特节的公开进程起点放在卡纳克诸神庙，并点名阿蒙、穆特与孔苏的神像。

The official record places the public procession’s departure at the Karnak temples and names the cult images of Amun, Mut, and Khonsu.

Evidence / 证据埃及文物主管部门卢克索神庙档案。claim.v070.egypt.opet_associated_karnak当前来源未给出每一尊神像在每一历史时期的完整次序。
02

仪仗联系The processional connection

卡纳克与卢克索之间由一条两侧有斯芬克斯像的仪仗道相连；本数据库不凭描述自行绘制精确古路线。

Karnak and Luxor were connected by a sphinx-bordered processional way; the database does not infer an exact ancient route geometry from this description.

Evidence / 证据官方遗址档案第一个说明段。claim.v0260.opet_way_sphinxes当前证据未说明各段统一的建造日期与完整保存状态。
03

抵达卢克索Arrival at Luxor

神像被运往卢克索神庙；官方说明把此行解释为拜访当地的阿蒙涅姆奥佩特。

The cult images were transported to Luxor Temple; the official account interprets the visit as directed to Amenemopet there.

Evidence / 证据官方卢克索神庙奥佩特段落。claim.v0260.opet_visits_amenemopet阿蒙涅姆奥佩特本轮只作来源中的名称保存，不与其他阿蒙形态合并。
04

柱廊保存的图像层Festival scenes on the colonnade

卢克索大神柱廊的装饰包含奥佩特节场景；这是现实纪念物的图像见证，不等于完整祭典脚本。

The Great Colonnade decoration includes scenes of the Opet Festival; this is a monumental visual witness, not a complete ritual script.

Evidence / 证据官方神庙档案的柱廊说明。claim.v0260.opet_colonnade_scenes完整图像学分段与各场景定位仍待专项研究。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

古代祭典、现实遗址与现代官方解释分层；未公开或当前来源未说明的仪式内容不补写。

古希腊 / Ancient Greek

厄琉息斯大祭的公开进程Public route of the Eleusinian procession

雅典卫城博物馆与希腊官方遗产资料共同保存从雅典、圣道到厄琉息斯仪式厅的公开路线层；秘仪内部内容不在此重构。

The Acropolis Museum and Greek official heritage record preserve the public route layer from Athens along the Sacred Way to the Telesterion at Eleusis; the internal mysteries are not reconstructed.

现代希腊博物馆对古代公开宗教进程及其考古语境的说明
Open source / 打开来源
01

雅典的公开起点Public beginning in Athens

博物馆档案说宗教进程从雅典开始，并把雅典厄琉西尼翁出土材料纳入路线展览。

The museum record says the religious procession began in Athens and includes material from the Athenian Eleusinion in the route exhibition.

Evidence / 证据雅典卫城博物馆展览说明。claim.v0260.eleusinion_procession_start当前档案不是逐日祭程表。
02

沿圣道前往厄琉息斯Along the Sacred Way to Eleusis

公开进程由雅典沿圣道通向厄琉息斯；达夫尼阿佛洛狄忒圣所等地点属于道路考古层，不是虚构的冒险节点。

The public procession moved from Athens along the Sacred Way toward Eleusis; sites such as the Aphrodite sanctuary at Daphne belong to the road’s archaeology, not an invented adventure sequence.

Evidence / 证据卫城博物馆路线说明与既有圣道档案。claim.v080.eleusinia_sacredway每一停驻点与日期尚未在当前来源中完整列出。
03

进入厄琉息斯遗址Entering the Eleusinian sanctuary

希腊官方遗产资料说明，圣道在厄琉息斯延续为进程道路，并通向遗址内部。

Greek official heritage information describes the Sacred Way continuing as the Processional Road inside the Eleusinian site.

Evidence / 证据希腊官方遗产页的遗址路线说明。claim.v080.sacredway_eleusis这里只处理可见道路与遗址关系。
04

在仪式厅结束的公开路线Public route culminating at the Telesterion

博物馆档案把公开进程的终点置于厄琉息斯圣所的特勒斯特里翁；这不授权重建保密入会仪式。

The museum record places the public procession’s culmination at the Telesterion in the Eleusinian sanctuary; this does not authorize reconstruction of secret initiation rites.

Evidence / 证据雅典卫城博物馆展览说明。claim.v0260.eleusinia_culminates_telesterion秘仪内部内容：当前公开证据未说明，且系统不补写。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

公开进程与保密入会仪式分开；“当前证据未说明”的部分不会以现代想象补齐。

古希腊 / Ancient Greek

赫西俄德笔下的泰坦战争Titanomachy in Hesiod

宙斯一方与克洛诺斯一方的泰坦交战；本页只呈现《神谱》617–735行的战斗见证。

The gods aligned with Zeus fight the Titans aligned with Cronus; this page presents only Theogony 617–735.

赫西俄德《神谱》的英语数字版定位
Open source / 打开来源
01

两方神族Two divine sides

《神谱》的这一段把与克洛诺斯相连的泰坦置于宙斯及其盟友的对立面。

This passage of the Theogony sets the Titans associated with Cronus against Zeus and his allies.

Evidence / 证据《神谱》617–735行。claim.v050.theogony.cronus_participated_titanomachy“泰坦战争”是现代通行事件名，见证范围仍指向原诗行。
02

雷霆与战斗Thunder and battle

宙斯是战斗的主要参与者；事件档案把雷、闪电和雷电武器记录为本段战斗手段，并记录百臂巨人的介入。

Zeus is a principal combatant; the event profile records thunder, lightning, and the thunderbolt as weapons in this passage and records the intervention of the Hundred-Handers.

Evidence / 证据《神谱》617–735行的事件与参与者层。claim.v050.theogony.zeus_participated_titanomachy没有把后世图像或现代叙事写入古诗。
03

战败与囚禁Defeat and confinement

在这一见证中，泰坦战败并被囚入塔耳塔罗斯；这项结果不自动代表所有古代作者的细节完全相同。

In this witness, the Titans are defeated and confined in Tartarus; the result does not imply that every ancient author gives identical details.

Evidence / 证据《神谱》617–735行及事件结果档案。claim.v050.theogony.titanomachy_appears其他见证将在独立版本中比较。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

以当前登记的《神谱》见证为限。

古希腊 / Ancient Greek

宙斯与提丰之战Zeus and Typhon in Hesiod

《神谱》中的宙斯以雷霆、闪电和雷电武器迎战提丰；其他作者的版本不在本页自动合并。

In the Theogony, Zeus confronts Typhon with thunder, lightning, and the thunderbolt; versions in other authors are not merged here.

01

提丰出现Typhon appears

赫西俄德的段落把提丰置于宙斯的对手位置，并以一场神圣冲突展开。

The Hesiodic passage places Typhon as the opponent of Zeus and unfolds as a divine conflict.

Evidence / 证据《神谱》820–885行。claim.typhon.participated_battle提丰的出身与形貌只在未来逐行版本中扩充。
02

宙斯发动雷霆Zeus unleashes thunder

事件档案记录宙斯以雷、闪电和雷电武器进攻；此处没有把后世宙斯武器造型反投到赫西俄德文本。

The event profile records Zeus attacking with thunder, lightning, and the thunderbolt; later visual forms of the weapon are not projected back into Hesiod.

Evidence / 证据《神谱》820–885行。claim.zeus.participated_typhon武器名与图像史须另行建模。
03

本见证的结局Outcome in this witness

提丰在赫西俄德的战斗段落中被击败；数据库把这个结局限定在该文本见证。

Typhon is defeated in the Hesiodic combat passage; the database scopes this outcome to that textual witness.

Evidence / 证据《神谱》820–885行。claim.typhon.participated_battle其他作者的结局版本未在本页合并。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

单一见证阅读版；后续版本须另建故事版本。

古希腊 / Ancient Greek

美狄亚恢复埃厄忒斯王位Medea restores Aeetes

《书库》1.9.28保存美狄亚回到科尔喀斯、杀死夺位者珀耳塞斯并恢复埃厄忒斯王位的短篇结局。

Library 1.9.28 preserves a short ending in which Medea returns to Colchis, kills the usurper Perses, and restores Aeetes.

伪阿波罗多洛斯神话汇编见证
01

回到科尔喀斯Return to Colchis

《书库》1.9.28把美狄亚写成返回科尔喀斯并采取行动的人物。

Library 1.9.28 presents Medea as the figure who returns to Colchis and acts.

Evidence / 证据《书库》1.9.28。claim.v090.medea_participated_restoration这是汇编中的短序列，不扩写对话或动机。
02

同名者的结局The fate of a namesake

在这一段中，被杀并被废黜的是赫利俄斯之子、埃厄忒斯兄弟的珀耳塞斯，而不是赫卡忒之父的泰坦珀耳塞斯。

The Perses killed and deposed here is the son of Helios and brother of Aeetes, not the Titan Perses who is father of Hecate.

Evidence / 证据《书库》1.9.28与同名实体审计。claim.v090.perses_participated_restoration严格防止同名人物合并。
03

王位恢复Restoration of the throne

短篇以埃厄忒斯恢复王位结束；当前证据没有为这一结局补写更长的政治过程。

The short sequence ends with Aeetes restored to the kingdom; the current evidence does not invent a longer political process.

Evidence / 证据《书库》1.9.28。claim.v090.aeetes_participated_restoration当前证据未说明的过程保持空缺。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

严格区分科尔喀斯的珀耳塞斯与其他同名人物。

古希腊 / Ancient Greek

阿佛洛狄忒的两种出身见证Variant origins of Aphrodite

《神谱》的海中诞生叙事与《伊利亚特》的宙斯—狄俄涅家谱并存；系统不强制选定唯一版本。

The sea-birth account in the Theogony coexists with the Zeus-and-Dione genealogy in the Iliad; the system does not force one version.

01

乌拉诺斯之后After Uranus

《神谱》的出身叙事把阿佛洛狄忒的出现连接到乌拉诺斯受创后落入海中的身体部分。

The Theogony connects the appearance of Aphrodite to what falls into the sea after the wounding of Uranus.

Evidence / 证据《神谱》188–206行。claim.v050.theogony.aphrodite_origin本站以简洁概述处理古代文本中的身体暴力。
02

海中诞生Birth from the sea

这一见证叙述她从海中形成并显现，因此不使用“宙斯之女”作为此版本的出生框架。

This witness narrates her formation and appearance from the sea and therefore does not use the daughter-of-Zeus genealogy as its birth frame.

Evidence / 证据《神谱》188–206行。claim.v050.theogony.aphrodite_origin只说明此版本，不否定其他古代见证。
03

版本边界Version boundary

海中诞生是《神谱》的故事版本；《伊利亚特》的家谱称谓在下一版本单独阅读。

The sea birth is the Theogony version; the genealogy terms in the Iliad are read separately in the next version.

Evidence / 证据冲突档案 conflict.greek.aphrodite_parentage_v050。claim.v050.theogony.aphrodite_origin系统不裁定唯一正确版本。

01

狄俄涅被称为母亲Dione is called mother

《伊利亚特》第5卷写阿佛洛狄忒来到母亲狄俄涅身边。这是亲属称谓见证，不是完整诞生场景。

Iliad Book 5 presents Aphrodite coming to her mother Dione. This is a kinship witness, not a complete birth scene.

Evidence / 证据《伊利亚特》第5卷370–372行。claim.v050.iliad5.aphrodite_child_dione不要从一处称谓推演未叙述的出生过程。
02

宙斯之女Daughter of Zeus

《伊利亚特》第14卷又把阿佛洛狄忒称为宙斯之女，形成与《神谱》海中诞生不同的家谱层。

Iliad Book 14 also calls Aphrodite a daughter of Zeus, creating a genealogical layer different from the sea-birth account in the Theogony.

Evidence / 证据《伊利亚特》第14卷193行。claim.v050.iliad14.aphrodite_child_zeus“女儿”是文本称谓；本页不补写其出生情节。
03

两个古代版本共存Two ancient versions coexist

数据库保留《神谱》与《伊利亚特》的差异，并通过冲突档案让读者并排查看，而不是按名称或现代常识合并。

The database preserves the difference between the Theogony and the Iliad and exposes it through a conflict record instead of merging the accounts by name or modern convention.

Evidence / 证据两个古代文本见证及显式冲突档案。claim.v050.iliad5.dione_mentioned冲突状态保持开放。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

两个古代见证逐段并列；“未陈述”与“已见证”分开，不合成统一出生叙事。

北欧 / Norse

阿斯克与恩布拉：两种北欧见证Ask and Embla in two witnesses

《女巫预言》与《欺骗古鲁菲》都记下最初人类阿斯克和恩布拉，却给出不同的神祇组合；两版分开阅读。

Voluspa and Gylfaginning both name Ask and Embla while presenting different divine triads; the two witnesses are read separately.

阿斯克与恩布拉Ask and Embla

《女巫预言》17–18节在最初人类段落中分别命名阿斯克与恩布拉。

Voluspa 17–18 names Ask and Embla in its first-human episode.

02

诗歌中的三位神The poetic triad

这段诗歌把奥丁、赫尼尔与洛杜尔放在同一创造人类场景中。

The poetic passage places Odin, Hoenir, and Lodurr together in the human-creation scene.

Evidence / 证据《女巫预言》17–18节。claim.v0190.voluspa_triad各神具体赐予项目将在逐词校勘后补充。
03

与散文版不同Different from the prose account

这里记录的是诗歌三神组合；《欺骗古鲁菲》的奥丁、维利、维组合不会覆盖它。

This is the poetic triad; the Odin, Vili, and Ve grouping in Gylfaginning does not overwrite it.

Evidence / 证据两个见证的版本对照。claim.v0190.voluspa_embla差异保持为版本并存，而非人物自动等同。

01

散文中的最初人类First humans in prose

《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章同样命名阿斯克与恩布拉，构成散文版的人类起源见证。

Gylfaginning 8–9 also names Ask and Embla, forming the prose witness to human origins.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章。claim.v0190.gylf_ask不把散文版当作诗歌版的简单翻译。
02

奥丁、维利与维Odin, Vili, and Ve

散文序列把奥丁、维利与维列为博尔之子，并放在这一创造框架中。

The prose sequence identifies Odin, Vili, and Ve as sons of Borr and places them in this creation frame.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章。claim.v0190.gylf_triad人物组合与《女巫预言》不同。
03

不强制对应No forced equivalence

系统不把维利／维与赫尼尔／洛杜尔按功能相似强制合并；当前只呈现两个文本明确给出的名字。

The system does not force Vili and Ve to equal Hoenir and Lodurr by perceived functional similarity; it presents only the names explicit in each text.

Evidence / 证据两个独立来源见证。claim.v0190.gylf_embla身份对应留作研究问题。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

诗歌与散文见证逐段并列；神祇组合差异进入显式冲突档案，逐词赐予层保持未建模。

Skaldskaparmal preserves the smithing-contest layer that places Gullinbursti, Draupnir, and Mjolnir in one treasure sequence.

锻造竞赛的参与者Participants in the contest

三件宝物Three treasures

交付诸神Presented to the gods

The event profile ends with the three treasures presented to the gods; modern ability lists are not treated as medieval text.

Evidence / 证据《诗语法》第35章的事件结果与神器Claims。claim.eitri.creator_draupnir更完整竞赛过程仍待逐段证据化。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

洛基同行Loki accompanies him

洛基在诗中同行并提供建议；这项同伴关系不表示索尔与洛基是兄弟。

寻回框架Recovery frame

Evidence / 证据诗歌整体定位1–32节。claim.v060.norse.thor_loki_thrymskvida详情将在后续逐节版本扩充。

新西兰政府Te Ara的公开、具原住民语境的策展概述
Open source / 打开来源
01

多种创世传统Many creation traditions

Te Ara明确说明毛利拥有多种创世传统，不同部族讲述不同版本；因此本站没有建立单一“标准毛利创世故事”。

Te Ara explicitly states that Maori have many creation traditions and that iwi tell different versions; the site therefore does not create one standard Maori creation story.

Evidence / 证据Te Ara公开策展概述。claim.v0230.maori_many公开页面只处理来源允许的概括层。
02

Io传统并非普遍Io is not universal

同一公开来源指出，并非所有部族都有Io传统；系统将这一差异保存为版本范围，而不是判断某一部族错误。

The same public source notes that not all iwi have an Io tradition; the system records this as version scope rather than judging any iwi to be wrong.

Evidence / 证据Te Ara公开概述。claim.v0230.maori_io不把一个部族版本推广为全体毛利传统。
03

权限先于收集Permission before collection

部族专属、非公开或具限制性的内容默认不收集；只有适当权威给予明确范围许可后，才可建立受控记录。

Iwi-specific, non-public, or restricted material is not collected by default; a controlled record requires scoped permission from an appropriate authority.

Evidence / 证据现有活态传统访问策略。claim.v0230.maori_permission此处不展示受限故事内容。
Te Ara：创世故事的共同线索Te Ara: common threads in creation stories4 sections

Narrative scope: 从黑暗到光明、天父地母的分离与自然之神

Rights note: 须署名 Te Ahukaramū Charles Royal 与 Te Ara；只发布公开页面的独立概述，不收集具名部族版本。

Title
Māori creation traditions
Institution
Te Ara / New Zealand Government
Locator
Page 1 — Common threads in creation stories
Rights
Reuse
Witness scope
Te Ara 新西兰百科中 Te Ahukaramū Charles Royal 撰写的“毛利创世传统”第1页
Open source / 打开来源
01

从黑暗中出现Out of darkness

Te Ara 写道：在毛利传统中，创世的核心行动是天与地的分离。故事从黑暗与虚无开始，天父兰吉努伊与地母帕帕图阿努库从中出现。起初天地紧紧相连，他们的孩子就出生在两者之间。

Te Ara writes that in Māori tradition the central act of creation is the separation of earth and sky. The story begins in darkness and nothingness, from which Ranginui the sky father and Papatūānuku the earth mother emerge. At first earth and sky are joined, and their children are born between them.

Evidence / 证据Te Ara“毛利创世传统”第1页。claim.v0310.maori.rangi_papa_joined
02

孩子们分开父母The children part their parents

孩子们决定把父母分开。天与地一旦分离，光就照进了世界——从黑暗走向光明，正是由孩子们的这一行动完成的。

The children decide to separate their parents. Once earth and sky are apart, light enters the world: the move from darkness to light is achieved by the children's act.

Evidence / 证据同一页面。claim.v0310.maori.ranginui_separatedTe Ara 说明：在部分部族版本中，塔内在分开天地时起重要作用；本站不选定某一部族的说法。
03

自然的众神Atua of the natural world

分离之后，孩子们成为自然各部分的神（atua）：塔内是森林之神，塔加罗阿是海洋之神，还有地震之神鲁奥莫科、风与天气之神塔维里马特阿。把这些神编进同一个谱系，是毛利人解释自然世界的传统方式。

After the separation the children become atua of different parts of nature: Tāne of the forests, Tangaroa of the sea, Rūaumoko of earthquakes, Tāwhirimātea of winds and weather. Weaving these atua into one genealogy is the traditional Māori way of explaining the natural world.

Evidence / 证据同一页面。claim.v0310.maori.tane_forests鲁奥莫科与塔维里马特阿尚未建立独立实体。
04

不是只有一个版本Not a single version

Te Ara 也提醒，各部族的创世故事差异很大，特别是关于至高存在的角色。这里只讲公开页面所总结的共同线索，具名部族的版本须经相应权威授权。

Te Ara also cautions that tribal versions vary considerably, especially on the role of a supreme being. This retelling covers only the common threads the public page summarizes; named iwi versions require permission from the appropriate authority.

Evidence / 证据Te Ara 故事摘要。claim.v0310.maori.diversity具名 iwi／hapū 版本仍为权限阻塞。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

不收集或公开未经适当授权的部族专属、非公开或限制性知识。

玛雅 / Maya

英雄双子与七金刚鹦鹉：公开基切语境见证Hero Twins and Seven Macaw — a public K'iche'-attributed overview

Smithsonian 的公开页面把胡纳普与伊斯巴兰克作为英雄双子，并叙述他们击败七金刚鹦鹉；本站只建立这一可公开定位的事件，不把它扩写成全体玛雅的标准故事。

A public Smithsonian page presents Hunahpu and Xbalanque as the Hero Twins and says they defeat Seven Macaw; this record models only that locatable episode and does not turn it into a standard story for all Maya communities.

中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese

女娲补天：《淮南子》与《列子》两种见证Nüwa repairs the sky — Huainanzi and Liezi witnesses

《淮南子·览冥训》与《列子·汤问》都记载女娲炼五色石、断鳌足立四极；前者另有黑龙与芦灰，后者把补天放在天地“物有不足”的问答里，并在其后才引出共工。两份见证分开阅读，不合成后世“共工撞山导致女娲补天”的版本。

Huainanzi “Lan Ming” and Liezi “Tang Wen” both have Nüwa refine five-colored stones and cut a turtle's legs to set up the four poles; the former adds the black dragon and reed ash, while the latter frames the repair as a remedy for deficient things and introduces Gonggong only afterwards. The two witnesses are read separately and are not merged into the later version in which Gonggong's collision causes the repair.

  • 女娲
  • 补天
  • 宇宙修复
  • 见证分离

来源与许可 / Source and rights

Title
Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun
Institution
Chinese Text Project
Locator
Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun — passage opening 往古之時
Rights
Site terms apply; ancient text is public-domain cultural heritage
Reuse
No bulk copying; cite the chapter page, verify edition metadata and publish independent summaries only.
Witness scope
西汉刘安主持编纂的《淮南子》传世本；Chinese Text Project 数字版章节定位
01

四极废，九州裂The four poles collapse

《淮南子·览冥训》以远古为背景：支撑天地的四极崩坏，九州开裂，天不能全覆、地不能全载，大火与洪水都无法止息，猛兽鸷鸟危害百姓。本段只按这一见证的顺序概述，不补入其他文献给出的起因。

Huainanzi “Lan Ming” sets the scene in remote antiquity: the four poles holding up the world have failed and the nine provinces have split; heaven no longer covers everything and earth no longer bears everything, fires and floods cannot be stopped, and wild beasts and birds of prey attack the people. This section follows only this witness and adds no cause taken from other texts.

Evidence / 证据《淮南子·览冥训》“往古之时”段；Chinese Text Project 章节页。claim.v0300.nuwa.lanming_collapse传世本用字存在异体；本站只发布独立概述，不复制整段原文。
02

炼五色石，断鳌足Five-colored stones and the turtle's legs

于是女娲炼五色石来修补苍天，又截断巨鳌的足，用来重新立起四极。

Nüwa then refines five-colored stones to patch the azure sky and cuts off the legs of a great turtle to set the four poles upright again.

Evidence / 证据同段女娲补天与立四极的句组。claim.v0300.nuwa.uses_stones炼字与鳌字在不同版本中有异体，不影响本段概述。
03

杀黑龙，积芦灰The black dragon and the reed ash

这一见证还写到女娲杀死黑龙以救济冀州，并堆积芦苇灰来堵住泛滥的洪水；随后天地重新安定，百姓得以存活。

This witness adds that Nüwa kills a black dragon to relieve Jizhou and piles up reed ash to stop the flood waters; afterwards heaven and earth are set right and the people survive.

Evidence / 证据同段黑龙、冀州与芦灰句组。claim.v0300.nuwa.kills_black_dragon冀州在此是文本中的九州之一，不映射为现代行政区或坐标；《列子·汤问》对应段落未陈述这两项。
04

这一段没有写共工No Gonggong in this passage

《览冥训》的女娲段落没有把天地崩坏归因于共工。《淮南子》确实在《天文训》另记共工怒触不周山，但那是另一章中独立的天地倾斜起源，本站不把两章拼接成因果链。

The Lan Ming Nüwa passage does not attribute the collapse to Gonggong. The Huainanzi does narrate Gonggong striking Mount Buzhou, but in a separate passage in the “Tian Wen” chapter that explains the tilt of heaven and earth; the site does not splice the two chapters into a causal chain.

Evidence / 证据《览冥训》女娲段与《天文训》共工段分别定位。claim.v0300.nuwa.lanming_gonggong_boundary把共工触山写成女娲补天起因的说法见于较晚文献，已作为独立接受层排队。
《列子·汤问》见证Liezi Tang Wen witness4 sections

Narrative scope: 天地亦物、炼石补阙、断鳌足立四极，其后共工触山与天倾西北

Title
Liezi, Tang Wen
Institution
Chinese Text Project
Locator
Liezi, Tang Wen — Tang and Xia Ge dialogue
Rights
Site terms apply; ancient text is public-domain cultural heritage
Reuse
No bulk copying; cite the chapter page, verify edition metadata and publish independent summaries only.
Witness scope
托名列御寇的《列子》传世本，成书年代有争议；Chinese Text Project 数字版章节定位
01

天地也是“物”Heaven and earth are also things

《列子·汤问》把女娲补天放在殷汤与夏革关于天地有无极限的问答中：天地也是物，物都有不足之处，所以古时女娲修补了它的缺口。

Liezi “Tang Wen” places the repair inside a dialogue between Tang of Yin and Xia Ge on whether heaven and earth have limits: heaven and earth are also things, things have deficiencies, and so in antiquity Nüwa mended the gap.

Evidence / 证据《列子·汤问》殷汤与夏革问答段。claim.v0300.nuwa.liezi_deficiency_frame《列子》托名列御寇，传世本成书年代有争议；本站不据此把它排在《淮南子》之前。
02

五色石与鳌足Five-colored stones and the turtle's legs

这一见证同样写女娲炼五色石补其缺口，并截断鳌足以立四极；它没有写黑龙、冀州或芦灰。

This witness likewise has Nüwa refine five-colored stones to fill the gap and cut the turtle's legs to set up the four poles; it does not mention the black dragon, Jizhou or reed ash.

Evidence / 证据《列子·汤问》女娲句组。claim.v0300.nuwa.uses_stones“未陈述”只描述本段范围，不是对《淮南子》细节的否定。
03

“其后”才是共工Gonggong comes “afterwards”

紧接补天之后，《汤问》用“其后”引出共工氏与颛顼争为帝、怒触不周之山。在这一见证里，共工事件发生在女娲补天之后，而不是它的起因。

Right after the repair, “Tang Wen” uses “afterwards” to introduce Gonggong contending with Zhuanxu for rulership and striking Mount Buzhou in anger. In this witness the Gonggong episode follows Nüwa's repair; it is not its cause.

Evidence / 证据《列子·汤问》“其后共工氏”句。claim.v0300.gonggong.liezi_after_nuwa后世“共工触山导致女娲补天”的因果顺序不回写进本见证。
04

天倾西北，水归东南Heaven tilts northwest, waters gather southeast

共工撞山折断天柱、断绝地维，于是天向西北倾斜，日月星辰向那里移动；地在东南不满，众水因此流归东南。

Gonggong's blow breaks the pillar of heaven and snaps the cords of the earth, so heaven tilts toward the northwest, where the sun, moon and stars move, and the earth falls short in the southeast, where the rivers and flood waters gather.

Evidence / 证据《列子·汤问》天倾西北句组。claim.v0300.gonggong.tilt_etiology这是文本中的天地形势起源解释，不作为地理或天文史实。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

两份传世文献逐段并列；《列子》传世本成书年代有争议，不据此排序。后世因果化的复述作为独立接受层排队。

中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese

共工触不周山：天倾西北的两处见证Gonggong strikes Mount Buzhou — two early witnesses

《淮南子·天文训》把共工与颛顼争帝、怒触不周山写成独立的天地倾斜起源；《列子·汤问》则用“其后”把同一事件接在女娲补天之后。两处见证都不把它写成女娲补天的起因。

Huainanzi “Tian Wen” narrates Gonggong contending with Zhuanxu and striking Mount Buzhou as a standalone explanation of why heaven and earth tilt; Liezi “Tang Wen” attaches the same event after Nüwa's repair with “afterwards”. Neither witness makes it the cause of Nüwa's repair.

Witness scope
《淮南子·天文训》中的天地形势起源段落
01

共工与颛顼争为帝Gonggong contends with Zhuanxu

《淮南子·天文训》说，从前共工与颛顼争夺帝位。

Huainanzi “Tian Wen” says that long ago Gonggong contended with Zhuanxu to become ruler.

Evidence / 证据《淮南子·天文训》“昔者共工”段。claim.v0300.gonggong.contends_zhuanxu颛顼在此作为传说中的君主登记，不作历史真实性判断。
02

怒触不周之山Striking Mount Buzhou in anger

共工发怒撞向不周山，支撑天的柱子折断，系住大地的绳索断绝。

In anger Gonggong strikes Mount Buzhou; the pillar holding up heaven breaks and the cords binding the earth snap.

Evidence / 证据同段不周山、天柱与地维句组。claim.v0300.gonggong.strikes_buzhou不周山不映射为现代山名或坐标。
03

一段独立的起源解释A standalone etiology

结果天向西北倾斜，日月星辰随之移动；地在东南不满，积水与尘埃都归向那里。这一段没有提到女娲，是《天文训》中独立的天地形势解释。

As a result heaven tilts to the northwest and the sun, moon and stars shift with it; the earth falls short in the southeast, where waters and dust collect. The passage does not mention Nüwa and stands as an independent explanation of cosmic orientation in “Tian Wen”.

Evidence / 证据《淮南子·天文训》共工段结尾。claim.v0300.gonggong.tianwen_standalone同书《览冥训》的女娲段与此段分属不同章节，不合成单一故事。
《列子·汤问》见证Liezi Tang Wen witness3 sections

Narrative scope: 以“其后”接续、共工氏与颛顼争帝、触山与天倾西北

Witness scope
《列子·汤问》中紧接女娲补天之后的共工段落
01

接在女娲补天之后Following Nüwa's repair

《列子·汤问》在叙述女娲补天之后，用“其后”转入共工的故事，因此这一见证中的共工事件有明确的先后位置。

Liezi “Tang Wen” turns to Gonggong with “afterwards” right after narrating Nüwa's repair, so in this witness the Gonggong episode has an explicit place in sequence.

Evidence / 证据《列子·汤问》“其后共工氏”句。claim.v0300.gonggong.liezi_after_nuwa叙事先后只属于这一见证，不是绝对年代。
02

共工氏与颛顼争帝Gonggong-shi contends with Zhuanxu

这里称“共工氏”，同样与颛顼争为帝，怒而撞向不周之山，折断天柱、断绝地维。

Here he is called “Gonggong-shi”; again he contends with Zhuanxu for rulership and strikes Mount Buzhou in anger, breaking the pillar of heaven and snapping the cords of the earth.

Evidence / 证据《列子·汤问》共工氏句组。claim.v0300.gonggong.strikes_buzhou“共工”与“共工氏”的称谓差异保留，不判定其为神祇、氏族首领或二者兼有。
03

天倾西北，百川东归Heaven tilts northwest, rivers flow southeast

天向西北倾斜，日月星辰向那里靠拢；地在东南不满，于是百川与积水都流归东南。与《天文训》相比，措辞略有不同。

Heaven tilts to the northwest and the sun, moon and stars draw toward it; the earth falls short in the southeast, so all rivers and flood waters flow there. The wording differs slightly from “Tian Wen”.

Evidence / 证据《列子·汤问》天倾西北句组。claim.v0300.gonggong.tilt_etiology措辞差异只在对读中标注，不改写任一见证。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

事件位置差异进入显式冲突档案；不周山不投射为现实坐标，颛顼不作历史真实性判断。

印度教传统 / Hindu

乳海搅拌：《摩诃婆罗多》精校本与《薄伽梵往世书》Churning of the Ocean — Mahābhārata critical edition and Bhāgavata Purāṇa witnesses

两份梵语见证都以曼陀罗山为搅棒、婆苏吉为搅绳，并让檀文陀利捧着甘露出现、罗睺被神盘斩首；但托山的龟在精校本中是龟王阿库帕拉，在《薄伽梵往世书》中是毗湿奴的龟形化身，湿婆饮毒也只见于后者的当前定位。吴哥寺浮雕继续作为独立的物质见证。

Both Sanskrit witnesses use Mount Mandara as the rod and Vāsuki as the rope, have Dhanvantari emerge with the amṛta and Rāhu beheaded by the discus; but the tortoise supporting the mountain is the tortoise king Akūpāra in the critical edition and Viṣṇu's tortoise form in the Bhāgavata Purāṇa, and Śiva drinking the poison appears only at the latter's locator. The Angkor Wat relief remains a separate material witness.

Open source / 打开来源
01

诸神求取甘露The gods seek the amṛta

精校本《初篇》1.15 中，诸神聚集在须弥山上商议如何得到甘露；那罗延（毗湿奴）建议诸神与阿修罗一起搅动大海。

In the critical edition at Ādiparvan 1.15, the gods gather on Mount Meru to deliberate how to obtain the amṛta; Nārāyaṇa (Viṣṇu) advises that gods and asuras churn the ocean together.

Evidence / 证据精校本初篇 1.15。claim.v0300.churn.amrita_sought“那罗延”在此按既有毗湿奴实体连接，不扩展神学解释。
02

曼陀罗山、婆苏吉与龟王Mandara, Vāsuki and the tortoise king

巨蛇阿难陀拔起曼陀罗山作为搅棒，龙王婆苏吉充当搅绳；山的底部由龟王阿库帕拉承托。这一见证没有说龟王是毗湿奴的化身。

The serpent Ananta uproots Mount Mandara to serve as the churning rod and the nāga Vāsuki serves as the rope; the tortoise king Akūpāra bears the base of the mountain. This witness does not say that the tortoise king is a form of Viṣṇu.

Evidence / 证据精校本初篇 1.16。claim.v0300.churn.akupara_supports阿难陀拔山一项为中等置信度，待逐颂校对；阿难陀尚未单独建实体。
03

檀文陀利捧着甘露出现Dhanvantari appears with the amṛta

搅拌中先后涌现多种存在与宝物，最后檀文陀利捧着盛满甘露的水罐出现。

As the churning goes on, a series of beings and treasures emerges, and finally Dhanvantari appears holding a vessel filled with the amṛta.

Evidence / 证据精校本初篇 1.16。claim.v0300.churn.dhanvantari_carries_amrita此前出现之物的逐项清单尚未建模，不从其他版本补齐。
04

迷惑的女身与罗睺The deluding female form and Rāhu

阿修罗夺走甘露后，那罗延化作迷惑人的女子形象，把甘露取回交给诸神。罗睺假扮天神偷饮甘露，被日月揭发后，那罗延以神盘斩下其头；随后天神与阿修罗爆发大战。

After the Dānavas seize the amṛta, Nārāyaṇa takes on a deluding female form and recovers it for the gods. Rāhu, disguised as a god, drinks some of it; when the Sun and Moon expose him, Nārāyaṇa cuts off his head with the discus, and a great battle between gods and Dānavas follows.

Evidence / 证据精校本初篇 1.16–1.17。claim.v0300.churn.rahu_beheaded专名实体“摩希尼”只连接《薄伽梵往世书》见证；此处保留“迷惑的女身”描述。
05

精校本正文的范围Scope of the constituted critical text

在精校本 1.15–1.17 的正文里，没有湿婆吞下毒药的情节。这只说明本定位“未陈述”，不代表所有写本或地方传统都没有；校勘记录与附录尚待逐项核对。

The constituted critical text at 1.15–1.17 does not include Śiva swallowing the poison. This records only that the locator does not state it; it does not mean that every manuscript or regional tradition lacks it, and the apparatus and appendices still have to be checked.

Evidence / 证据精校本初篇 1.15–1.17 正文范围与 BORI 精校本说明。claim.v0300.churn.mbh_poison_scope写本校勘与附录已作为独立研究队列排队。

01

诸神与阿修罗暂时结盟A truce between gods and asuras

《薄伽梵往世书》第八篇第5–6章中，诸神按毗湿奴的建议与阿修罗暂时结盟，合力拔起曼陀罗山并运往大海，毗湿奴亲自出手相助。

In Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.5–8.6, on Viṣṇu's advice the gods make a temporary truce with the asuras; together they uproot Mount Mandara and carry it toward the ocean, with Viṣṇu himself stepping in to help.

Evidence / 证据《薄伽梵往世书》8.5–8.6。claim.v0300.churn.bhp_devas_asuras_uproot运山过程的细节只作概述，待逐颂校对。
02

毗湿奴化身巨龟Viṣṇu takes the form of a tortoise

以婆苏吉为绳开始搅拌后，曼陀罗山沉入水中；毗湿奴化作巨龟潜到山下，用背托起大山。

Once the churning begins with Vāsuki as the rope, Mount Mandara sinks into the water; Viṣṇu assumes the form of a giant tortoise, dives beneath it and raises the mountain on his back.

Evidence / 证据《薄伽梵往世书》8.7。claim.v0300.churn.kurma_supports与精校本的龟王阿库帕拉构成显式见证差异，不做身份等同。
03

湿婆饮下毒药Śiva drinks the poison

搅拌最先产生可怕的毒药，向各方蔓延；众生求助于湿婆，湿婆把毒药饮下。

The churning first produces a terrible poison that spreads in every direction; beings turn to Śiva, who drinks it.

Evidence / 证据《薄伽梵往世书》8.7。claim.v0300.churn.shiva_drinks_poison后续图像传统与虔信解释未在本批次逐句建模。
04

檀文陀利与甘露Dhanvantari and the amṛta

其后又涌现多种宝物与存在，最后檀文陀利捧着盛满甘露的水罐出现。

A series of treasures and beings then emerges, and finally Dhanvantari appears holding a vessel filled with the amṛta.

Evidence / 证据《薄伽梵往世书》8.8。claim.v0300.churn.dhanvantari_carries_amrita此前出现之物的逐项清单尚未建模，不与精校本合并。
05

摩希尼与罗睺Mohinī and Rāhu

阿修罗抢走甘露后，毗湿奴化身为摩希尼迷惑他们，并把甘露分给诸神。罗睺假扮天神混入其中饮下甘露，被日月指认后，毗湿奴以神盘斩下其头。

When the asuras snatch the amṛta, Viṣṇu becomes Mohinī, captivates them and distributes the amṛta to the gods. Rāhu, disguised as a god, slips in and drinks; when the Sun and Moon point him out, Viṣṇu severs his head with the discus.

Evidence / 证据《薄伽梵往世书》8.8–8.9。claim.v0300.churn.mohini_distributes罗睺后来的天体身份不在本批次建模。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

精校本正文与往世书分开对读；“未陈述”不等于所有写本都没有。现代虔信译本不复制，神学解释不作裁定。

古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian

从阿图姆到荷鲁斯：埃及众神的一家From Atum to Horus — a family of Egyptian gods

UCL 数字埃及把零散的古代文献整理成一个神族谱系：太阳神阿图姆生出舒与泰芙努特，他们又生出大地之神盖布与天空女神努特；舒把天地分开。后来塞特杀害兄弟奥西里斯，伊西斯收集遗体并怀上荷鲁斯。这是一份学术综述，不是某一部古代文献的原文顺序。

UCL Digital Egypt orders scattered ancient references into one divine family: the sun-god Atum brings forth Shu and Tefnet, who produce Geb (earth) and Nut (sky); Shu holds them apart. Later Seth murders his brother Osiris, and Isis gathers the body and conceives Horus. It is a scholarly synthesis, not the sequence of a single ancient text.

01

太阳神的第一对子女The creator's first pair

在埃及的创世观念里，主要的创造者是太阳神，他也被称为阿图姆。他最先生出一男一女：舒和泰芙努特。学者认为“舒”这个名字和“干燥”有关，“泰芙努特”则代表湿润。

In Egyptian creation thought the principal creator is the sun-god, also called Atum. His first offshoots are a son and a daughter, Shu and Tefnet; scholars link Shu's name to dryness and read Tefnet as moisture.

Evidence / 证据UCL Digital Egypt 创世页面。claim.v0310.egypt.shu_child_atum“干燥／湿润”是现代学者的词源解释。
02

大地与天空出生Earth and sky are born

舒和泰芙努特又生下两个孩子：大地之神盖布和天空女神努特。干燥与湿润，变成了坚实的大地和古埃及人眼中充满水的天空。

Shu and Tefnet in turn produce two children: Geb, god of the earth, and Nut, goddess of the sky — dryness and moisture crystallised into solid earth and a sky the Egyptians thought of as watery.

Evidence / 证据同一页面的子女段落。claim.v0310.egypt.geb_child_shu
03

舒把天地分开Shu holds earth and sky apart

盖布和努特被舒分开，天在上、地在下。零散的古代记载暗示，这种分离必须被强制维持，否则天地还会继续生出神来。

Shu separates Geb and Nut, sky above and earth below. Scattered references suggest the separation had to be enforced, or earth and sky would keep producing divine offspring.

Evidence / 证据UCL 页面关于天地分离的说明。claim.v0310.egypt.shu_separation这是学者对零散材料的归纳，不是单一文本的原话。
04

塞特、奥西里斯与伊西斯Seth, Osiris and Isis

后来，奥西里斯“无序的兄弟”塞特出于嫉妒杀害了他，把遗体分开，并取代他成为世界之王。伊西斯找回奥西里斯的肢体，让遗体恢复到足以受孕，生下了荷鲁斯。

Later Osiris's anarchic brother Seth, out of jealousy, murders him, dismembers him and takes his place as king of the world. Isis collects the limbs and revives the body enough to conceive a child, Horus.

Evidence / 证据UCL 页面的奥西里斯故事概述。claim.v0310.egypt.seth_kills_osiris古代文献中这个故事有许多版本；本站不合成“标准版”。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

学术综述层；古代文本见证尚未逐条拆分，各神亲属关系以 UCL 页面的表述为准。

古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian

拉的眼睛化作塞赫麦特：天牛之书The Eye of Ra becomes Sekhmet — Tale of the Heavenly Cow

人类反叛太阳神拉。拉把自己的“眼睛”派出去惩罚人类，她化作塞赫麦特，几乎把人类消灭。众神把一湖啤酒染成血红色，她喝醉后恢复成慈爱的哈托尔。

Humankind rebels against the sun-god Ra. He sends out his Eye to punish them; as Sekhmet she almost wipes humanity out, until the gods dye a lake of beer blood-red and she drinks, growing gentle again as Hathor.

  • 惩罚
  • 拉的眼睛
  • 塞赫麦特
  • 哈托尔

Open source / 打开来源
01

人类反叛拉Humankind rebels against Ra

一部新王国时期的宗教文献记载：人们起来反叛太阳神拉。拉决定惩罚他们，后来他也离开人间，退到天上。

A New Kingdom religious composition records that people rebel against the sun-god Ra. He resolves to punish them, and in the end he withdraws to the sky.

Evidence / 证据UCL 页面对《天牛之书》的概述。claim.v0310.egypt.ra_rebellion
02

眼睛化作塞赫麦特The Eye becomes Sekhmet

拉把自己的眼睛从身体里派出去，成为一个独立的“女儿”。她化作塞赫麦特——意思是“强大的女神”——几乎把人类全部消灭。

Ra sends his Eye out from his body as a separate offshoot, a 'daughter'. As Sekhmet, 'the mighty goddess', she almost annihilates humankind.

Evidence / 证据同一概述的“拉的眼睛”段落。claim.v0310.egypt.sekhmet_eye_of_ra“强大的女神”是 UCL 给出的释义。
03

一湖红色的啤酒A lake of red beer

众神用计：他们把一湖啤酒染成红色，看起来像人血。她喝下后醉得香甜，脾气也软化下来，重新变回慈爱、温柔的哈托尔。

The gods play a trick: they colour a lake of beer red to look like human blood. She drinks, becomes sweetly drunk, and reverts to her loving, maternal character as Hathor.

Evidence / 证据同一概述的结尾。claim.v0310.egypt.sekhmet_to_hathor这是故事中的性格转变；数据库不把两位女神合并为同一实体。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据 UCL 对新王国宗教文献《天牛之书》的概述；塞赫麦特与哈托尔在数据库中仍是两个实体。

古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian

称量心脏：阿尼纸草中的阿努比斯与托特Weighing the heart — Anubis and Thoth in the Papyrus of Ani

大英博物馆藏《阿尼纸草》第3框画着审判大厅：天平一边放着阿尼的心脏，另一边是代表玛阿特的羽毛。胡狼头的阿努比斯扶稳天平，托特既以狒狒形象坐在天平上，又以朱鹭头书吏的形象准备记录结果。

British Museum Papyrus of Ani, frame 3, shows the Hall of Judgment: Ani's heart in one pan, a feather representing Maat in the other. Jackal-headed Anubis steadies the balance, and Thoth appears both as a baboon on top of it and as the ibis-headed scribe ready to record the result.

  • 审判
  • 冥界
  • 阿努比斯
  • 托特
  • 玛阿特

Open source / 打开来源
01

心脏与羽毛A heart and a feather

画面中央是一座天平。左边的秤盘里放着阿尼的心脏，右边是一根羽毛，代表真理与秩序女神玛阿特。

At the centre stands a balance. In the left pan lies Ani's heart; in the right, a feather representing Maat, goddess of truth and order.

Evidence / 证据大英博物馆 EA10470,3 藏品描述。claim.v0310.egypt.ani_depicts_weighing
02

阿努比斯扶稳天平Anubis steadies the balance

胡狼头的阿努比斯跪在天平旁，拉着右边秤盘的绳子，扶稳天平的铅锤。博物馆的说明称他是“在防腐之所的那一位”。

Jackal-headed Anubis kneels by the balance, holding the cord of the right-hand pan and steadying its plumb bob. The museum describes him as 'he who is in the place of embalming'.

Evidence / 证据同一藏品描述中关于阿努比斯的部分。claim.v0310.egypt.anubis_weighing
03

托特准备记录Thoth stands ready to record

托特在画中出现两次：一只小狒狒坐在天平顶端；右边站着朱鹭头的“众神书吏”，手持调色板和芦苇笔，准备记下审问的结果。

Thoth appears twice: as a small baboon sitting on top of the balance, and as the ibis-headed scribe of the gods standing to the right with palette and reed, ready to write down the result.

Evidence / 证据同一藏品描述中关于托特的部分。claim.v0310.egypt.thoth_weighing称量的最终结果和吞噬心脏的怪兽不在这条博物馆描述里，本站不补写。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据博物馆对单件纸草画面的描述；结果判定与怪兽阿米特未在本框记录中出现，不补写。

古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian

两位哀悼的女神：伊西斯与奈芙蒂斯Two mourning goddesses — Isis and Nephthys

大都会艺术博物馆的一对托勒密时期木像：奈芙蒂斯跪着，举手掩面哀悼；另一尊是同样姿势的伊西斯。博物馆说明，在古埃及的图像中，这两位女神常守在奥西里斯遗体的两侧。

A Ptolemaic pair of wooden figures at the Met: Nephthys kneels with a hand raised before her face in mourning, and a companion figure shows Isis. The museum notes that the two mourning goddesses accompany the body of Osiris in Egyptian representations.

01

举手掩面的奈芙蒂斯Nephthys raises her hand

这尊小木像跪在地上，一只手举到脸前，是古埃及的哀悼姿势。她头上的象形文字符号，表明她就是女神奈芙蒂斯。

The small wooden figure kneels and raises one hand before her face, an Egyptian gesture of mourning. The hieroglyph on her head identifies her as the goddess Nephthys.

Evidence / 证据大都会艺术博物馆 12.182.23a。claim.v0310.egypt.nephthys_figure
02

和伊西斯成对A pair with Isis

博物馆说明，这尊像和另一尊伊西斯像（12.182.23b）是一对，两位女神以同样的姿势哀悼。

The museum states that the figure forms a pair with a figure of Isis (12.182.23b), the two goddesses mourning in the same posture.

Evidence / 证据两件藏品的说明互相引用。claim.v0310.egypt.mourners_pair
03

守在奥西里斯身旁Beside the body of Osiris

在古埃及图像中，这两位哀悼女神陪伴在奥西里斯的遗体旁，一左一右。博物馆推测，这对木像原本可能放在死者棺椁的两端。

In Egyptian representations the two mourning goddesses accompany the body of Osiris, one at each end. The museum suggests these figures were presumably placed at either end of the deceased's sarcophagus.

Evidence / 证据大都会艺术博物馆藏品说明。claim.v0310.egypt.nephthys_mourns_osiris“放在棺椁两端”是博物馆的推测，不是出土记录。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

物质见证层：依据博物馆藏品说明；“放在棺椁两端”是博物馆的推测。

日本神道 / Japanese Shinto

伊邪那岐与伊邪那美：造岛、黄泉与禊祓Izanagi and Izanami — making the land, Yomi and purification

天神命伊邪那岐与伊邪那美巩固漂浮的大地。两神站在天浮桥上，用天沼矛搅动海水，滴下的盐水堆成淤能碁吕岛。伊邪那美生下火神后被烧伤而死；伊邪那岐追到黄泉，她却说已经吃了黄泉的食物。伊邪那岐回来后洗净身体，最后从左眼、右眼和鼻子生出天照大神、月读和须佐之男。

The heavenly deities command Izanagi and Izanami to consolidate the drifting land. From the Floating Bridge of Heaven they stir the brine with a jewelled spear, and the drops become Onogoro Island. Izanami dies after bearing the fire deity; Izanagi follows her to Yomi, but she has already eaten of its hearth. Returning, he purifies himself, and from his left eye, right eye and nose are born Amaterasu, Tsukuyomi and Susanoo.

  • 创世
  • 黄泉
  • 禊祓
  • 三贵子

张伯伦 1882 年《古事记》英译（公共领域）
Open source / 打开来源
01

天浮桥上的长矛A spear from the Floating Bridge

天上的众神命令伊邪那岐和伊邪那美去巩固还在漂浮的大地，并交给他们一支镶玉的天沼矛。两神站在天浮桥上，把矛伸进海里搅动；提起矛时，矛尖滴下的盐水层层堆积，成了淤能碁吕岛。

The heavenly deities command Izanagi and Izanami to make firm the drifting land and give them a jewelled spear. Standing on the Floating Bridge of Heaven, they stir the brine; when they lift the spear, the drops from its tip pile up into Onogoro Island.

Evidence / 证据张伯伦译本第三节（kj010）。claim.v0310.japan.onogoro_result《日本书纪》说两神是自己决定动手，本版不合并。
02

火神出生The birth of the fire deity

两神结合，生出许多岛屿和神灵。可是伊邪那美生下火神时被烧伤，病倒在床，最终“神去”——也就是死去。

Joined together, the pair bring forth islands and deities. But when Izanami gives birth to the Deity-of-Fire she is burned, falls sick, and at length 'divinely retires' — she dies.

Evidence / 证据张伯伦译本第七节（kj014）。claim.v0310.japan.izanami_death
03

追到黄泉Following her to Yomi

伊邪那岐想再见到妻子，便追到黄泉，请她回去：“我们造的国土还没有完成。”伊邪那美回答：你来得太晚了，我已经吃了黄泉灶火煮的东西——不过她答应去和黄泉的神商量。

Longing to see her, Izanagi follows Izanami to Yomi and asks her to come back, for the lands they made are not yet finished. She answers that he has come too late: she has already eaten from the hearth of Yomi — yet she agrees to consult the deities there.

Evidence / 证据张伯伦译本第九节（kj016）。claim.v0310.japan.izanagi_yomi之后的逃离情节可参看本库“黄泉中的八雷神”故事。
04

洗出三位贵神Three deities born of washing

伊邪那岐回来后在水中洗净自己，身上不断化生出神。最后，他洗左眼时生出天照大神，洗右眼时生出月读，洗鼻子时生出须佐之男。

Back from Yomi, Izanagi bathes to purify himself, and deities are born as he washes. Last of all, washing his left eye brings forth Amaterasu, his right eye Tsukuyomi, and his nose Susanoo.

Evidence / 证据张伯伦译本第十节（kj017）及译注。claim.v0310.japan.tsukuyomi_born三神的通行名称来自译者注释。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据张伯伦 1882 年英译《古事记》的第三至第十节；《日本书纪》的不同说法另行保存，不合并。

北欧 / Norse

华纳神族：尼奥尔德、弗雷与芙蕾雅Njörðr, Freyr and Freyja — the Vanir family

尼奥尔德住在诺欧通，掌管风和海，原本在华纳海姆长大，作为人质来到阿萨神族。他的儿子弗雷掌管雨水、阳光和丰收；女儿芙蕾雅住在弗尔克范格，每次出征都分走一半战死者，乘坐两只猫拉的车。弗雷后来为了娶葛德，交出了自己的宝剑。

Njörðr lives at Nóatún and rules wind and sea; raised in Vanaheim, he came to the Æsir as a hostage. His son Freyr rules rain, sunshine and harvests; his daughter Freyja lives at Fólkvangr, takes half the slain and drives a chariot pulled by two cats. Later Freyr gives away his sword to win Gerðr.

  • 海洋
  • 丰收
  • 芙蕾雅

Open source / 打开来源
01

海风之神尼奥尔德Njörðr of wind and sea

尼奥尔德住在天上一个叫诺欧通的地方。他掌管风的运行，能平息海浪和火焰；出海航行和捕鱼的人都向他祈祷。他在华纳海姆长大，后来作为人质被送到阿萨神族。

Njörðr lives in heaven at a place called Nóatún. He rules the motion of the wind and can calm sea and fire; those who sail or fish pray to him. He was raised in Vanaheim and later given to the Æsir as a hostage.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第23章。claim.v0310.norse.njord_wind_sea
02

弗雷与芙蕾雅Freyr and Freyja

尼奥尔德有一儿一女。儿子弗雷掌管雨水、阳光和大地的收成，人们向他祈求丰年与富足。女儿芙蕾雅住在弗尔克范格，每当她骑马出战，就分得一半战死者，另一半归奥丁；她乘坐的车由两只猫拉着。

Njörðr has a son and a daughter. Freyr rules rain, sunshine and the produce of the earth, and people pray to him for good seasons and wealth. Freyja lives at Fólkvangr; whenever she rides to battle she takes half the slain, Odin the other half, and her chariot is drawn by two cats.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第24章。claim.v0310.norse.freyja_slain
03

弗雷交出宝剑Freyr gives away his sword

后来，弗雷爱上了巨人之女葛德。为了娶到她，他把自己的宝剑交给了别人作为代价——这把剑从此不在他手中。

Later Freyr falls in love with Gerðr, a giant's daughter. To win her he gives away his sword as the price, and the sword is no longer his.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第37章。claim.v0310.norse.freyr_sword葛德尚未单独建立实体；诸神黄昏中的后果另行登记。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《欺骗古鲁菲》第23、24、37章；诗体埃达中的对应诗篇另行登记。

北欧 / Norse

提尔的手：束缚芬里尔Týr's hand and the binding of Fenrir

提尔是众神中最勇敢的一位。众神要捆住越长越大的狼芬里尔，狼要求有人把手放进它嘴里作担保。只有提尔伸出了右手；当狼发现自己挣脱不开，就咬断了他的手。所以提尔只有一只手。

Týr is the boldest of the gods. When they set out to bind the growing wolf Fenrir, the wolf demands a hand in its mouth as a pledge. Only Týr offers his right hand; when the wolf finds it cannot break free, it bites the hand off — and so Týr is one-handed.

  • 勇气
  • 誓约

01

最勇敢的神The boldest god

斯诺里说，提尔是众神中最勇敢、最无畏的一位，打仗时人们向他祈求胜利。他只有一只手。

Snorri says Týr is the boldest and most valiant of the gods, invoked for victory in battle — and he has only one hand.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第25章。claim.v0310.norse.tyr_brave
02

一只手作担保A hand as a pledge

众神想用一条魔法绳索捆住狼芬里尔。狼起了疑心，要求有人把手放进它嘴里，保证这不是诡计。众神面面相觑，只有提尔伸出了右手。

The gods mean to bind the wolf Fenrir with a magic fetter. Suspicious, the wolf demands that someone put a hand in its mouth as a pledge of good faith. Only Týr holds out his right hand.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第34章。claim.v0310.norse.tyr_fenrir
03

狼咬断了手The wolf bites

绳索越挣越紧，狼发现自己被骗了，便咬断了提尔的手。众神都笑了，只有提尔没有笑。

The more the wolf struggles, the tighter the fetter grows. Realizing it has been tricked, Fenrir bites off Týr's hand — and all the gods laugh, except Týr.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第34章。claim.v0310.norse.fenrir_bound
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《欺骗古鲁菲》第25、34章；芬里尔在诸神黄昏中的结局见既有故事。

北欧 / Norse

海姆达尔：众神的守望者Heimdallr, watchman of the gods

海姆达尔住在彩虹桥边的希敏约格，替众神守着桥，防备山巨人。他睡得比鸟还少，夜里能看见一百里远，连草和羊毛生长的声音都听得见。他有一支加拉尔号角，吹响时所有世界都能听到。人们还说他是九位姐妹母亲的儿子。

Heimdallr lives at Himinbjörg beside the rainbow bridge Bifröst and guards it for the gods against the mountain-giants. He needs less sleep than a bird, sees a hundred leagues by night, and hears grass and wool growing. His horn Gjallarhorn can be heard in all worlds, and he is called the son of nine mothers who were sisters.

  • 守望者
  • 号角
  • 彩虹桥

01

桥头的守望者Watchman at the bridge

海姆达尔是众神的守望者。他住在彩虹桥边一个叫希敏约格的地方，在天的尽头守着桥，防止山巨人闯进来。

Heimdallr is the watchman of the gods. He lives at Himinbjörg by Bifröst, at the edge of heaven, guarding the bridge against the mountain-giants.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第27章。claim.v0310.norse.heimdall_watchman
02

看得远，听得清Keen eyes and ears

他需要的睡眠比鸟还少，无论白天黑夜都能看见一百里远；他还能听见草在地上生长、羊毛在羊身上生长的声音。

He needs less sleep than a bird and sees a hundred leagues by day or night; he can hear grass growing in the ground and wool growing on sheep.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第27章。claim.v0310.norse.heimdall_senses
03

加拉尔号角与九位母亲Gjallarhorn and nine mothers

海姆达尔有一支号角，叫加拉尔号角，吹响时所有世界都能听见。斯诺里还说，他是九位母亲的儿子，而这九位母亲是姐妹。

Heimdallr has a horn called Gjallarhorn, whose blast is heard in all worlds. Snorri also says he is the son of nine mothers, who were sisters.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第27章。claim.v0310.norse.heimdall_horn九位母亲的名字见其他诗篇，本版不列出。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《欺骗古鲁菲》第27章。

北欧 / Norse

尤弥尔与世界的形成Ymir and the shaping of the world

太初有一道深渊，北方的寒霜遇到南方的热气，化出巨人尤弥尔。后来奥丁和他的兄弟维利、维杀死了尤弥尔，用他的身体造出世界：肉成了大地，血成了海洋和湖泊，骨头成了山石，头骨成了天空。

In the beginning there is a great void where rime from the north meets heat from the south, and from it the giant Ymir arises. Later Odin and his brothers Vili and Vé kill Ymir and build the world from his body: his flesh becomes the earth, his blood the sea and lakes, his bones the rocks, his skull the sky.

  • 创世
  • 巨人
  • 世界身体

01

冰与火之间Between ice and fire

世界还不存在时，只有一道叫金伦加的深渊。北方的寒霜与南方的热气在这里相遇，融化的水滴里生出了巨人尤弥尔。

Before the world exists there is only the void of Ginnungagap. Where rime from the north meets heat from the south, the thawing drops bring forth the giant Ymir.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第5章。claim.v0310.norse.ymir_origin
02

奥丁兄弟杀死尤弥尔Odin and his brothers kill Ymir

布尔的儿子们——奥丁、维利和维——杀死了巨人尤弥尔。

The sons of Borr — Odin, Vili and Vé — kill the giant Ymir.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第7章。claim.v0310.norse.odin_kills_ymir
03

用身体造世界A world made from a body

他们用尤弥尔的身体造出世界：肉变成大地，血变成海洋和湖泊，骨头变成山岩，头骨被举起来成为天空。

From Ymir's body they make the world: his flesh becomes the earth, his blood the sea and lakes, his bones the rocks, and his skull is raised up as the sky.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第8章。claim.v0310.norse.ymir_world斯诺里的散文综述与诗歌见证可能有细节差异。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《欺骗古鲁菲》第5–8章；《女巫的预言》等诗歌见证另行对读。

北欧 / Norse

弗丽嘉的誓言与巴德尔之死Frigg's oaths and the death of Baldr

弗丽嘉是奥丁的妻子，知道一切命运。她的儿子巴德尔梦见自己有危险，她便让火、水、金属、石头、草木和野兽都发誓不伤害他——唯独漏掉了一株幼小的槲寄生。巴德尔最后正是被槲寄生杀死的。

Frigg, Odin's wife, knows all fates. When her son Baldr dreams of danger, she makes fire, water, metals, stones, plants and beasts swear not to harm him — all except a young shoot of mistletoe. In the end it is mistletoe that kills Baldr.

  • 誓言
  • 命运

01

知晓命运的女神The goddess who knows all fates

弗丽嘉是奥丁的妻子。斯诺里说，她知道一切人的命运，只是从不说出来。

Frigg is Odin's wife. Snorri says she knows the fates of all, though she does not speak them aloud.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第20章。claim.v0310.norse.frigg_wife
02

万物发誓Everything swears

巴德尔做了不祥的梦。弗丽嘉便去找火、水、铁和各种金属、石头、土地、树木、疾病、野兽、鸟和蛇，让它们都发誓不伤害巴德尔。只有长在英灵殿西边的一株小槲寄生，她觉得太小，没有让它发誓。

Baldr has ominous dreams, so Frigg makes fire, water, iron and all metals, stones, earth, trees, sicknesses, beasts, birds and serpents swear not to harm him. Only a young mistletoe shoot she thinks too small to ask.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第49章。claim.v0310.norse.frigg_oaths
03

槲寄生The mistletoe

众神以为巴德尔刀枪不入，便拿东西扔他取乐。然而那株没有发誓的槲寄生被做成了投枪，巴德尔被它刺中而死。

Believing Baldr unharmable, the gods amuse themselves by throwing things at him. But the mistletoe that never swore is made into a dart, and Baldr is struck down and dies.

Evidence / 证据《欺骗古鲁菲》第49章。claim.v0310.norse.baldr_death投掷者与幕后之人的情节将在后续批次逐段登记。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《欺骗古鲁菲》第20、49章；之后的冥界求情与报复情节尚未逐段登记。

墨西卡 / Mexica

蛇山上的诞生：维齐洛波奇特利The birth of Huitzilopochtli on Serpent Mountain

科阿特利库埃在蛇山打扫时，一团羽毛飘落，她把它塞在腰间，因此怀孕。女儿科约尔沙乌基认为这是耻辱，带着四百个兄弟要杀死母亲。就在他们冲上山顶时，维齐洛波奇特利全副武装地出生，砍下科约尔沙乌基的头，把兄弟们赶散。

While sweeping on Coatepec, Coatlicue picks up a ball of feathers, tucks it at her waist and conceives. Her daughter Coyolxauhqui, shamed, leads the four hundred brothers to kill their mother. As they reach the summit, Huitzilopochtli is born fully armed, beheads Coyolxauhqui and scatters the brothers.

  • 战神
  • 蛇山
  • 墨西加

01

一团羽毛A ball of feathers

科阿特利库埃住在蛇山上。一天她打扫时，一团羽毛从天上飘下来。她把羽毛捡起来，塞在腰间——就这样怀孕了。

Coatlicue lives on Coatepec. One day as she sweeps, a ball of feathers drifts down. She picks it up and tucks it at her waist — and so she becomes pregnant.

Evidence / 证据《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章。claim.v0310.mexica.coatlicue_feathers
02

兄妹要杀母亲Her children turn against her

她的女儿科约尔沙乌基对兄弟们——四百个南方人——说：母亲让我们蒙羞，我们必须杀了她。于是他们全副武装，朝蛇山进发。

Her daughter Coyolxauhqui tells her brothers, the four hundred Centzon Huitznahua, that their mother has shamed them and must be killed. Armed, they set out for Coatepec.

Evidence / 证据《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章。claim.v0310.mexica.coyol_event
03

全副武装地出生Born fully armed

腹中的维齐洛波奇特利安慰母亲：不要怕，我知道该怎么做。敌人冲上山顶的那一刻，他出生了，手持盾牌、投枪和投枪器。

From the womb Huitzilopochtli comforts his mother: do not be afraid, I know what to do. The moment the attackers reach the summit he is born, carrying shield, darts and dart-thrower.

Evidence / 证据《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章。claim.v0310.mexica.huitz_armed
04

山顶的胜利Victory on the summit

维齐洛波奇特利砍下科约尔沙乌基的头，她的身体沿着蛇山滚落下去。四百个兄弟也被他从山顶追得四散奔逃。

Huitzilopochtli cuts off Coyolxauhqui's head, and her body tumbles down the side of Coatepec. He drives the four hundred brothers from the summit, scattering them in every direction.

Evidence / 证据《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章。claim.v0310.mexica.huitz_kills_coyol学者认为这段叙事与大神庙的布局相呼应，属于解释，不写成事实。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章（萨阿贡编纂，16世纪）；学者把这段故事与大神庙布局相联系属于解释层。

墨西卡 / Mexica

图拉的羽蛇神与蒂特拉卡瓦Quetzalcoatl and Titlacauan at Tollan

《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷讲述图拉的大法师羽蛇神。巫师们来到他身边，其中一位叫蒂特拉卡瓦，也就是特斯卡特利波卡；他们带来的种种征兆让羽蛇神的荣光走向终结。羽蛇神离开时，随行的人在两座雪山之间冻死。

Florentine Codex Book 3 tells of Quetzalcoatl, a great wizard at Tollan. Sorcerers come to him — among them Titlacauan, also called Tezcatlipoca — and their portents bring his glory to an end. As he departs, his followers freeze to death between two snow-capped volcanoes.

  • 特斯卡特利波卡
  • 图拉
  • 离去

01

被当作神的蒂特拉卡瓦Titlacauan, held to be a god

第三卷第2章讲人们怎样把一位叫蒂特拉卡瓦、也叫特斯卡特利波卡的人物当作神来敬奉。

Chapter 2 of Book 3 tells how one called Titlacauan, or Tezcatlipoca, came to be regarded as a god.

Evidence / 证据《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第2章。claim.v0310.mexica.tezca_titlacauan
02

图拉的大法师The great wizard of Tollan

第3章讲羽蛇神——图拉的一位大法师。第4章说，当巫师们来到他身边，他的荣光便走到了尽头；第5章又记下蒂特拉卡瓦带来的另一个异兆。

Chapter 3 tells of Quetzalcoatl, a great wizard at Tollan. Chapter 4 relates how his glory came to an end when the sorcerers came to him, and chapter 5 records another portent brought about by Titlacauan.

Evidence / 证据《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第3–5章。claim.v0310.mexica.quetzal_event本段依据章节标题与概要，具体情节尚未逐句核对。
03

雪山之间Between the snow mountains

第14章记载，羽蛇神离去时，追随他的人经过伊斯塔克特佩特尔与波波卡特佩特尔两座雪山之间，被冻死在冰雪里。

Chapter 14 records that as Quetzalcoatl departs, his vassals freeze to death in the ice as they pass between Iztactepetl and Popocatepetl.

Evidence / 证据《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第14章。claim.v0310.mexica.vassals_froze
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据第三卷第2–5、14章的章节标题与概要；各章细节尚未逐段建模。

墨西卡 / Mexica

一座金字塔上的两位神：大神庙Two gods on one pyramid — the Templo Mayor

特诺奇蒂特兰的大神庙顶上并立着两座神殿：南边供奉太阳战神、墨西加人的守护神维齐洛波奇特利，北边供奉与农业相连的雨神特拉洛克。墨西哥国家人类学与历史研究所说，神庙始建于1325年，此后多次重建与扩建。

Atop the Templo Mayor of Tenochtitlan stood two shrines: on the south, Huitzilopochtli, solar god of war and patron of the Mexica; on the north, Tlaloc, god of rain linked to agriculture. INAH says the first small temple was built at the city's founding in 1325 and was rebuilt and enlarged many times.

  • 战争与雨水
  • 考古遗址

01

南边的战神The war god on the south

大神庙南侧的神殿属于维齐洛波奇特利——墨西加人的太阳战神，也是他们的守护神。

The southern chapel of the Templo Mayor belongs to Huitzilopochtli, the Mexica's solar god of war and their patron.

Evidence / 证据INAH 大神庙遗址博物馆说明。claim.v0310.mexica.huitz_templo
02

北边的雨神The rain god on the north

北侧的神殿属于特拉洛克——掌管雨水、与农业紧密相连的神。战争与农业，两位神并立在同一座金字塔的顶端。

The northern temple belongs to Tlaloc, god of rain and closely tied to agriculture. War and farming stand side by side at the top of one pyramid.

Evidence / 证据INAH 大神庙遗址博物馆说明。claim.v0310.mexica.tlaloc_templo
03

一层层长高的神庙A temple that grew in layers

据 INAH 介绍，墨西加人在1325年建立特诺奇蒂特兰时修了一座小神庙，此后经过多次重建和扩建，越修越高。

According to INAH, the Mexica built a small temple when they founded Tenochtitlan in 1325, and it was rebuilt and enlarged many times over.

Evidence / 证据INAH 大神庙遗址博物馆说明。claim.v0310.mexica.templo_layers各建造阶段的具体年代仍有不确定之处。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

现实遗址层：依据墨西哥国家人类学与历史研究所（INAH）遗址博物馆的公开说明；不推测坐标。

玛雅 / Maya

石头、陶盘与文字中的三位玛雅神Three Maya gods in stone, clay and glyph

三份独立见证，各讲一位玛雅神：奇琴伊察的卡斯蒂略金字塔又叫“库库尔坎神庙”；大都会博物馆的一只陶盘上，雨神恰克半身浸在水中；同一博物馆的展览介绍年老的天神伊察姆纳和他的鸟形化身。三份见证分开阅读，不拼成一个统一神话。

Three separate witnesses, one god each: Chichen-Itza's El Castillo is also called the Temple of Kukulkan; a Met plate shows the rain god Chahk waist-deep in water; and a Met exhibition presents the aged sky god Itzamnaaj and his bird avatar. They are read separately, not stitched into one myth.

  • 库库尔坎
  • 恰克
  • 伊察姆纳
  • 物质见证

Title
Pre-Hispanic City of Chichen-Itza
Institution
UNESCO World Heritage Centre
Locator
World Heritage property 483 — brief synthesis
Rights
Reuse
Witness scope
联合国教科文组织世界遗产 483 号记录
Open source / 打开来源
01

奇琴伊察的金字塔A pyramid at Chichen-Itza

奇琴伊察是尤卡坦半岛上的前西班牙时期古城，已列入世界遗产名录。联合国教科文组织的记录把卡斯蒂略金字塔列为城市北部建筑群中的主要建筑。

Chichen-Itza is a pre-Hispanic city on the Yucatán peninsula, inscribed on the World Heritage List. UNESCO's record names El Castillo among the chief buildings of the city's northern group.

Evidence / 证据联合国教科文组织世界遗产 483 号记录。claim.v0310.maya.castillo_part
02

库库尔坎神庙The Temple of Kukulkan

奇琴伊察北区最重要的建筑是卡斯蒂略金字塔，联合国教科文组织的记录说，它又被称为“库库尔坎神庙”。

The chief monument of Chichen-Itza's northern group is El Castillo, which UNESCO's record says is also known as the Temple of Kukulkan.

Evidence / 证据联合国教科文组织世界遗产 483 号记录。claim.v0310.maya.castillo_kukulkan
03

一个名字的翻译A name in translation

同一记录提到一种最常见的说法：图拉之王“一芦苇·托皮尔钦·羽蛇”占领了这座城市，而“库库尔坎”正是玛雅人对“羽蛇”这个名字的翻译。

The same record cites the most common version: the King of Tula, Ce Acatl Topiltzin Quetzalcoatl, took the city — and 'Kukulkan' is how the Maya translated his name.

Evidence / 证据联合国教科文组织世界遗产 483 号记录。claim.v0310.maya.kukulkan_name这是关于统治者名号的传统说法，不把玛雅与墨西加的神合并为同一实体。

01

水中的雨神The rain god in the water

这只古典期的玛雅陶盘上，雨神恰克半身浸在水波纹里，四周写着表示“海”的象形文字。

On this Classic Maya plate the rain god Chahk stands waist-deep in watery motifs, surrounded by hieroglyphs for 'sea'.

Evidence / 证据大都会艺术博物馆 2021.320 号。claim.v0310.maya.chahk_plate
02

Chahk 这个名字The name Chahk

在古典期的玛雅象形文字里，这位雨神的名字写作 Chahk；人们常写的“恰克”就是这个名字。

In Classic-period Maya hieroglyphs the rain god's name is written Chahk — the familiar 'Chaac'.

Evidence / 证据大都会艺术博物馆藏品说明。claim.v0310.maya.chahk_name
03

进入博物馆Into the museum

The plate entered The Metropolitan Museum of Art in 2021 as a gift of the Mol Collection. The museum's entry describes the image itself; it does not narrate a full rain-god myth.

01

年老的天神The aged god

2022 年的展览按“众神的一生”展开：从婴儿、壮年到老年。其中的老年之神叫伊察姆纳（Itzamnaaj），他还有一个鸟形化身，两者在太初神话中都很重要。

The 2022 exhibition followed the gods through life — infancy, prime and old age. The aged god is Itzamnaaj, who has a bird avatar; both play important roles in primordial myths.

Evidence / 证据大都会艺术博物馆 2022 年展览说明。claim.v0310.maya.itzamnaaj_aged
02

名字仍有疑问A name still in question

“Itzamnaaj”是殖民时期尤卡坦一位重要神祇的名字。博物馆提醒，古典期这位老神的名字符号该怎样读，学界还没有定论。

'Itzamnaaj' is the name of a major deity in colonial Yucatán. The museum notes that how to read the Classic-period name glyph of this elderly god is still uncertain.

Evidence / 证据大都会艺术博物馆展览说明。claim.v0310.maya.itzamnaaj_name名字读法未定，本站同时保留“伊察姆纳”与“Itzamnaaj”。
03

故事还要等更多见证The story awaits more witnesses

这里依据的是一份现代展览介绍，而不是古代文本。展览说伊察姆纳在太初神话中很重要，但没有在说明里讲出那些神话；本站因此不替它补写情节。

This version rests on a modern exhibition text, not an ancient one. The exhibition says Itzamnaaj mattered in primordial myths but does not retell them, so the site does not fill in a plot.

Evidence / 证据大都会艺术博物馆展览说明。claim.v0310.maya.itzamnaaj_scope待找到可定位的古代或殖民时期文本见证后再扩充。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

三个版本分别来自联合国教科文组织遗产记录与大都会艺术博物馆的两份说明；不把玛雅与墨西加的羽蛇神合并。

苏美尔 / Sumerian

恩基与宁胡尔萨格：迪尔蒙的植物Enki and Ninhursaĝa in Dilmun

故事从纯净的迪尔蒙开始。恩基吃掉了那里长出的植物，宁胡尔萨格怒而诅咒他，恩基因此病倒。后来宁胡尔萨格回心转意，为恩基身上每一处病痛生出一位神灵，把他治好。

The story begins in pure Dilmun. Enki eats the plants that have grown there; Ninhursaĝa curses him in anger, and he falls ill. Later she relents and brings forth a deity for each ailing part of his body, healing him.

  • 治愈

苏美尔 / Sumerian

月神南纳航向尼普尔Nanna-Suen sails to Nibru

月神南纳想念父亲恩利尔的城市尼普尔，便装满一船礼物，沿河航行前往。到了尼普尔，恩利尔欢迎他，赐给他满船的丰饶，让他带回自己的城市乌尔。

The moon god Nanna-Suen longs for Nibru, the city of his father Enlil. He loads a boat with gifts and sails there; Enlil welcomes him and grants him abundance to carry home to his own city, Urim.

苏美尔 / Sumerian

太阳神乌图与吉尔伽美什的远征Utu helps Gilgameš set out against Ḫuwawa

吉尔伽美什决定进山建立名声。伙伴恩奇都提醒他：山是太阳神乌图的事，得先告诉乌图。吉尔伽美什向乌图祈求，乌图接受了他的眼泪作为礼物，赐给他七位勇士做向导，去面对山中的守护者胡瓦瓦。

Gilgameš resolves to make his name in the mountains. His companion Enkidu warns that the mountains are Utu's concern, so the sun god must be told. Gilgameš prays, and Utu accepts his tears as a gift and grants him seven warriors as guides to face Ḫuwawa, the guardian of the mountains.

中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese

伏羲始作八卦Fuxi makes the eight trigrams

《周易·系辞下》说：古时包牺氏（伏羲）治理天下，他抬头观察天象，低头观察地理，看鸟兽身上的纹理和各地的物产，近取自身、远取万物，于是开始画出八卦；他又把绳子打结做成网，用来打猎捕鱼。

The Xi Ci II says that when Baoxi (Fuxi) ruled in antiquity, he looked up at heaven and down at the earth, studied the markings of birds and beasts and the land, drew on his own body and on distant things, and first made the eight trigrams; he also knotted cords into nets for hunting and fishing.

  • 伏羲
  • 八卦
  • 文明起源

Title
Book of Changes, Xi Ci II (Great Commentary, part 2)
Institution
Chinese Text Project
Locator
Xi Ci II, ch. 2
Rights
Site terms apply; ancient text is public-domain cultural heritage
Reuse
No bulk copying; cite the chapter page and publish an independent summary.
Witness scope
中国哲学书电子化计划所收《周易·系辞下》传世本
Open source / 打开来源
01

包牺氏治理天下When Baoxi ruled the world

《系辞下》讲古代圣人的发明，第一位就是包牺氏——后世通常写作伏羲。书里说，远古的时候，是他在治理天下。

Xi Ci II lists the inventions of the ancient sages, and the first is Baoxi — usually written Fuxi in later texts. In remote antiquity, it says, he ruled all under heaven.

Evidence / 证据《系辞下》第二章开头。claim.v0310.china.fuxi_baoxi“包牺”即“伏羲”是传统注疏的读法。
02

仰观天，俯察地Looking up, looking down

他抬头观察天上的日月星辰，低头观察大地的形势；又细看鸟兽身上的花纹，看各地适合生长什么。近处，他从自己的身体取法；远处，他从万物取法。

He looked up at the images in the sky and down at the patterns of the earth; he studied the markings of birds and beasts and what each land was suited for. Near at hand he drew on his own body, and far off on all things.

Evidence / 证据《系辞下》第二章。claim.v0310.china.fuxi_observes
03

画出八卦The first eight trigrams

于是他开始画出八卦——八个由三条线组成的符号，用来通达神明的德性，把万物的情状分门别类。

Thereupon he first made the eight trigrams — eight figures of three lines each — to communicate with spirit-like virtue and to sort the dispositions of the myriad things.

Evidence / 证据《系辞下》第二章“于是始作八卦”。claim.v0310.china.fuxi_bagua
04

结绳为网Cords become nets

他还把绳子打结，做成罗网，人们用它打猎、捕鱼。《系辞》接下来再讲神农等后来的圣人，这里只讲伏羲这一段。

He also knotted cords into nets, which people used to hunt and fish. The Xi Ci then moves on to later sages such as Shennong; this retelling covers only Baoxi's part.

Evidence / 证据《系辞下》第二章“作结绳而为罔罟”。claim.v0310.china.fuxi_nets后续圣人的发明不在本故事范围内。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《系辞下》第二章；“包牺即伏羲”采用传统注疏的读法。

中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese

黄帝战蚩尤：涿鹿之战The Yellow Emperor and Chiyou at Zhuolu

《史记·五帝本纪》说：神农氏衰落，诸侯互相攻伐，轩辕操练军队征讨不服者。蚩尤最为暴虐，作乱不听帝命。轩辕征召诸侯之兵，在涿鹿的原野擒杀蚩尤，诸侯于是尊他为天子，这就是黄帝。

The Shiji's Annals of the Five Emperors say that as Shennong's house declined and the lords fought one another, Xuanyuan trained troops. Chiyou, the most violent, rebelled against the ruler's command; Xuanyuan levied the lords' armies, captured and killed him in the wilds of Zhuolu, and was honoured as Son of Heaven — the Yellow Emperor.

  • 黄帝
  • 蚩尤
  • 战争
  • 上古帝王

Open source / 打开来源
01

神农氏衰落Shennong's line declines

黄帝是少典的儿子，姓公孙，名轩辕。他那个时代，神农氏的统治衰落了，诸侯互相攻打、欺压百姓，神农氏却无力征讨。轩辕于是操练军队，去讨伐不肯朝贡的诸侯。

The Yellow Emperor was the son of Shaodian, surnamed Gongsun and named Xuanyuan. In his time the rule of Shennong had declined; the lords attacked one another and oppressed the people, and Shennong could not punish them. So Xuanyuan trained troops to subdue the lords who would not pay tribute.

Evidence / 证据《五帝本纪》开篇。claim.v0310.china.huangdi_identity此前轩辕与炎帝在阪泉的三次交战本故事未展开。
02

蚩尤作乱Chiyou rebels

诸侯之中，蚩尤最为凶暴，没有人能制服他。蚩尤起来作乱，不听从帝王的命令。

Among the lords Chiyou was the most violent, and no one could subdue him. Chiyou rose in rebellion and would not obey the ruler's command.

Evidence / 证据《五帝本纪》“蚩尤作乱，不用帝命”。claim.v0310.china.chiyou_rebels
03

涿鹿之野In the wilds of Zhuolu

轩辕征召各路诸侯的军队，在涿鹿的原野上与蚩尤交战，终于把蚩尤擒住并杀死。

Xuanyuan called up the armies of the lords and fought Chiyou in the wilds of Zhuolu, and at last captured and killed him.

Evidence / 证据《五帝本纪》“遂禽杀蚩尤”。claim.v0310.china.huangdi_kills_chiyou涿鹿不在本站映射为现代坐标。
04

尊为天子Honoured as Son of Heaven

诸侯们都尊奉轩辕为天子，取代了神农氏，这就是黄帝。

The lords all honoured Xuanyuan as Son of Heaven in place of Shennong: this was the Yellow Emperor.

Evidence / 证据《五帝本纪》“诸侯咸尊轩辕为天子”。claim.v0310.china.huangdi_son_of_heaven
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《史记·五帝本纪》；司马迁以史笔叙述，本站只登记文本所述，不作历史真实性断言。

中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese

盘古开天地：《三五历纪》佚文Pangu between heaven and earth

三国吴人徐整的《三五历纪》已经失传，只在后世类书中留有引文。《艺文类聚》卷一引它说：天地未分时混沌如鸡蛋，盘古生在其中；一万八千年后天地开辟，清者为天、浊者为地；天每天高一丈，地每天厚一丈，盘古每天长一丈，又过一万八千年，天极高、地极深、盘古极长。

Xu Zheng's Sanwu liji is lost, surviving only in later encyclopedias. Yiwen leiju juan 1 quotes it: before heaven and earth parted, all was chaos like a hen's egg, and Pangu was born inside; after 18,000 years they opened, clear becoming heaven and murky becoming earth; each day heaven rose a zhang, earth thickened a zhang and Pangu grew a zhang, until after another 18,000 years heaven was highest, earth deepest and Pangu tallest.

  • 盘古
  • 创世
  • 天地开辟

维基文库所收《艺文类聚》卷一（据明本补）
Open source / 打开来源
01

混沌如鸡蛋Chaos like an egg

很久以前，天和地还没有分开，到处是一团混沌，样子像一个鸡蛋。盘古就生在这团混沌之中。

Long ago heaven and earth were not yet apart; everything was one chaos shaped like a hen's egg, and Pangu was born inside it.

Evidence / 证据《艺文类聚》卷一引《三五历纪》；《太平御览》卷一引文字句略异。claim.v0310.china.pangu_egg原书已佚，只能依靠类书引文。
02

清者为天，浊者为地Clear above, murky below

过了一万八千年，天地开辟：轻而清的阳气上升成为天，重而浊的阴气下沉成为地。盘古就在天地之间，一天之中变化九次，比天更神，比地更圣。

After 18,000 years heaven and earth opened: the light, clear yang rose to become heaven, the heavy, murky yin sank to become earth. Pangu stood between them, changing nine times a day, more divine than heaven and wiser than earth.

Evidence / 证据同一引文“阳清为天，阴浊为地”。claim.v0310.china.pangu_separation
03

一天长高一丈A zhang a day

从此天每天升高一丈，地每天加厚一丈，盘古也每天长高一丈。这样又过了一万八千年，天高到了极点，地深到了极点，盘古也长到了极点。在这之后，才有了三皇。

From then on heaven rose a zhang each day, earth grew a zhang thicker, and Pangu grew a zhang taller. After another 18,000 years heaven was as high as it could be, earth as deep, and Pangu as tall. Only after that came the Three Sovereigns.

Evidence / 证据同一引文“天日高一丈……盘古日长一丈”。claim.v0310.china.pangu_growth“盘古垂死化身为日月山川”出自另一部书，本故事不合并。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《艺文类聚》卷一所引《三五历纪》，并以《太平御览》卷一的引文对照；盘古身体化生万物另见他书，不在此合并。

中国民间信仰 / Chinese Folk Religion

哪吒大战孙悟空：《西游记》第四回Nezha against Sun Wukong (Journey to the West, ch. 4)

孙悟空嫌弼马温官小，回花果山自称齐天大圣。托塔李天王与哪吒三太子奉命捉拿。哪吒变出三头六臂，悟空也变三头六臂；悟空拔毫毛变出分身，真身绕到背后打中哪吒，哪吒负痛败走。

Finding his stable post too lowly, Sun Wukong returns home and calls himself Great Sage Equal to Heaven. Heavenly King Li and his third son Nezha are sent to seize him. Nezha takes three heads and six arms; Wukong does the same, then turns a hair into a double and strikes Nezha from behind, and Nezha retreats in pain.

  • 哪吒
  • 孙悟空
  • 变化
  • 明代小说

Open source / 打开来源
01

天兵来讨Heaven sends its army

孙悟空发现“弼马温”只是个看马的小官，一气之下回到花果山，自称“齐天大圣”。玉帝派托塔李天王和他的三太子哪吒，带领天兵前去捉拿。

Discovering that his title merely made him a keeper of horses, Sun Wukong storms back to his mountain and calls himself Great Sage Equal to Heaven. The Jade Emperor sends Heavenly King Li, bearer of the pagoda, and his third son Nezha with the heavenly troops to seize him.

Evidence / 证据《西游记》第四回。claim.v0310.china.nezha_title
02

三头六臂Three heads, six arms

哪吒大喝一声，变成三头六臂，六只手各拿一件兵器打来。悟空见了，也变成三头六臂，把金箍棒一晃变成三条，六只手拿着架住。

Nezha gives a shout and becomes a figure with three heads and six arms, a weapon in every hand. Wukong does the same: he shakes his staff into three and parries with six hands.

Evidence / 证据《西游记》第四回。claim.v0310.china.nezha_form
03

毫毛分身A hair becomes a double

两人斗得难分难解。悟空拔下一根毫毛，变成自己的模样继续迎敌，真身却绕到哪吒背后打了一棒。哪吒负痛败走，回去禀报父王：这猴子神通广大。

Neither can win. Wukong plucks a hair and turns it into a double of himself to keep fighting, while his true body circles behind Nezha and strikes. Nezha flees in pain and reports to his father that the monkey's powers are great.

Evidence / 证据《西游记》第四回。claim.v0310.china.wukong_defeats_nezha这是小说情节，属于后世文学层。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

明代小说《西游记》属于后世文学接受层，不当作古代神话见证；哪吒闹海等故事出自其他作品，未在此合并。

爱尔兰 / Irish

第二次马格图雷德之战：达格达、莫莉甘、布里格与鲁格The Second Battle of Mag Tuired

中世纪爱尔兰传说《第二次马格图雷德之战》：达格达拥有“没有人吃不饱”的大锅；“百艺之主”鲁格来到塔拉；萨温节时达格达在河边遇见莫莉甘；布里格为战死的儿子哀哭，是爱尔兰第一次听到哀歌；鲁格用投石击毁巴洛尔的毒眼；战后莫莉甘向全爱尔兰宣告胜利。

The medieval Irish saga Cath Maige Tuired: the Dagda's cauldron leaves no one unsatisfied; Lug, master of all arts, comes to Tara; at Samain the Dagda meets the Morrígan by the river; Bríg keens for her slain son, the first keening in Ireland; Lug's sling stone drives Balor's deadly eye through his head; and the Morrígan proclaims the victory across Ireland.

  • 达格达
  • 莫莉甘
  • 布里格
  • 鲁格
  • 战争

Open source / 打开来源
01

达格达的大锅The Dagda's cauldron

达努神族带来了四件宝物，其中一件是从穆里亚斯城带来的达格达的大锅：凡是围着它吃饭的人，从没有一个是不饱足地离开的。

The Túatha Dé Danann brought four treasures. One was the Dagda's cauldron from the city of Murias: no company that ate from it ever went away unsatisfied.

Evidence / 证据Gray 本 §6。claim.v0310.irish.dagda_cauldron
02

百艺之主来到塔拉The master of all arts at Tara

鲁格来到塔拉的大门前，守门人问他会什么手艺。他报出一门又一门，而宫里每门手艺都已有人；最后他问：有没有一个人能同时精通所有这些？于是他以“百艺之主”的身份被请了进去。

Lug comes to the door of Tara, and the doorkeeper asks what art he brings. He names one after another, and each already has a master within — until he asks whether anyone there has all of them at once. So he is admitted as the Samildánach, master of all arts.

Evidence / 证据Gray 本 §§53–72。claim.v0310.irish.lugh_samildanach
03

萨温节的河边By the river at Samain

萨温节前后，达格达在乌恩辛河边遇见一个女人正在洗东西，一只脚踩在南岸，一只脚踩在北岸。她就是莫莉甘。两人结合之后，她告诉达格达弗魔族将在哪里登陆，并许诺要取弗魔王因德赫的心血。

Around Samain the Dagda meets a woman washing at the river Unshin, one foot on the southern bank and one on the northern. She is the Morrígan. After they unite she tells him where the Fomoire will land and promises to take the heart's blood of their king Indech.

Evidence / 证据Gray 本 §84。claim.v0310.irish.dagda_morrigan
04

第一次哀歌The first keening

布里格是达格达的女儿，她和布雷斯的儿子鲁阿丹被派去刺杀铁匠戈伊布纽，却被戈伊布纽杀死。布里格赶来为儿子哀哭，先是尖声恸哭，最后低声啜泣——这是爱尔兰第一次听到哀歌与哭泣。

Rúadán, son of Bres and of Bríg the Dagda's daughter, is sent to kill the smith Goibniu and is killed instead. Bríg comes and keens for her son, shrieking first and weeping at the end — the first time keening and weeping were heard in Ireland.

Evidence / 证据Gray 本 §§123–125。claim.v0310.irish.brig_keens文本写作 Bríg；与数据库中“布里吉德”实体的对应依学界通行用法。
05

巴洛尔的毒眼Balor's deadly eye

弗魔族勇士巴洛尔有一只毒眼，只在战场上睁开，要四个人用把手才能抬起眼皮。鲁格用投石一击，石头把那只眼睛从巴洛尔脑后打了出去，结果看见它的反而是他自己的军队。

The Fomorian champion Balor has a deadly eye, opened only on the battlefield, its lid lifted by four men with a handle. Lug casts a sling stone that drives the eye out through the back of Balor's head, so that it is his own host that looks upon it.

Evidence / 证据Gray 本 §§133–135。claim.v0310.irish.lugh_kills_balor
06

莫莉甘宣告胜利The Morrígan proclaims the victory

战斗结束后，恩玛斯的女儿莫莉甘把这场大胜宣告给爱尔兰的王者高地、神丘里的众灵、各条大河与河口，然后又说出关于未来的预言。

When the battle is won, the Morrígan, daughter of Ernmas, proclaims the great victory to the royal heights of Ireland, its síd-hosts, its chief waters and river-mouths — and then speaks prophecy.

Evidence / 证据Gray 本 §§166–167。claim.v0310.irish.morrigan_proclaims预言诗的内容未逐句建模。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据 Elizabeth A. Gray 1982 年校译本（CELT T300010）的段落编号；译文受版权保护，只发布定位与独立概述。

斯拉夫 / Slavic

基辅山岗上的神像：佩伦与莫科什The idols on the Kyiv hill — Perun and Mokosh

《往年纪事》980 年条：弗拉基米尔在基辅宫殿外的山岗上立起神像——银头金须的木雕佩伦，还有霍尔斯、达日博格、斯特里博格、西马尔格尔和莫科什，人们向它们献祭。988 年他受洗后下令推倒神像，佩伦被绑在马尾上拖下山，丢进第聂伯河。

The Primary Chronicle's annal for 980: Volodimer sets up idols on the hill outside his Kyiv palace — a wooden Perun with a silver head and golden moustache, then Khors, Dazhbog, Stribog, Simargl and Mokosh — and people make offerings to them. After his baptism in 988 he has them cast down; Perun is tied to a horse's tail, dragged down the hill and thrown into the Dnieper.

  • 佩伦
  • 莫科什
  • 神像
  • 编年史

Open source / 打开来源
01

山岗上的神像Idols on the hill

《往年纪事》980 年条记载：弗拉基米尔开始在基辅独自为王，就在宫殿庭院外的山岗上立起一排神像。

The Primary Chronicle's annal for 980 records that when Volodimer began to reign alone in Kyiv, he set up a row of idols on the hill outside the palace courtyard.

Evidence / 证据《往年纪事》980 年条。claim.v0310.slavic.pvl_980_witness
02

银头金须的佩伦Perun with a silver head

最前面的是佩伦：木头雕成，头是银的，胡须是金的。

First stood Perun, carved of wood, with a silver head and a golden moustache.

Evidence / 证据《往年纪事》980 年条。claim.v060.slavic.perun_980_image
03

名单最后的莫科什Mokosh at the end of the list

接下来是霍尔斯、达日博格、斯特里博格、西马尔格尔，名单的最后一位是莫科什。人们向这些神像献上祭品。编年史只记下了莫科什的名字，没有说她掌管什么。

Then came Khors, Dazhbog, Stribog and Simargl, and last on the list, Mokosh. People brought offerings to the idols. The chronicle records only Mokosh's name, not what she governed.

Evidence / 证据《往年纪事》980 年条神名名单。claim.v0310.slavic.mokosh_980关于莫科什职能的说法来自其他材料与现代研究，本故事不补写。
04

988 年：神像倒下988: the idols fall

八年后，弗拉基米尔受洗，下令推倒这些神像：有的砍碎，有的烧掉。佩伦被绑在马尾上拖下山，有人用棍子抽打，最后被扔进第聂伯河。

Eight years later Volodimer was baptized and ordered the idols overthrown — some cut to pieces, others burned. Perun was tied to a horse's tail and dragged down the hill, beaten with sticks, and thrown into the Dnieper.

Evidence / 证据《往年纪事》988 年条。claim.v0310.slavic.perun_cast_down这是皈依一方的记载，不是对斯拉夫宗教的中立描述。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据《往年纪事》980 与 988 年条；编年史由皈依后的基督教修士编写，对旧信仰的描述带有立场；莫科什在此只有名字。

琐罗亚斯德传统 / Zoroastrian

广阔牧场之主密特拉：《密赫尔颂》Mithra of the wide pastures — the Mihr Yasht

阿维斯塔第十颂《密赫尔颂》：阿胡拉·马兹达说，他创造密特拉时，使他与自己同样值得祭祀和祈祷；对密特拉说谎、背弃契约的人会给整个国家带来死亡；密特拉有千只耳朵、万只眼睛，在不朽的快马太阳之前最先越过哈拉山。

The Mihr Yasht, tenth hymn of the Avesta: Ahura Mazda says he made Mithra as worthy of sacrifice and prayer as himself; whoever lies to Mithra and breaks the contract brings death to the whole land; Mithra has a thousand ears and ten thousand eyes and is the first god to rise over Mount Hara, before the swift-horsed sun.

  • 密特拉
  • 契约
  • 阿维斯塔

Public-domain translation; site transcription
Reuse
Summarize respectfully; no ritual instructions; cite section numbers.
Witness scope
avesta.org 所收达梅斯特泰尔《东方圣书》译本
Open source / 打开来源
01

与造物主同样尊贵As worthy as his maker

颂歌一开头，阿胡拉·马兹达对查拉图斯特拉说：我创造广阔牧场之主密特拉时，使他与我自己一样，值得献祭、值得祈祷。

The hymn opens with Ahura Mazda telling Zarathustra: when I created Mithra, lord of wide pastures, I made him as worthy of sacrifice and as worthy of prayer as myself.

Evidence / 证据Yt 10.1。claim.v0310.zoro.mithra_created
02

不可背弃契约Never break the contract

对密特拉说谎、背弃契约的恶人，会给整个国家带来死亡。所以契约绝不可背弃——不论是与信徒订立的，还是与不信者订立的。

The wretch who lies to Mithra, breaking the contract, brings death upon the whole land. So the contract must never be broken, whether made with one of the faithful or with an unbeliever.

Evidence / 证据Yt 10.2。claim.v0310.zoro.mithra_contract“契约”是译者对 miθra 一词的理解，学界另有讨论。
03

千耳万眼A thousand ears, ten thousand eyes

颂歌赞美密特拉说真话，是集会中的首领；他有一千只耳朵，一万只眼睛，什么都瞒不过他。

The hymn praises Mithra as truth-speaking, a chief in assemblies, with a thousand ears and ten thousand eyes — nothing escapes him.

Evidence / 证据Yt 10.7。claim.v0310.zoro.mithra_eyes
04

越过哈拉山Over Mount Hara

每天清晨，他是天上诸神中第一个越过哈拉山的，比不朽的、驾着快马的太阳还要早。

Each morning he is the first of the heavenly gods to reach over Mount Hara, ahead of the undying, swift-horsed sun.

Evidence / 证据Yt 10.13。claim.v0310.zoro.mithra_hara
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据达梅斯特泰尔译本（avesta.org）第10颂第1、2、7、13节；现代学者对诗句的理解另有讨论。

琐罗亚斯德传统 / Zoroastrian

十六片良地与它们的灾祸：《万迪达德》第一章Sixteen good lands and their plagues — Videvdad 1

《万迪达德》第一章：阿胡拉·马兹达创造了十六片良地，第一片是善河岱提亚旁的雅利安瓦伊贾；每创造一片，“全然是死亡”的安格拉·曼纽就造出一种灾祸与之对抗——第一片是河中之蛇和寒冬，第二片是蝗虫。

Videvdad 1: Ahura Mazda makes sixteen good lands, the first being Airyana Vaejah by the good river Daitya; for each, Angra Mainyu, who is all death, counter-creates a plague — the serpent in the river and winter for the first, the locust for the second.

  • 安格拉·曼纽
  • 阿胡拉·马兹达
  • 创造与反创造

Open source / 打开来源
01

第一片良地The first good land

阿胡拉·马兹达说：我所创造的良地中，第一片是雅利安瓦伊贾，在善河岱提亚旁边。

Ahura Mazda says: the first of the good lands I created was Airyana Vaejah, by the good river Daitya.

Evidence / 证据第一章第一片良地。claim.v0310.zoro.airyana_vaejah雅利安瓦伊贾不映射为现代坐标。
02

全然是死亡的对手The one who is all death

随后，“全然是死亡”的安格拉·曼纽来了，他针对这片良地造出了河中之蛇和寒冬，那是恶魔们的作品。

Then came Angra Mainyu, who is all death, and against it he counter-created the serpent in the river and winter, a work of the daevas.

Evidence / 证据第一章第一片良地。claim.v0310.zoro.angra_counter
03

十六地，十六灾Sixteen lands, sixteen plagues

名单一片接一片地继续：第二片是粟特人居住的平原，安格拉·曼纽就造出蝗虫。如此一共列出十六片良地，每一片都对应一种反创造的灾祸。

The list goes on land by land: the second is the plain where the Sughdhas dwell, and Angra Mainyu makes the locust. In all, sixteen good lands are named, each answered by a counter-created plague.

Evidence / 证据第一章第二片良地及以下。claim.v0310.zoro.locust译者认为这是宗教地理，混合了神话之地与真实国家；只有部分地名可以确认。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

依据达梅斯特泰尔译本（avesta.org）第一章；译者认为这是伊朗的宗教地理，而不是迁徙记录。

夏威夷 / Hawaiian

佩蕾的旅程：从卡希基到哈雷茂茂Pele's journey to Halemaʻumaʻu

美国国家公园管理局概述夏威夷口述传统：火山女神佩蕾生在遥远的卡希基，为寻找适合她的火与家人的居所，远航来到夏威夷群岛，一路向东南迁移，最后在基拉韦厄山顶的哈雷茂茂火山口安家，据说至今仍住在那里。按照夏威夷传统，经过她的土地要先请求许可。

The National Park Service summarizes Hawaiian oral history: the volcano deity Pele was born in distant Kahiki and voyaged to the Hawaiian Islands seeking a home for her fire and family, moving southeast until she settled in Halemaʻumaʻu crater at the summit of Kīlauea, where she is said still to live. In Hawaiian tradition one asks her permission to travel through her land.

  • 佩蕾
  • 火山
  • 旅程
  • 活态传统

Open source / 打开来源
01

生在卡希基Born in Kahiki

国家公园管理局介绍说，按照口述传统，佩蕾是夏威夷的火山之神，是一股自然之力，也是这些火山地貌的创造者。她出生在遥远的卡希基，为了给自己的火和家人寻找合适的家，乘船来到夏威夷群岛。

The National Park Service explains that, in oral history, Pele is the Hawaiian volcano deity, an elemental force and the creator of these volcanic landscapes. Born in the distant land of Kahiki, she voyaged to the Hawaiian Islands to find a suitable home for her fire and family.

Evidence / 证据NPS“Pele”与“Holo Mai Pele”页面。claim.v0310.hawaii.pele_kahiki
02

一路向东南Island by island to the southeast

她一座岛一座岛地向东南迁移，寻找能安顿下来的地方。

She moved southeast through the islands, one after another, looking for a place to settle.

Evidence / 证据NPS“Pele”页面。claim.v0310.hawaii.pele_southeast各岛上的具体经历因版本而异，本站不补写。
03

哈雷茂茂的家A home in Halemaʻumaʻu

最后她来到基拉韦厄山顶的哈雷茂茂火山口，把这里定为永久的家。据说她至今仍住在那里。

At last she came to Halemaʻumaʻu crater at the summit of Kīlauea and made it her permanent home. She is said to live there still.

Evidence / 证据NPS 两页介绍。claim.v0310.hawaii.pele_halemaumau
04

经过她的土地Passing through her land

在夏威夷传统中，经过佩蕾的土地要先请求她的许可。国家公园管理局也请访客在这片神圣的地景中保持尊重。公园还提醒：不同的 moʻolelo 版本可能差别很大。

In Hawaiian tradition it is customary to ask Pele's permission to travel through her land, and the park asks visitors to behave respectfully in this sacred landscape. It also notes that versions of the moʻolelo may differ, sometimes greatly.

Evidence / 证据NPS“Pele”页面。claim.v0310.hawaii.pele_permission这里只讲公开概述；家族与地方传统不在收集范围。
Editorial boundary / 编辑边界

须署名的公开概述：依据美国国家公园管理局两页公开介绍；各家族与地方的 moʻolelo 版本差异很大，本站不收集。
