中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese · 有完整故事Full story

伏羲 Fuxi

《系辞下》中的包牺氏（伏羲）：仰观天象、俯察地法，始作八卦，又结绳为网。

Baoxi (Fuxi) in Xi Ci II: the ancient ruler who observed heaven, earth and living things, first made the eight trigrams and knotted nets.

  1. 01

    包牺氏治理天下When Baoxi ruled the world

    《系辞下》讲古代圣人的发明，第一位就是包牺氏——后世通常写作伏羲。书里说，远古的时候，是他在治理天下。

    Xi Ci II lists the inventions of the ancient sages, and the first is Baoxi — usually written Fuxi in later texts. In remote antiquity, it says, he ruled all under heaven.

    Book of Changes, Xi Ci II (Great Commentary, part 2) · Xi Ci II, ch. 2 · 《系辞下》第二章开头。“包牺”即“伏羲”是传统注疏的读法。
  2. 02

    仰观天，俯察地Looking up, looking down

    他抬头观察天上的日月星辰，低头观察大地的形势；又细看鸟兽身上的花纹，看各地适合生长什么。近处，他从自己的身体取法；远处，他从万物取法。

    He looked up at the images in the sky and down at the patterns of the earth; he studied the markings of birds and beasts and what each land was suited for. Near at hand he drew on his own body, and far off on all things.

    Book of Changes, Xi Ci II (Great Commentary, part 2) · Xi Ci II, ch. 2 · 《系辞下》第二章。
  3. 03

    画出八卦The first eight trigrams

    于是他开始画出八卦——八个由三条线组成的符号，用来通达神明的德性，把万物的情状分门别类。

    Thereupon he first made the eight trigrams — eight figures of three lines each — to communicate with spirit-like virtue and to sort the dispositions of the myriad things.

    Book of Changes, Xi Ci II (Great Commentary, part 2) · Xi Ci II, ch. 2 · 《系辞下》第二章“于是始作八卦”。
  4. 04

    结绳为网Cords become nets

    他还把绳子打结，做成罗网，人们用它打猎、捕鱼。《系辞》接下来再讲神农等后来的圣人，这里只讲伏羲这一段。

    He also knotted cords into nets, which people used to hunt and fish. The Xi Ci then moves on to later sages such as Shennong; this retelling covers only Baoxi's part.

    Book of Changes, Xi Ci II (Great Commentary, part 2) · Xi Ci II, ch. 2 · 《系辞下》第二章“作结绳而为罔罟”。后续圣人的发明不在本故事范围内。
中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese · 有完整故事Full story

共工 Gonggong

Figure who contends with Zhuanxu and strikes Mount Buzhou in the Huainanzi Tian Wen and Liezi Tang Wen witnesses; whether deity, clan head or rival ruler is foregrounded varies by source and is not resolved here.

  1. 01

    共工与颛顼争为帝Gonggong contends with Zhuanxu

    《淮南子·天文训》说，从前共工与颛顼争夺帝位。

    Huainanzi “Tian Wen” says that long ago Gonggong contended with Zhuanxu to become ruler.

    Huainanzi, Tian Wen Xun · Huainanzi, Tian Wen Xun — passage opening 昔者共工 · 《淮南子·天文训》“昔者共工”段。颛顼在此作为传说中的君主登记，不作历史真实性判断。
  2. 02

    怒触不周之山Striking Mount Buzhou in anger

    共工发怒撞向不周山，支撑天的柱子折断，系住大地的绳索断绝。

    In anger Gonggong strikes Mount Buzhou; the pillar holding up heaven breaks and the cords binding the earth snap.

    Huainanzi, Tian Wen Xun · Huainanzi, Tian Wen Xun — passage opening 昔者共工 · 同段不周山、天柱与地维句组。不周山不映射为现代山名或坐标。
  3. 03

    一段独立的起源解释A standalone etiology

    结果天向西北倾斜，日月星辰随之移动；地在东南不满，积水与尘埃都归向那里。这一段没有提到女娲，是《天文训》中独立的天地形势解释。

    As a result heaven tilts to the northwest and the sun, moon and stars shift with it; the earth falls short in the southeast, where waters and dust collect. The passage does not mention Nüwa and stands as an independent explanation of cosmic orientation in “Tian Wen”.

    Huainanzi, Tian Wen Xun · Huainanzi, Tian Wen Xun — passage opening 昔者共工 · 《淮南子·天文训》共工段结尾。同书《览冥训》的女娲段与此段分属不同章节，不合成单一故事。
中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese · 有完整故事Full story

女娲 Nüwa

  1. 01

    四极废，九州裂The four poles collapse

    《淮南子·览冥训》以远古为背景：支撑天地的四极崩坏，九州开裂，天不能全覆、地不能全载，大火与洪水都无法止息，猛兽鸷鸟危害百姓。本段只按这一见证的顺序概述，不补入其他文献给出的起因。

    Huainanzi “Lan Ming” sets the scene in remote antiquity: the four poles holding up the world have failed and the nine provinces have split; heaven no longer covers everything and earth no longer bears everything, fires and floods cannot be stopped, and wild beasts and birds of prey attack the people. This section follows only this witness and adds no cause taken from other texts.

    Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun · Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun — passage opening 往古之時 · 《淮南子·览冥训》“往古之时”段；Chinese Text Project 章节页。传世本用字存在异体；本站只发布独立概述，不复制整段原文。
  2. 02

    炼五色石，断鳌足Five-colored stones and the turtle's legs

    于是女娲炼五色石来修补苍天，又截断巨鳌的足，用来重新立起四极。

    Nüwa then refines five-colored stones to patch the azure sky and cuts off the legs of a great turtle to set the four poles upright again.

    Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun · Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun — passage opening 往古之時 · 同段女娲补天与立四极的句组。炼字与鳌字在不同版本中有异体，不影响本段概述。
  3. 03

    杀黑龙，积芦灰The black dragon and the reed ash

    这一见证还写到女娲杀死黑龙以救济冀州，并堆积芦苇灰来堵住泛滥的洪水；随后天地重新安定，百姓得以存活。

    This witness adds that Nüwa kills a black dragon to relieve Jizhou and piles up reed ash to stop the flood waters; afterwards heaven and earth are set right and the people survive.

    Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun · Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun — passage opening 往古之時 · 同段黑龙、冀州与芦灰句组。冀州在此是文本中的九州之一，不映射为现代行政区或坐标；《列子·汤问》对应段落未陈述这两项。
  4. 04

    这一段没有写共工No Gonggong in this passage

    《览冥训》的女娲段落没有把天地崩坏归因于共工。《淮南子》确实在《天文训》另记共工怒触不周山，但那是另一章中独立的天地倾斜起源，本站不把两章拼接成因果链。

    The Lan Ming Nüwa passage does not attribute the collapse to Gonggong. The Huainanzi does narrate Gonggong striking Mount Buzhou, but in a separate passage in the “Tian Wen” chapter that explains the tilt of heaven and earth; the site does not splice the two chapters into a causal chain.

    Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun · Huainanzi, Lan Ming Xun — passage opening 往古之時 · 《览冥训》女娲段与《天文训》共工段分别定位。把共工触山写成女娲补天起因的说法见于较晚文献，已作为独立接受层排队。
中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese · 有完整故事Full story

盘古 Pangu

徐整《三五历纪》佚文中的盘古：生于如鸡子的混沌之中，天地分开时日长一丈。

Pangu as quoted from Xu Zheng's lost Sanwu liji: born in an egg-like chaos, he grows a zhang a day as heaven and earth separate.

  1. 01

    混沌如鸡蛋Chaos like an egg

    很久以前，天和地还没有分开，到处是一团混沌，样子像一个鸡蛋。盘古就生在这团混沌之中。

    Long ago heaven and earth were not yet apart; everything was one chaos shaped like a hen's egg, and Pangu was born inside it.

    Yiwen leiju, juan 1 (Heaven, part 1) · juan 1, Heaven · 《艺文类聚》卷一引《三五历纪》；《太平御览》卷一引文字句略异。原书已佚，只能依靠类书引文。
  2. 02

    清者为天，浊者为地Clear above, murky below

    过了一万八千年，天地开辟：轻而清的阳气上升成为天，重而浊的阴气下沉成为地。盘古就在天地之间，一天之中变化九次，比天更神，比地更圣。

    After 18,000 years heaven and earth opened: the light, clear yang rose to become heaven, the heavy, murky yin sank to become earth. Pangu stood between them, changing nine times a day, more divine than heaven and wiser than earth.

    Yiwen leiju, juan 1 (Heaven, part 1) · juan 1, Heaven · 同一引文“阳清为天，阴浊为地”。
  3. 03

    一天长高一丈A zhang a day

    从此天每天升高一丈，地每天加厚一丈，盘古也每天长高一丈。这样又过了一万八千年，天高到了极点，地深到了极点，盘古也长到了极点。在这之后，才有了三皇。

    From then on heaven rose a zhang each day, earth grew a zhang thicker, and Pangu grew a zhang taller. After another 18,000 years heaven was as high as it could be, earth as deep, and Pangu as tall. Only after that came the Three Sovereigns.

    Yiwen leiju, juan 1 (Heaven, part 1) · juan 1, Heaven · 同一引文“天日高一丈……盘古日长一丈”。“盘古垂死化身为日月山川”出自另一部书，本故事不合并。
中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese · 有完整故事Full story

雷泽雷神 Thunder Spirit of Leize

《山海经·海内东经》的雷泽无名雷神；与后世雷公不自动合并。

Unnamed thunder spirit of Leize in the Classic of Mountains and Seas; not automatically merged with later Leigong.

  1. 01

    雷泽中的神A spirit in Leize

    《山海经·海内东经》的登记段落在雷泽描写一位无名雷神。

    The registered Hai Nei Dong Jing passage describes an unnamed thunder spirit in Leize.

    Classic of Mountains and Seas, Hai Nei Dong Jing · Hai Nei Dong Jing · 《山海经·海内东经》。“雷泽雷神”是数据库定位名，不是文本给出的专名。
  2. 02

    击腹发雷Thunder from the belly

    该段把雷声与神灵击打腹部相连，系统把它保存为文本中的神话叙述。

    The passage connects thunder with the spirit striking its belly, and the system preserves this as a mythic statement in the text.

    Classic of Mountains and Seas, Hai Nei Dong Jing · Hai Nei Dong Jing · 《山海经·海内东经》。不把神话陈述当作自然科学解释。
  3. 03

    与后世雷公分开Separate from later Leigong

    因为文本没有给出后世雷公的专名与完整形象，系统不按“雷神”这个共通标签自动合并。

    Because the passage does not give the later proper name and full image of Leigong, the system does not merge the figures under the generic label thunder god.

    Classic of Mountains and Seas, Hai Nei Dong Jing · Hai Nei Dong Jing · 身份区分研究备注。后世接受史将在独立版本中处理。
中国上古神话 / Ancient Chinese · 有完整故事Full story

黄帝 Yellow Emperor

《史记·五帝本纪》中少典之子轩辕，于涿鹿擒杀蚩尤并被尊为天子；本站不作历史真实性断言。

Xuanyuan, son of Shaodian, who in the Shiji fights Chiyou at Zhuolu and is honoured as Son of Heaven; no historical-reality claim is made.

  1. 01

    神农氏衰落Shennong's line declines

    黄帝是少典的儿子，姓公孙，名轩辕。他那个时代，神农氏的统治衰落了，诸侯互相攻打、欺压百姓，神农氏却无力征讨。轩辕于是操练军队，去讨伐不肯朝贡的诸侯。

    The Yellow Emperor was the son of Shaodian, surnamed Gongsun and named Xuanyuan. In his time the rule of Shennong had declined; the lords attacked one another and oppressed the people, and Shennong could not punish them. So Xuanyuan trained troops to subdue the lords who would not pay tribute.

    Shiji, Annals of the Five Emperors · Wu di ben ji · 《五帝本纪》开篇。此前轩辕与炎帝在阪泉的三次交战本故事未展开。
  2. 02

    蚩尤作乱Chiyou rebels

    诸侯之中，蚩尤最为凶暴，没有人能制服他。蚩尤起来作乱，不听从帝王的命令。

    Among the lords Chiyou was the most violent, and no one could subdue him. Chiyou rose in rebellion and would not obey the ruler's command.

    Shiji, Annals of the Five Emperors · Wu di ben ji · 《五帝本纪》“蚩尤作乱，不用帝命”。
  3. 03

    涿鹿之野In the wilds of Zhuolu

    轩辕征召各路诸侯的军队，在涿鹿的原野上与蚩尤交战，终于把蚩尤擒住并杀死。

    Xuanyuan called up the armies of the lords and fought Chiyou in the wilds of Zhuolu, and at last captured and killed him.

    Shiji, Annals of the Five Emperors · Wu di ben ji · 《五帝本纪》“遂禽杀蚩尤”。涿鹿不在本站映射为现代坐标。
  4. 04

    尊为天子Honoured as Son of Heaven

    诸侯们都尊奉轩辕为天子，取代了神农氏，这就是黄帝。

    The lords all honoured Xuanyuan as Son of Heaven in place of Shennong: this was the Yellow Emperor.

    Shiji, Annals of the Five Emperors · Wu di ben ji · 《五帝本纪》“诸侯咸尊轩辕为天子”。
中国民间信仰 / Chinese Folk Religion · 有完整故事Full story

哪吒 Nezha

《西游记》第四回中托塔李天王的三太子，变三头六臂迎战孙悟空（后世文学层）。

Third prince of Heavenly King Li in Journey to the West ch. 4, who takes three heads and six arms against Sun Wukong (later literary layer).

  1. 01

    天兵来讨Heaven sends its army

    孙悟空发现“弼马温”只是个看马的小官，一气之下回到花果山，自称“齐天大圣”。玉帝派托塔李天王和他的三太子哪吒，带领天兵前去捉拿。

    Discovering that his title merely made him a keeper of horses, Sun Wukong storms back to his mountain and calls himself Great Sage Equal to Heaven. The Jade Emperor sends Heavenly King Li, bearer of the pagoda, and his third son Nezha with the heavenly troops to seize him.

    Journey to the West, chapter 4 · ch. 4 · 《西游记》第四回。
  2. 02

    三头六臂Three heads, six arms

    哪吒大喝一声，变成三头六臂，六只手各拿一件兵器打来。悟空见了，也变成三头六臂，把金箍棒一晃变成三条，六只手拿着架住。

    Nezha gives a shout and becomes a figure with three heads and six arms, a weapon in every hand. Wukong does the same: he shakes his staff into three and parries with six hands.

    Journey to the West, chapter 4 · ch. 4 · 《西游记》第四回。
  3. 03

    毫毛分身A hair becomes a double

    两人斗得难分难解。悟空拔下一根毫毛，变成自己的模样继续迎敌，真身却绕到哪吒背后打了一棒。哪吒负痛败走，回去禀报父王：这猴子神通广大。

    Neither can win. Wukong plucks a hair and turns it into a double of himself to keep fighting, while his true body circles behind Nezha and strikes. Nezha flees in pain and reports to his father that the monkey's powers are great.

    Journey to the West, chapter 4 · ch. 4 · 《西游记》第四回。这是小说情节，属于后世文学层。
中国民间信仰 / Chinese Folk Religion · 证据卡Evidence card

雷公 Leigong

跨时期雷神人格入口；先秦诗歌、汉代批判记录、敦煌图像与后世信仰分层登记。

Diachronic thunder-deity entry with classical poetic, Han critical, Dunhuang visual and later layers kept separate.

乌加里特巴力 Baal of Ugarit

乌加里特 Baʿlu／Haddu：风暴、降雨、战士、土地与王权层按 KTU 泥板和图像证据登记。“Baal”亦为头衔，绝不自动合并所有同名神。

Ugaritic Baʿlu/Haddu in tablet and iconographic witnesses; Baal is also a title, so other Baal-named figures remain separate.

北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

尤弥尔 Ymir

在金伦加鸿沟中形成的原初巨人；被奥丁兄弟杀死，世界由他的身体造成（《欺骗古鲁菲》第5–8章）。

Primordial giant formed in Ginnungagap; killed by Odin and his brothers, who make the world from his body (Gylfaginning 5–8).

  1. 01

    冰与火之间Between ice and fire

    世界还不存在时，只有一道叫金伦加的深渊。北方的寒霜与南方的热气在这里相遇，融化的水滴里生出了巨人尤弥尔。

    Before the world exists there is only the void of Ginnungagap. Where rime from the north meets heat from the south, the thawing drops bring forth the giant Ymir.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 5–8 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第5章。
  2. 02

    奥丁兄弟杀死尤弥尔Odin and his brothers kill Ymir

    布尔的儿子们——奥丁、维利和维——杀死了巨人尤弥尔。

    The sons of Borr — Odin, Vili and Vé — kill the giant Ymir.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 5–8 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第7章。
  3. 03

    用身体造世界A world made from a body

    他们用尤弥尔的身体造出世界：肉变成大地，血变成海洋和湖泊，骨头变成山岩，头骨被举起来成为天空。

    From Ymir's body they make the world: his flesh becomes the earth, his blood the sea and lakes, his bones the rocks, and his skull is raised up as the sky.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 5–8 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第8章。斯诺里的散文综述与诗歌见证可能有细节差异。
北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

尼奥尔德 Njörðr

《欺骗古鲁菲》第23章中的华纳神：住在诺欧通，掌管风与海，是弗雷与芙蕾雅之父。

Vanir god in Gylfaginning 23: lives at Nóatún, rules wind and sea, father of Freyr and Freyja.

  1. 01

    海风之神尼奥尔德Njörðr of wind and sea

    尼奥尔德住在天上一个叫诺欧通的地方。他掌管风的运行，能平息海浪和火焰；出海航行和捕鱼的人都向他祈祷。他在华纳海姆长大，后来作为人质被送到阿萨神族。

    Njörðr lives in heaven at a place called Nóatún. He rules the motion of the wind and can calm sea and fire; those who sail or fish pray to him. He was raised in Vanaheim and later given to the Æsir as a hostage.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 23, 24 and 37 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第23章。
  2. 02

    弗雷与芙蕾雅Freyr and Freyja

    尼奥尔德有一儿一女。儿子弗雷掌管雨水、阳光和大地的收成，人们向他祈求丰年与富足。女儿芙蕾雅住在弗尔克范格，每当她骑马出战，就分得一半战死者，另一半归奥丁；她乘坐的车由两只猫拉着。

    Njörðr has a son and a daughter. Freyr rules rain, sunshine and the produce of the earth, and people pray to him for good seasons and wealth. Freyja lives at Fólkvangr; whenever she rides to battle she takes half the slain, Odin the other half, and her chariot is drawn by two cats.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 23, 24 and 37 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第24章。
  3. 03

    弗雷交出宝剑Freyr gives away his sword

    后来，弗雷爱上了巨人之女葛德。为了娶到她，他把自己的宝剑交给了别人作为代价——这把剑从此不在他手中。

    Later Freyr falls in love with Gerðr, a giant's daughter. To win her he gives away his sword as the price, and the sword is no longer his.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 23, 24 and 37 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第37章。葛德尚未单独建立实体；诸神黄昏中的后果另行登记。
北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

巴德尔 Baldr

  1. 01

    知晓命运的女神The goddess who knows all fates

    弗丽嘉是奥丁的妻子。斯诺里说，她知道一切人的命运，只是从不说出来。

    Frigg is Odin's wife. Snorri says she knows the fates of all, though she does not speak them aloud.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 20 and 49 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第20章。
  2. 02

    万物发誓Everything swears

    巴德尔做了不祥的梦。弗丽嘉便去找火、水、铁和各种金属、石头、土地、树木、疾病、野兽、鸟和蛇，让它们都发誓不伤害巴德尔。只有长在英灵殿西边的一株小槲寄生，她觉得太小，没有让它发誓。

    Baldr has ominous dreams, so Frigg makes fire, water, iron and all metals, stones, earth, trees, sicknesses, beasts, birds and serpents swear not to harm him. Only a young mistletoe shoot she thinks too small to ask.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 20 and 49 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第49章。
  3. 03

    槲寄生The mistletoe

    众神以为巴德尔刀枪不入，便拿东西扔他取乐。然而那株没有发誓的槲寄生被做成了投枪，巴德尔被它刺中而死。

    Believing Baldr unharmable, the gods amuse themselves by throwing things at him. But the mistletoe that never swore is made into a dart, and Baldr is struck down and dies.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 20 and 49 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第49章。投掷者与幕后之人的情节将在后续批次逐段登记。

    博尔是布里的孩子。

    Gylfaginning presents Borr as Búri’s son.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 6

弗丽嘉 Frigg

奥丁之妻，知晓一切命运；她让万物发誓不伤害巴德尔，唯独漏掉了槲寄生（《欺骗古鲁菲》第20、49章）。

Odin's wife who knows all fates; she takes oaths from all things to protect Baldr, except the mistletoe (Gylfaginning 20, 49).

  1. 01

    知晓命运的女神The goddess who knows all fates

    弗丽嘉是奥丁的妻子。斯诺里说，她知道一切人的命运，只是从不说出来。

    Baldr has ominous dreams, so Frigg makes fire, water, iron and all metals, stones, earth, trees, sicknesses, beasts, birds and serpents swear not to harm him. Only a young mistletoe shoot she thinks too small to ask.

    槲寄生The mistletoe

    众神以为巴德尔刀枪不入，便拿东西扔他取乐。然而那株没有发誓的槲寄生被做成了投枪，巴德尔被它刺中而死。

弗雷 Freyr

尼奥尔德之子，掌管雨水、阳光与丰收；为娶葛德而交出宝剑（《欺骗古鲁菲》第24、37章）。

Son of Njörðr who rules rain, sunshine and the fruitfulness of the earth; gives away his sword to win Gerðr (Gylfaginning 24, 37).

  1. 01

    海风之神尼奥尔德Njörðr of wind and sea

提尔 Týr

众神中最勇敢的一位；束缚芬里尔时失去一只手（《欺骗古鲁菲》第25、34章）。

The boldest of the gods, who loses his hand when the wolf Fenrir is bound (Gylfaginning 25, 34).

  1. 01

    最勇敢的神The boldest god

    斯诺里说，提尔是众神中最勇敢、最无畏的一位，打仗时人们向他祈求胜利。他只有一只手。

    Snorri says Týr is the boldest and most valiant of the gods, invoked for victory in battle — and he has only one hand.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 25 and 34 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第25章。
  2. 02

    一只手作担保A hand as a pledge

    众神想用一条魔法绳索捆住狼芬里尔。狼起了疑心，要求有人把手放进它嘴里，保证这不是诡计。众神面面相觑，只有提尔伸出了右手。

    The gods mean to bind the wolf Fenrir with a magic fetter. Suspicious, the wolf demands that someone put a hand in its mouth as a pledge of good faith. Only Týr holds out his right hand.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 25 and 34 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第34章。
  3. 03

    狼咬断了手The wolf bites

    绳索越挣越紧，狼发现自己被骗了，便咬断了提尔的手。众神都笑了，只有提尔没有笑。

    The more the wolf struggles, the tighter the fetter grows. Realizing it has been tricked, Fenrir bites off Týr's hand — and all the gods laugh, except Týr.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 25 and 34 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第34章。
北欧 / Norse · 证据卡Evidence card

梅利 Meili

    索尔／雷神托尔与梅利是兄弟姐妹。

    Old Norse poetic and Snorrian witness terminology calls Thor the brother of Meili.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Skáldskaparmál · Thor kenning: brother of Meili
  2. 02

洛基 Loki

古诺斯文本中的洛基；与索尔在多则叙事中互动，但兄弟关系只登记在现代改编层。古诺斯赫尔为洛基与安格尔波达之女。

Loki in Old Norse witnesses, interacting with Thor without an ancient sibling claim; Hel is Loki and Angrboda’s daughter in Snorri’s witness.

  1. 01

    索尔寻找锤子Thor seeks the hammer

    《索列姆之歌》把索尔置于寻找妙尔尼尔的中心位置。

    Thrymskvida places Thor at the center of the search for Mjolnir.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Þrymskviða stanzas 1–32 · 《索列姆之歌》1–32节。当前版不扩写尚未逐节入库的情节。
  2. 02

    洛基同行Loki accompanies him

    洛基在诗中同行并提供建议；这项同伴关系不表示索尔与洛基是兄弟。

    Loki accompanies and advises him in the poem; this companionship does not make Thor and Loki brothers.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Þrymskviða stanzas 1–32 · 《索列姆之歌》1–32节与关系纠错Claim。保持亲属关系与故事同伴关系分离。
  3. 03

    寻回框架Recovery frame

    当前证据层确认两者共同处于妙尔尼尔寻回故事，但婚礼伪装与逐节行动尚未在本版本拆成独立Claims。

    The current evidence layer confirms both figures in the recovery story, while the wedding disguise and stanza-by-stanza actions have not yet been modeled as separate claims.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Þrymskviða stanzas 1–32 · 诗歌整体定位1–32节。详情将在后续逐节版本扩充。
北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

洛杜尔 Lóðurr

《女巫的预言》人类起源三神见证中的独立神祇；与 Loki 或 Vé 的同一说仅作待审研究问题。

Distinct deity in Völuspá; proposed identities with Loki or Vé remain unasserted research questions.

  1. 01

    阿斯克与恩布拉Ask and Embla

    《女巫预言》17–18节在最初人类段落中分别命名阿斯克与恩布拉。

    Voluspa 17–18 names Ask and Embla in its first-human episode.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Völuspá stanzas 17–18 · 《女巫预言》17–18节。当前中文概述不复制现代译文。
  2. 02

    诗歌中的三位神The poetic triad

    这段诗歌把奥丁、赫尼尔与洛杜尔放在同一创造人类场景中。

    The poetic passage places Odin, Hoenir, and Lodurr together in the human-creation scene.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Völuspá stanzas 17–18 · 《女巫预言》17–18节。各神具体赐予项目将在逐词校勘后补充。
  3. 03

    与散文版不同Different from the prose account

    这里记录的是诗歌三神组合；《欺骗古鲁菲》的奥丁、维利、维组合不会覆盖它。

    This is the poetic triad; the Odin, Vili, and Ve grouping in Gylfaginning does not overwrite it.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Völuspá stanzas 17–18 · 两个见证的版本对照。差异保持为版本并存，而非人物自动等同。
北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

海姆达尔 Heimdallr

住在彩虹桥边希敏约格的众神守望者，掌管加拉尔号角（《欺骗古鲁菲》第27章）。

Watchman of the gods at Himinbjörg by Bifröst, keeper of Gjallarhorn (Gylfaginning 27).

  1. 01

    桥头的守望者Watchman at the bridge

    海姆达尔是众神的守望者。他住在彩虹桥边一个叫希敏约格的地方，在天的尽头守着桥，防止山巨人闯进来。

    Heimdallr is the watchman of the gods. He lives at Himinbjörg by Bifröst, at the edge of heaven, guarding the bridge against the mountain-giants.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 27 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第27章。
  2. 02

    看得远，听得清Keen eyes and ears

    他需要的睡眠比鸟还少，无论白天黑夜都能看见一百里远；他还能听见草在地上生长、羊毛在羊身上生长的声音。

    He needs less sleep than a bird and sees a hundred leagues by day or night; he can hear grass growing in the ground and wool growing on sheep.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 27 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第27章。
  3. 03

    加拉尔号角与九位母亲Gjallarhorn and nine mothers

    海姆达尔有一支号角，叫加拉尔号角，吹响时所有世界都能听见。斯诺里还说，他是九位母亲的儿子，而这九位母亲是姐妹。

    Heimdallr has a horn called Gjallarhorn, whose blast is heard in all worlds. Snorri also says he is the son of nine mothers, who were sisters.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 27 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第27章。九位母亲的名字见其他诗篇，本版不列出。

    The current evidence layer confirms both figures in the recovery story, while the wedding disguise and stanza-by-stanza actions have not yet been modeled as separate claims.

约尔德／大地女神 Jord

《散文埃达》所见索尔之母；大地人格层与具体文本见证分开保存。

Mother of Thor in the registered Prose Edda witness; earth-personification scope remains witness-specific.

    索尔／雷神托尔是约尔德／大地女神的孩子。

北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

维 Vé

Brother of Odin and Vili; participates in cosmogonic acts in Gylfaginning.

  1. 01

    散文中的最初人类First humans in prose

    《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章同样命名阿斯克与恩布拉，构成散文版的人类起源见证。

    Gylfaginning 8–9 also names Ask and Embla, forming the prose witness to human origins.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Prologue and Gylfaginning · Gylfaginning 8–9 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章。不把散文版当作诗歌版的简单翻译。
  2. 02

    奥丁、维利与维Odin, Vili, and Ve

    散文序列把奥丁、维利与维列为博尔之子，并放在这一创造框架中。

    The prose sequence identifies Odin, Vili, and Ve as sons of Borr and places them in this creation frame.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Prologue and Gylfaginning · Gylfaginning 8–9 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章。人物组合与《女巫预言》不同。
  3. 03

    不强制对应No forced equivalence

    系统不把维利／维与赫尼尔／洛杜尔按功能相似强制合并；当前只呈现两个文本明确给出的名字。

    The system does not force Vili and Ve to equal Hoenir and Lodurr by perceived functional similarity; it presents only the names explicit in each text.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Prologue and Gylfaginning · Gylfaginning 8–9 · 两个独立来源见证。身份对应留作研究问题。
北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

维利 Vili

Brother of Odin and Vé; participates in cosmogonic acts in Gylfaginning.

  1. 01

    散文中的最初人类First humans in prose

    《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章同样命名阿斯克与恩布拉，构成散文版的人类起源见证。

    Gylfaginning 8–9 also names Ask and Embla, forming the prose witness to human origins.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Prologue and Gylfaginning · Gylfaginning 8–9 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章。不把散文版当作诗歌版的简单翻译。
  2. 02

    奥丁、维利与维Odin, Vili, and Ve

    散文序列把奥丁、维利与维列为博尔之子，并放在这一创造框架中。

    The prose sequence identifies Odin, Vili, and Ve as sons of Borr and places them in this creation frame.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Prologue and Gylfaginning · Gylfaginning 8–9 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》8–9章。人物组合与《女巫预言》不同。
  3. 03

    不强制对应No forced equivalence

    系统不把维利／维与赫尼尔／洛杜尔按功能相似强制合并；当前只呈现两个文本明确给出的名字。

    The system does not force Vili and Ve to equal Hoenir and Lodurr by perceived functional similarity; it presents only the names explicit in each text.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Prologue and Gylfaginning · Gylfaginning 8–9 · 两个独立来源见证。身份对应留作研究问题。
北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

芙蕾雅 Freyja

尼奥尔德之女；住在弗尔克范格，分得一半战死者，乘两只猫拉的车（《欺骗古鲁菲》第24章）。

Daughter of Njörðr; lives at Fólkvangr, takes half the slain and drives a chariot drawn by two cats (Gylfaginning 24).

  1. 01

    海风之神尼奥尔德Njörðr of wind and sea

    尼奥尔德住在天上一个叫诺欧通的地方。他掌管风的运行，能平息海浪和火焰；出海航行和捕鱼的人都向他祈祷。他在华纳海姆长大，后来作为人质被送到阿萨神族。

    Njörðr lives in heaven at a place called Nóatún. He rules the motion of the wind and can calm sea and fire; those who sail or fish pray to him. He was raised in Vanaheim and later given to the Æsir as a hostage.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 23, 24 and 37 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第23章。
  2. 02

    弗雷与芙蕾雅Freyr and Freyja

    尼奥尔德有一儿一女。儿子弗雷掌管雨水、阳光和大地的收成，人们向他祈求丰年与富足。女儿芙蕾雅住在弗尔克范格，每当她骑马出战，就分得一半战死者，另一半归奥丁；她乘坐的车由两只猫拉着。

    Njörðr has a son and a daughter. Freyr rules rain, sunshine and the produce of the earth, and people pray to him for good seasons and wealth. Freyja lives at Fólkvangr; whenever she rides to battle she takes half the slain, Odin the other half, and her chariot is drawn by two cats.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 23, 24 and 37 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第24章。
  3. 03

    弗雷交出宝剑Freyr gives away his sword

    后来，弗雷爱上了巨人之女葛德。为了娶到她，他把自己的宝剑交给了别人作为代价——这把剑从此不在他手中。

    Later Freyr falls in love with Gerðr, a giant's daughter. To win her he gives away his sword as the price, and the sword is no longer his.

    Snorri Sturluson, Edda · Gylfaginning 23, 24 and 37 · 《欺骗古鲁菲》第37章。葛德尚未单独建立实体；诸神黄昏中的后果另行登记。
北欧 / Norse · 证据卡Evidence card

莫迪 Modi

Son of Thor; the registered genealogy witness does not name his mother.

  1. 01

    Skaldskaparmal names Modi as a child of Thor.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Skáldskaparmál · Thor kennings section

赫尔 Hel

    Gylfaginning names Hel as a child of Loki and Angrboda.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Prologue and Gylfaginning · chapter 34
北欧 / Norse · 有完整故事Full story

赫尼尔 Hœnir

《女巫的预言》人类起源三神见证中的独立神祇；不与 Vili 自动等同。

A distinct deity in the Völuspá human-origin triad; not automatically identified with Vili.

  1. 01

    阿斯克与恩布拉Ask and Embla

    《女巫预言》17–18节在最初人类段落中分别命名阿斯克与恩布拉。

    Voluspa 17–18 names Ask and Embla in its first-human episode.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Völuspá stanzas 17–18 · 《女巫预言》17–18节。当前中文概述不复制现代译文。
  2. 02

    诗歌中的三位神The poetic triad

    这段诗歌把奥丁、赫尼尔与洛杜尔放在同一创造人类场景中。

    The poetic passage places Odin, Hoenir, and Lodurr together in the human-creation scene.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Völuspá stanzas 17–18 · 《女巫预言》17–18节。各神具体赐予项目将在逐词校勘后补充。
  3. 03

    与散文版不同Different from the prose account

    这里记录的是诗歌三神组合；《欺骗古鲁菲》的奥丁、维利、维组合不会覆盖它。

    This is the poetic triad; the Odin, Vili, and Ve grouping in Gylfaginning does not overwrite it.

    Codex Regius electronic edition · Völuspá stanzas 17–18 · 两个见证的版本对照。差异保持为版本并存，而非人物自动等同。
北欧 / Norse · 证据卡Evidence card

马格尼 Magni

索尔之子；《诗语法》另称其母为雅恩莎撒。

Son of Thor; Skaldskaparmal also names Jarnsaxa as his mother.

  1. 01

    Skaldskaparmal identifies Jarnsaxa as Magni’s mother.

    Snorri Sturluson: Edda. Skáldskaparmál · Hrungnir episode

印度教传统 / Hindu · 有完整故事Full story

俱利摩（龟形化身） Kūrma

Tortoise form assumed by Viṣṇu to support Mount Mandara in the Bhāgavata Purāṇa witness; kept separate from the Mahābhārata tortoise king Akūpāra.

  1. 01

    诸神与阿修罗暂时结盟A truce between gods and asuras

    《薄伽梵往世书》第八篇第5–6章中，诸神按毗湿奴的建议与阿修罗暂时结盟，合力拔起曼陀罗山并运往大海，毗湿奴亲自出手相助。

    In Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.5–8.6, on Viṣṇu's advice the gods make a temporary truce with the asuras; together they uproot Mount Mandara and carry it toward the ocean, with Viṣṇu himself stepping in to help.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.5–8.6。运山过程的细节只作概述，待逐颂校对。
  2. 02

    毗湿奴化身巨龟Viṣṇu takes the form of a tortoise

    以婆苏吉为绳开始搅拌后，曼陀罗山沉入水中；毗湿奴化作巨龟潜到山下，用背托起大山。

    Once the churning begins with Vāsuki as the rope, Mount Mandara sinks into the water; Viṣṇu assumes the form of a giant tortoise, dives beneath it and raises the mountain on his back.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.7。与精校本的龟王阿库帕拉构成显式见证差异，不做身份等同。
  3. 03

    湿婆饮下毒药Śiva drinks the poison

    搅拌最先产生可怕的毒药，向各方蔓延；众生求助于湿婆，湿婆把毒药饮下。

    The churning first produces a terrible poison that spreads in every direction; beings turn to Śiva, who drinks it.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.7。后续图像传统与虔信解释未在本批次逐句建模。
  4. 04

    檀文陀利与甘露Dhanvantari and the amṛta

    其后又涌现多种宝物与存在，最后檀文陀利捧着盛满甘露的水罐出现。

    A series of treasures and beings then emerges, and finally Dhanvantari appears holding a vessel filled with the amṛta.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.8。此前出现之物的逐项清单尚未建模，不与精校本合并。
  5. 05

    摩希尼与罗睺Mohinī and Rāhu

    阿修罗抢走甘露后，毗湿奴化身为摩希尼迷惑他们，并把甘露分给诸神。罗睺假扮天神混入其中饮下甘露，被日月指认后，毗湿奴以神盘斩下其头。

    When the asuras snatch the amṛta, Viṣṇu becomes Mohinī, captivates them and distributes the amṛta to the gods. Rāhu, disguised as a god, slips in and drinks; when the Sun and Moon point him out, Viṣṇu severs his head with the discus.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.8–8.9。罗睺后来的天体身份不在本批次建模。
印度教传统 / Hindu · 有完整故事Full story

摩希尼 Mohinī

Enchanting female form of Viṣṇu who distributes the amṛta in the Bhāgavata Purāṇa witness; the Mahābhārata witness is linked only through its own description of a deluding female form.

  1. 01

    诸神与阿修罗暂时结盟A truce between gods and asuras

    《薄伽梵往世书》第八篇第5–6章中，诸神按毗湿奴的建议与阿修罗暂时结盟，合力拔起曼陀罗山并运往大海，毗湿奴亲自出手相助。

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.5–8.6。运山过程的细节只作概述，待逐颂校对。
  2. 02

  5. 05

    摩希尼与罗睺Mohinī and Rāhu

    阿修罗抢走甘露后，毗湿奴化身为摩希尼迷惑他们，并把甘露分给诸神。罗睺假扮天神混入其中饮下甘露，被日月指认后，毗湿奴以神盘斩下其头。

印度教传统 / Hindu · 证据卡Evidence card

梵天 Brahma

《薄伽梵往世书》8.5 中，梵天带领受困的众神去见毗湿奴，随后才有乳海搅拌。

In Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.5 Brahmā leads the distressed gods to Viṣṇu before the churning of the ocean.

  1. 01

    梵天出现在“乳海搅拌（文本叙事）”之中。

    In Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.5 the distressed gods go to Brahmā, who leads them to the Lord Ajita (Viṣṇu) and praises him; the churning follows in 8.6–8.9.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · BhP 8.5
印度教传统 / Hindu · 有完整故事Full story

檀文陀利 Dhanvantari

Divine figure who emerges from the churned ocean carrying the amṛta in both registered textual witnesses.

  1. 01

    诸神求取甘露The gods seek the amṛta

    精校本《初篇》1.15 中，诸神聚集在须弥山上商议如何得到甘露；那罗延（毗湿奴）建议诸神与阿修罗一起搅动大海。

    In the critical edition at Ādiparvan 1.15, the gods gather on Mount Meru to deliberate how to obtain the amṛta; Nārāyaṇa (Viṣṇu) advises that gods and asuras churn the ocean together.

    Mahābhārata, Ādiparvan (critical-edition e-text) · Mahābhārata CE, Ādiparvan 1.15–1.17 (Āstīkaparvan) · 精校本初篇 1.15。“那罗延”在此按既有毗湿奴实体连接，不扩展神学解释。
  2. 02

    曼陀罗山、婆苏吉与龟王Mandara, Vāsuki and the tortoise king

    巨蛇阿难陀拔起曼陀罗山作为搅棒，龙王婆苏吉充当搅绳；山的底部由龟王阿库帕拉承托。这一见证没有说龟王是毗湿奴的化身。

    The serpent Ananta uproots Mount Mandara to serve as the churning rod and the nāga Vāsuki serves as the rope; the tortoise king Akūpāra bears the base of the mountain. This witness does not say that the tortoise king is a form of Viṣṇu.

    Mahābhārata, Ādiparvan (critical-edition e-text) · Mahābhārata CE, Ādiparvan 1.15–1.17 (Āstīkaparvan) · 精校本初篇 1.16。阿难陀拔山一项为中等置信度，待逐颂校对；阿难陀尚未单独建实体。
  3. 03

    檀文陀利捧着甘露出现Dhanvantari appears with the amṛta

    搅拌中先后涌现多种存在与宝物，最后檀文陀利捧着盛满甘露的水罐出现。

    As the churning goes on, a series of beings and treasures emerges, and finally Dhanvantari appears holding a vessel filled with the amṛta.

    Mahābhārata, Ādiparvan (critical-edition e-text) · Mahābhārata CE, Ādiparvan 1.15–1.17 (Āstīkaparvan) · 精校本初篇 1.16。此前出现之物的逐项清单尚未建模，不从其他版本补齐。
  4. 04

    迷惑的女身与罗睺The deluding female form and Rāhu

    阿修罗夺走甘露后，那罗延化作迷惑人的女子形象，把甘露取回交给诸神。罗睺假扮天神偷饮甘露，被日月揭发后，那罗延以神盘斩下其头；随后天神与阿修罗爆发大战。

    After the Dānavas seize the amṛta, Nārāyaṇa takes on a deluding female form and recovers it for the gods. Rāhu, disguised as a god, drinks some of it; when the Sun and Moon expose him, Nārāyaṇa cuts off his head with the discus, and a great battle between gods and Dānavas follows.

    Mahābhārata, Ādiparvan (critical-edition e-text) · Mahābhārata CE, Ādiparvan 1.15–1.17 (Āstīkaparvan) · 精校本初篇 1.16–1.17。专名实体“摩希尼”只连接《薄伽梵往世书》见证；此处保留“迷惑的女身”描述。
  5. 05

    精校本正文的范围Scope of the constituted critical text

    在精校本 1.15–1.17 的正文里，没有湿婆吞下毒药的情节。这只说明本定位“未陈述”，不代表所有写本或地方传统都没有；校勘记录与附录尚待逐项核对。

    The constituted critical text at 1.15–1.17 does not include Śiva swallowing the poison. This records only that the locator does not state it; it does not mean that every manuscript or regional tradition lacks it, and the apparatus and appendices still have to be checked.

    Mahābhārata, Ādiparvan (critical-edition e-text) · Mahābhārata CE, Ādiparvan 1.15–1.17 (Āstīkaparvan) · 精校本初篇 1.15–1.17 正文范围与 BORI 精校本说明。写本校勘与附录已作为独立研究队列排队。
印度教传统 / Hindu · 有完整故事Full story

毗湿奴 Vishnu

  1. 01

    诸神求取甘露The gods seek the amṛta

    精校本《初篇》1.15 中，诸神聚集在须弥山上商议如何得到甘露；那罗延（毗湿奴）建议诸神与阿修罗一起搅动大海。

  3. 03

    檀文陀利捧着甘露出现Dhanvantari appears with the amṛta

    搅拌中先后涌现多种存在与宝物，最后檀文陀利捧着盛满甘露的水罐出现。

    As the churning goes on, a series of beings and treasures emerges, and finally Dhanvantari appears holding a vessel filled with the amṛta.

    Mahābhārata, Ādiparvan (critical-edition e-text) · Mahābhārata CE, Ādiparvan 1.15–1.17 (Āstīkaparvan) · 精校本初篇 1.16。此前出现之物的逐项清单尚未建模，不从其他版本补齐。
  4. 04

    迷惑的女身与罗睺The deluding female form and Rāhu

    阿修罗夺走甘露后，那罗延化作迷惑人的女子形象，把甘露取回交给诸神。罗睺假扮天神偷饮甘露，被日月揭发后，那罗延以神盘斩下其头；随后天神与阿修罗爆发大战。

    After the Dānavas seize the amṛta, Nārāyaṇa takes on a deluding female form and recovers it for the gods. Rāhu, disguised as a god, drinks some of it; when the Sun and Moon expose him, Nārāyaṇa cuts off his head with the discus, and a great battle between gods and Dānavas follows.

    Mahābhārata, Ādiparvan (critical-edition e-text) · Mahābhārata CE, Ādiparvan 1.15–1.17 (Āstīkaparvan) · 精校本初篇 1.16–1.17。专名实体“摩希尼”只连接《薄伽梵往世书》见证；此处保留“迷惑的女身”描述。
  5. 05

    精校本正文的范围Scope of the constituted critical text

    在精校本 1.15–1.17 的正文里，没有湿婆吞下毒药的情节。这只说明本定位“未陈述”，不代表所有写本或地方传统都没有；校勘记录与附录尚待逐项核对。

    The constituted critical text at 1.15–1.17 does not include Śiva swallowing the poison. This records only that the locator does not state it; it does not mean that every manuscript or regional tradition lacks it, and the apparatus and appendices still have to be checked.

    Mahābhārata, Ādiparvan (critical-edition e-text) · Mahābhārata CE, Ādiparvan 1.15–1.17 (Āstīkaparvan) · 精校本初篇 1.15–1.17 正文范围与 BORI 精校本说明。写本校勘与附录已作为独立研究队列排队。
印度教传统 / Hindu · 有完整故事Full story

湿婆 Shiva

  1. 01

    诸神与阿修罗暂时结盟A truce between gods and asuras

    《薄伽梵往世书》第八篇第5–6章中，诸神按毗湿奴的建议与阿修罗暂时结盟，合力拔起曼陀罗山并运往大海，毗湿奴亲自出手相助。

    In Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.5–8.6, on Viṣṇu's advice the gods make a temporary truce with the asuras; together they uproot Mount Mandara and carry it toward the ocean, with Viṣṇu himself stepping in to help.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.5–8.6。运山过程的细节只作概述，待逐颂校对。
  2. 02

    毗湿奴化身巨龟Viṣṇu takes the form of a tortoise

    以婆苏吉为绳开始搅拌后，曼陀罗山沉入水中；毗湿奴化作巨龟潜到山下，用背托起大山。

    Once the churning begins with Vāsuki as the rope, Mount Mandara sinks into the water; Viṣṇu assumes the form of a giant tortoise, dives beneath it and raises the mountain on his back.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.7。与精校本的龟王阿库帕拉构成显式见证差异，不做身份等同。
  3. 03

    湿婆饮下毒药Śiva drinks the poison

    搅拌最先产生可怕的毒药，向各方蔓延；众生求助于湿婆，湿婆把毒药饮下。

    The churning first produces a terrible poison that spreads in every direction; beings turn to Śiva, who drinks it.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.7。后续图像传统与虔信解释未在本批次逐句建模。
  4. 04

    檀文陀利与甘露Dhanvantari and the amṛta

    其后又涌现多种宝物与存在，最后檀文陀利捧着盛满甘露的水罐出现。

    A series of treasures and beings then emerges, and finally Dhanvantari appears holding a vessel filled with the amṛta.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.8。此前出现之物的逐项清单尚未建模，不与精校本合并。
  5. 05

    摩希尼与罗睺Mohinī and Rāhu

    阿修罗抢走甘露后，毗湿奴化身为摩希尼迷惑他们，并把甘露分给诸神。罗睺假扮天神混入其中饮下甘露，被日月指认后，毗湿奴以神盘斩下其头。

    When the asuras snatch the amṛta, Viṣṇu becomes Mohinī, captivates them and distributes the amṛta to the gods. Rāhu, disguised as a god, slips in and drinks; when the Sun and Moon point him out, Viṣṇu severs his head with the discus.

    Bhāgavatapurāṇa (GRETIL e-text) · Bhāgavatapurāṇa, Skandha 8, adhyāyas 5–9 · 《薄伽梵往世书》8.8–8.9。罗睺后来的天体身份不在本批次建模。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

伊西斯 Isis

在 UCL 对奥西里斯故事的概述中，伊西斯收集奥西里斯的肢体并怀上荷鲁斯；大都会博物馆的哀悼像把她与奈芙蒂斯配成一对。

In the UCL summary of the Osiris cycle Isis gathers Osiris's limbs and conceives Horus; Met mourning figures pair her with Nephthys.

  1. 01

    太阳神的第一对子女The creator's first pair

    在埃及的创世观念里，主要的创造者是太阳神，他也被称为阿图姆。他最先生出一男一女：舒和泰芙努特。学者认为“舒”这个名字和“干燥”有关，“泰芙努特”则代表湿润。

    In Egyptian creation thought the principal creator is the sun-god, also called Atum. His first offshoots are a son and a daughter, Shu and Tefnet; scholars link Shu's name to dryness and read Tefnet as moisture.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL Digital Egypt 创世页面。“干燥／湿润”是现代学者的词源解释。
  2. 02

    大地与天空出生Earth and sky are born

    舒和泰芙努特又生下两个孩子：大地之神盖布和天空女神努特。干燥与湿润，变成了坚实的大地和古埃及人眼中充满水的天空。

    Shu and Tefnet in turn produce two children: Geb, god of the earth, and Nut, goddess of the sky — dryness and moisture crystallised into solid earth and a sky the Egyptians thought of as watery.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · 同一页面的子女段落。
  3. 03

    舒把天地分开Shu holds earth and sky apart

    盖布和努特被舒分开，天在上、地在下。零散的古代记载暗示，这种分离必须被强制维持，否则天地还会继续生出神来。

    Shu separates Geb and Nut, sky above and earth below. Scattered references suggest the separation had to be enforced, or earth and sky would keep producing divine offspring.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL 页面关于天地分离的说明。这是学者对零散材料的归纳，不是单一文本的原话。
  4. 04

    塞特、奥西里斯与伊西斯Seth, Osiris and Isis

    后来，奥西里斯“无序的兄弟”塞特出于嫉妒杀害了他，把遗体分开，并取代他成为世界之王。伊西斯找回奥西里斯的肢体，让遗体恢复到足以受孕，生下了荷鲁斯。

    Later Osiris's anarchic brother Seth, out of jealousy, murders him, dismembers him and takes his place as king of the world. Isis collects the limbs and revives the body enough to conceive a child, Horus.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL 页面的奥西里斯故事概述。古代文献中这个故事有许多版本；本站不合成“标准版”。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 证据卡Evidence card

凯布利／赫普里 Khepri

与晨日、生成和圣甲虫意象相关的太阳神形态；具体认同关系按文本登记。

Solar deity/form associated with becoming, the rising sun and scarab imagery; identifications remain witness-specific.

  1. 01

    凯布利／赫普里被认同为拉-哈拉赫提。

    One Spell 17 explanatory gloss identifies Khepri in the boat as Ra-Horakhty himself.

    Book of the Dead chapter 17 · Spell 17, explanatory gloss
  2. 02

    凯布利／赫普里见于凯布利赞歌石碑 EA826。

    British Museum stela EA826 contains a twenty-one-line hymn to Khepri according to the catalogue.

    Stela with hymn to the sun god Khepri · BM EA826
  3. 03

    凯布利／赫普里与太阳船相关。

    Spell 17 places Khepri amid the divine boat in the registered explanatory passage.

    Book of the Dead chapter 17 · Spell 17, Allen Part a
  4. 04

    凯布利／赫普里出现在《亡灵书》第17咒文中。

    Khepri appears in the registered Book of the Dead Spell 17 section amid solar-barque explanations.

    Book of the Dead chapter 17 · Spell 17, Allen Part a
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

卜塔-索卡尔-奥西里斯 Ptah-Sokar-Osiris

由卜塔、索卡尔与奥西里斯构成的丧葬复合神档案；三个组成神继续独立存在。

Funerary composite-deity dossier connecting Ptah, Sokar and Osiris without merging their component records.

古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

哈托尔 Hathor

在《天牛之书》中，拉的眼睛喝下红色啤酒后回归的慈爱、母性的形象。

In the Tale of the Heavenly Cow, the loving, maternal character to which the raging Eye of Ra returns after drinking red beer.

  1. 01

    人类反叛拉Humankind rebels against Ra

    一部新王国时期的宗教文献记载：人们起来反叛太阳神拉。拉决定惩罚他们，后来他也离开人间，退到天上。

    A New Kingdom religious composition records that people rebel against the sun-god Ra. He resolves to punish them, and in the end he withdraws to the sky.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation — the Tale of the Heavenly Cow · UCL 页面对《天牛之书》的概述。
  2. 02

    眼睛化作塞赫麦特The Eye becomes Sekhmet

    拉把自己的眼睛从身体里派出去，成为一个独立的“女儿”。她化作塞赫麦特——意思是“强大的女神”——几乎把人类全部消灭。

    Ra sends his Eye out from his body as a separate offshoot, a 'daughter'. As Sekhmet, 'the mighty goddess', she almost annihilates humankind.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation — the Tale of the Heavenly Cow · 同一概述的“拉的眼睛”段落。“强大的女神”是 UCL 给出的释义。
  3. 03

    一湖红色的啤酒A lake of red beer

    众神用计：他们把一湖啤酒染成红色，看起来像人血。她喝下后醉得香甜，脾气也软化下来，重新变回慈爱、温柔的哈托尔。

    The gods play a trick: they colour a lake of beer red to look like human blood. She drinks, becomes sweetly drunk, and reverts to her loving, maternal character as Hathor.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation — the Tale of the Heavenly Cow · 同一概述的结尾。这是故事中的性格转变；数据库不把两位女神合并为同一实体。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

塞赫麦特 Sekhmet

意为“强大的女神”：《天牛之书》中拉的眼睛化成的毁灭形态，几乎灭绝人类。

'The mighty goddess': the destructive form of Ra's Eye that almost annihilates humankind in the Tale of the Heavenly Cow.

  1. 01

    人类反叛拉Humankind rebels against Ra

    一部新王国时期的宗教文献记载：人们起来反叛太阳神拉。拉决定惩罚他们，后来他也离开人间，退到天上。

  3. 03

    一湖红色的啤酒A lake of red beer

    众神用计：他们把一湖啤酒染成红色，看起来像人血。她喝下后醉得香甜，脾气也软化下来，重新变回慈爱、温柔的哈托尔。

    The gods play a trick: they colour a lake of beer red to look like human blood. She drinks, becomes sweetly drunk, and reverts to her loving, maternal character as Hathor.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation — the Tale of the Heavenly Cow · 同一概述的结尾。这是故事中的性格转变；数据库不把两位女神合并为同一实体。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

奈芙蒂斯 Nephthys

大都会博物馆托勒密时期木像中的哀悼女神，与伊西斯一起出现在奥西里斯遗体两侧。

Mourning goddess shown kneeling in a Ptolemaic wooden figure at the Met, paired with Isis around the body of Osiris.

  1. 01

    举手掩面的奈芙蒂斯Nephthys raises her hand

    这尊小木像跪在地上，一只手举到脸前，是古埃及的哀悼姿势。她头上的象形文字符号，表明她就是女神奈芙蒂斯。

    The small wooden figure kneels and raises one hand before her face, an Egyptian gesture of mourning. The hieroglyph on her head identifies her as the goddess Nephthys.

    Mourning Nephthys · 12.182.23a — catalogue description · 大都会艺术博物馆 12.182.23a。
  2. 02

    和伊西斯成对A pair with Isis

    博物馆说明，这尊像和另一尊伊西斯像（12.182.23b）是一对，两位女神以同样的姿势哀悼。

    The museum states that the figure forms a pair with a figure of Isis (12.182.23b), the two goddesses mourning in the same posture.

    Mourning Nephthys · 12.182.23a — catalogue description · 两件藏品的说明互相引用。
  3. 03

    守在奥西里斯身旁Beside the body of Osiris

    在古埃及图像中，这两位哀悼女神陪伴在奥西里斯的遗体旁，一左一右。博物馆推测，这对木像原本可能放在死者棺椁的两端。

    In Egyptian representations the two mourning goddesses accompany the body of Osiris, one at each end. The museum suggests these figures were presumably placed at either end of the deceased's sarcophagus.

    Mourning Nephthys · 12.182.23a — catalogue description · 大都会艺术博物馆藏品说明。“放在棺椁两端”是博物馆的推测，不是出土记录。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

奥西里斯 Osiris

  1. 01

    太阳神的第一对子女The creator's first pair

    在埃及的创世观念里，主要的创造者是太阳神，他也被称为阿图姆。他最先生出一男一女：舒和泰芙努特。学者认为“舒”这个名字和“干燥”有关，“泰芙努特”则代表湿润。

    In Egyptian creation thought the principal creator is the sun-god, also called Atum. His first offshoots are a son and a daughter, Shu and Tefnet; scholars link Shu's name to dryness and read Tefnet as moisture.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL Digital Egypt 创世页面。“干燥／湿润”是现代学者的词源解释。
  2. 02

    大地与天空出生Earth and sky are born

    舒和泰芙努特又生下两个孩子：大地之神盖布和天空女神努特。干燥与湿润，变成了坚实的大地和古埃及人眼中充满水的天空。

    Shu and Tefnet in turn produce two children: Geb, god of the earth, and Nut, goddess of the sky — dryness and moisture crystallised into solid earth and a sky the Egyptians thought of as watery.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · 同一页面的子女段落。
  3. 03

    舒把天地分开Shu holds earth and sky apart

    盖布和努特被舒分开，天在上、地在下。零散的古代记载暗示，这种分离必须被强制维持，否则天地还会继续生出神来。

    Shu separates Geb and Nut, sky above and earth below. Scattered references suggest the separation had to be enforced, or earth and sky would keep producing divine offspring.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL 页面关于天地分离的说明。这是学者对零散材料的归纳，不是单一文本的原话。
  4. 04

    塞特、奥西里斯与伊西斯Seth, Osiris and Isis

    后来，奥西里斯“无序的兄弟”塞特出于嫉妒杀害了他，把遗体分开，并取代他成为世界之王。伊西斯找回奥西里斯的肢体，让遗体恢复到足以受孕，生下了荷鲁斯。

    Later Osiris's anarchic brother Seth, out of jealousy, murders him, dismembers him and takes his place as king of the world. Isis collects the limbs and revives the body enough to conceive a child, Horus.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL 页面的奥西里斯故事概述。古代文献中这个故事有许多版本；本站不合成“标准版”。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 证据卡Evidence card

孔苏 Khonsu

底比斯月神；本批仅建立与阿蒙、穆特和奥佩特节相关的来源基线。

Theban lunar deity with a first source-backed triad and Opet baseline.

  1. 01

    孔苏属于底比斯三神组。

    Khonsu is the child member of the Theban triad in the registered scholarly synthesis.

    Anthropomorphic Deities · UEE 2008 p.6
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

托特 Thoth

在阿尼的心脏称量场景中出现两次：天平上的狒狒，以及手持调色板的朱鹭头“众神书吏”。

Shown twice in the weighing of Ani's heart: as a baboon on the balance and as the ibis-headed scribe of the gods.

  1. 01

    心脏与羽毛A heart and a feather

    画面中央是一座天平。左边的秤盘里放着阿尼的心脏，右边是一根羽毛，代表真理与秩序女神玛阿特。

    At the centre stands a balance. In the left pan lies Ani's heart; in the right, a feather representing Maat, goddess of truth and order.

    Papyrus of Ani, frame 3 · EA10470,3 — museum description of the Hall of Judgment · 大英博物馆 EA10470,3 藏品描述。
  2. 02

    阿努比斯扶稳天平Anubis steadies the balance

    胡狼头的阿努比斯跪在天平旁，拉着右边秤盘的绳子，扶稳天平的铅锤。博物馆的说明称他是“在防腐之所的那一位”。

    Jackal-headed Anubis kneels by the balance, holding the cord of the right-hand pan and steadying its plumb bob. The museum describes him as 'he who is in the place of embalming'.

    Papyrus of Ani, frame 3 · EA10470,3 — museum description of the Hall of Judgment · 同一藏品描述中关于阿努比斯的部分。
  3. 03

    托特准备记录Thoth stands ready to record

    托特在画中出现两次：一只小狒狒坐在天平顶端；右边站着朱鹭头的“众神书吏”，手持调色板和芦苇笔，准备记下审问的结果。

    Thoth appears twice: as a small baboon sitting on top of the balance, and as the ibis-headed scribe of the gods standing to the right with palette and reed, ready to write down the result.

    Papyrus of Ani, frame 3 · EA10470,3 — museum description of the Hall of Judgment · 同一藏品描述中关于托特的部分。称量的最终结果和吞噬心脏的怪兽不在这条博物馆描述里，本站不补写。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 证据卡Evidence card

拉-哈拉赫提 Ra-Horakhty

拉与“双地平线的荷鲁斯”复合表达；不等同于所有荷鲁斯形态。

Composite solar-horizon deity; not a blanket alias for every Horus form.

  1. 01

    凯布利／赫普里被认同为拉-哈拉赫提。

    One Spell 17 explanatory gloss identifies Khepri in the boat as Ra-Horakhty himself.

    Book of the Dead chapter 17 · Spell 17, explanatory gloss
  2. 02

    阿图姆-哈拉赫提是包含拉-哈拉赫提的复合神名。

    The registered hymn address combines Atum with the Horakhty expression represented by the existing Ra-Horakhty dossier.

    Book of the Dead Chapter 15: sun hymns · UCL Book of the Dead 15, transliteration/translation lines 15-40
  3. 03

    拉-哈拉赫提是包含荷鲁斯的复合神名。

    Ra-Horakhty incorporates Horus specifically in the horizon-protector scope described by the registered catalogue.

    Stela with man offering to Re-Harakhty, unfinished · Met O.C.81
  4. 04

    拉-哈拉赫提是包含拉／瑞的复合神名。

    Ra-Horakhty is a syncretized expression involving Ra; the Ra record remains independent.

    Anthropomorphic Deities · UEE 2008 p.4; Met O.C.81
  5. 05

    太阳升起与落下形态与拉-哈拉赫提相关。

    The Met curatorial context presents Ra-Horakhty as a favored rising-sun focal deity connected with continued life.

    Stela of the God's-Father of Amun Pakeshi · Met object 90.6.30 catalogue context and object description
  6. 06

    拉-哈拉赫提釉陶护符 74.51.4497描绘了拉-哈拉赫提。

    The Met catalogues faience amulet 74.51.4497 as Ra Horakhty.

    Faience amulet of Ra Horakhty · Met 74.51.4497
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

拉／瑞 Ra

  1. 01

    人类反叛拉Humankind rebels against Ra

    一部新王国时期的宗教文献记载：人们起来反叛太阳神拉。拉决定惩罚他们，后来他也离开人间，退到天上。

    A New Kingdom religious composition records that people rebel against the sun-god Ra. He resolves to punish them, and in the end he withdraws to the sky.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation — the Tale of the Heavenly Cow · UCL 页面对《天牛之书》的概述。
  2. 02

    眼睛化作塞赫麦特The Eye becomes Sekhmet

    拉把自己的眼睛从身体里派出去，成为一个独立的“女儿”。她化作塞赫麦特——意思是“强大的女神”——几乎把人类全部消灭。

    Ra sends his Eye out from his body as a separate offshoot, a 'daughter'. As Sekhmet, 'the mighty goddess', she almost annihilates humankind.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation — the Tale of the Heavenly Cow · 同一概述的“拉的眼睛”段落。“强大的女神”是 UCL 给出的释义。
  3. 03

    一湖红色的啤酒A lake of red beer

    众神用计：他们把一湖啤酒染成红色，看起来像人血。她喝下后醉得香甜，脾气也软化下来，重新变回慈爱、温柔的哈托尔。

    The gods play a trick: they colour a lake of beer red to look like human blood. She drinks, becomes sweetly drunk, and reverts to her loving, maternal character as Hathor.

    塞特、奥西里斯与伊西斯Seth, Osiris and Isis

    后来，奥西里斯“无序的兄弟”塞特出于嫉妒杀害了他，把遗体分开，并取代他成为世界之王。伊西斯找回奥西里斯的肢体，让遗体恢复到足以受孕，生下了荷鲁斯。

    Later Osiris's anarchic brother Seth, out of jealousy, murders him, dismembers him and takes his place as king of the world. Isis collects the limbs and revives the body enough to conceive a child, Horus.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL 页面的奥西里斯故事概述。古代文献中这个故事有许多版本；本站不合成“标准版”。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

荷鲁斯 Horus

  1. 01

    太阳神的第一对子女The creator's first pair

    在埃及的创世观念里，主要的创造者是太阳神，他也被称为阿图姆。他最先生出一男一女：舒和泰芙努特。学者认为“舒”这个名字和“干燥”有关，“泰芙努特”则代表湿润。

    In Egyptian creation thought the principal creator is the sun-god, also called Atum. His first offshoots are a son and a daughter, Shu and Tefnet; scholars link Shu's name to dryness and read Tefnet as moisture.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL Digital Egypt 创世页面。“干燥／湿润”是现代学者的词源解释。
  2. 02

    大地与天空出生Earth and sky are born

    舒和泰芙努特又生下两个孩子：大地之神盖布和天空女神努特。干燥与湿润，变成了坚实的大地和古埃及人眼中充满水的天空。

    Shu and Tefnet in turn produce two children: Geb, god of the earth, and Nut, goddess of the sky — dryness and moisture crystallised into solid earth and a sky the Egyptians thought of as watery.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · 同一页面的子女段落。
  3. 03

    舒把天地分开Shu holds earth and sky apart

    盖布和努特被舒分开，天在上、地在下。零散的古代记载暗示，这种分离必须被强制维持，否则天地还会继续生出神来。

    Shu separates Geb and Nut, sky above and earth below. Scattered references suggest the separation had to be enforced, or earth and sky would keep producing divine offspring.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL 页面关于天地分离的说明。这是学者对零散材料的归纳，不是单一文本的原话。
  4. 04

    塞特、奥西里斯与伊西斯Seth, Osiris and Isis

    后来，奥西里斯“无序的兄弟”塞特出于嫉妒杀害了他，把遗体分开，并取代他成为世界之王。伊西斯找回奥西里斯的肢体，让遗体恢复到足以受孕，生下了荷鲁斯。

    Later Osiris's anarchic brother Seth, out of jealousy, murders him, dismembers him and takes his place as king of the world. Isis collects the limbs and revives the body enough to conceive a child, Horus.

    Gods and goddesses: creation · Gods and goddesses: creation · UCL 页面的奥西里斯故事概述。古代文献中这个故事有许多版本；本站不合成“标准版”。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

阿努比斯 Anubis

胡狼头神；在大英博物馆《阿尼纸草》第3框的称量心脏场景中扶稳天平。

Jackal-headed god who steadies the balance in the weighing of Ani's heart (British Museum EA10470,3).

  1. 01

    心脏与羽毛A heart and a feather

    画面中央是一座天平。左边的秤盘里放着阿尼的心脏，右边是一根羽毛，代表真理与秩序女神玛阿特。

    At the centre stands a balance. In the left pan lies Ani's heart; in the right, a feather representing Maat, goddess of truth and order.

    Papyrus of Ani, frame 3 · EA10470,3 — museum description of the Hall of Judgment · 大英博物馆 EA10470,3 藏品描述。
  2. 02

    阿努比斯扶稳天平Anubis steadies the balance

    胡狼头的阿努比斯跪在天平旁，拉着右边秤盘的绳子，扶稳天平的铅锤。博物馆的说明称他是“在防腐之所的那一位”。

    Jackal-headed Anubis kneels by the balance, holding the cord of the right-hand pan and steadying its plumb bob. The museum describes him as 'he who is in the place of embalming'.

    Papyrus of Ani, frame 3 · EA10470,3 — museum description of the Hall of Judgment · 同一藏品描述中关于阿努比斯的部分。
  3. 03

    托特准备记录Thoth stands ready to record

    托特在画中出现两次：一只小狒狒坐在天平顶端；右边站着朱鹭头的“众神书吏”，手持调色板和芦苇笔，准备记下审问的结果。

    Thoth appears twice: as a small baboon sitting on top of the balance, and as the ibis-headed scribe of the gods standing to the right with palette and reed, ready to write down the result.

    Papyrus of Ani, frame 3 · EA10470,3 — museum description of the Hall of Judgment · 同一藏品描述中关于托特的部分。称量的最终结果和吞噬心脏的怪兽不在这条博物馆描述里，本站不补写。
古埃及 / Ancient Egyptian · 有完整故事Full story

  2. 02

  3. 03

古希腊 / Ancient Greek · 有完整故事Full story

克洛诺斯 Cronus

  1. 01

    两方神族Two divine sides

    《神谱》的这一段把与克洛诺斯相连的泰坦置于宙斯及其盟友的对立面。

    This passage of the Theogony sets the Titans associated with Cronus against Zeus and his allies.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 617–735 · 《神谱》617–735行。“泰坦战争”是现代通行事件名，见证范围仍指向原诗行。
  2. 02

    雷霆与战斗Thunder and battle

    宙斯是战斗的主要参与者；事件档案把雷、闪电和雷电武器记录为本段战斗手段，并记录百臂巨人的介入。

    Zeus is a principal combatant; the event profile records thunder, lightning, and the thunderbolt as weapons in this passage and records the intervention of the Hundred-Handers.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 617–735 · 《神谱》617–735行的事件与参与者层。没有把后世图像或现代叙事写入古诗。
  3. 03

    战败与囚禁Defeat and confinement

    在这一见证中，泰坦战败并被囚入塔耳塔罗斯；这项结果不自动代表所有古代作者的细节完全相同。

    In this witness, the Titans are defeated and confined in Tartarus; the result does not imply that every ancient author gives identical details.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 617–735 · 《神谱》617–735行及事件结果档案。其他见证将在独立版本中比较。

哈得斯 Hades

《神谱》家谱中的克洛诺斯与瑞亚之子；《荷马颂歌·致得墨忒耳》中的冥界统治者与劫掠者。

Son of Cronus and Rhea and underworld ruler in the Hymn to Demeter.

  1. 01

    女儿被带走The daughter is taken

    赞歌开篇把珀耳塞福涅置于被带往冥界的事件中心，并说哈得斯在本见证中得到宙斯同意。

    The hymn opens with Persephone at the center of the taking to the underworld and says that Hades acts with the consent of Zeus in this witness.

    Homeric Hymn 2 to Demeter, Evelyn-White English text · Hymn 2, lines 1–495 · 《荷马颂歌·致得墨忒耳》1–32行。这里只陈述本赞歌的叙事，不代表所有希腊版本。
  2. 02

    得墨忒耳寻找Demeter searches

    得墨忒耳离开诸神寻找女儿。赫卡忒与赫利俄斯出现在寻找段落中，承担的角色以已登记的赞歌行号为限。

    Demeter leaves the gods and searches for her daughter. Hecate and Helios appear in the search passage, with their roles limited to the registered hymn lines.

    Homeric Hymn 2 to Demeter, Evelyn-White English text · Hymn 2, lines 1–495 · 赞歌1–87行及25–62行的已登记Claims。未把其他作者的寻找情节混入。
  3. 03

    厄琉息斯与回返框架Eleusis and the return framework

    事件档案记录得墨忒耳来到厄琉息斯；赞歌继续处理珀耳塞福涅的回返安排及仪式建立。秘密仪式的内部内容没有从公开证据重构。

    The event record brings Demeter to Eleusis; the hymn continues with arrangements for Persephone to return and the establishment of rites. The internal content of the mysteries is not reconstructed from public evidence.

    Homeric Hymn 2 to Demeter, Evelyn-White English text · Hymn 2, lines 1–495 · 赞歌事件索引与厄琉息斯资料层。仪式内部内容明确保留为未知。

宙斯 Zeus

希腊文本传统中的神王；当前页面分别连接雷霆、奥林匹亚、提丰之战和赫西俄德家谱证据。

Divine ruler in registered Greek textual and cult evidence.

  1. 01

    两方神族Two divine sides

    《神谱》的这一段把与克洛诺斯相连的泰坦置于宙斯及其盟友的对立面。

    This passage of the Theogony sets the Titans associated with Cronus against Zeus and his allies.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 617–735 · 《神谱》617–735行。“泰坦战争”是现代通行事件名，见证范围仍指向原诗行。
  2. 02

    雷霆与战斗Thunder and battle

    宙斯是战斗的主要参与者；事件档案把雷、闪电和雷电武器记录为本段战斗手段，并记录百臂巨人的介入。

    Zeus is a principal combatant; the event profile records thunder, lightning, and the thunderbolt as weapons in this passage and records the intervention of the Hundred-Handers.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 617–735 · 《神谱》617–735行的事件与参与者层。没有把后世图像或现代叙事写入古诗。
  3. 03

    战败与囚禁Defeat and confinement

    在这一见证中，泰坦战败并被囚入塔耳塔罗斯；这项结果不自动代表所有古代作者的细节完全相同。

    In this witness, the Titans are defeated and confined in Tartarus; the result does not imply that every ancient author gives identical details.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 617–735 · 《神谱》617–735行及事件结果档案。其他见证将在独立版本中比较。

狄俄涅 Dione

《伊利亚特》第5卷称阿佛洛狄忒投入其母狄俄涅怀中；该谱系与赫西俄德起源叙事并存。

The Iliad names Dione as Aphrodite’s mother; this coexists with the Hesiodic origin account.

  1. 01

    狄俄涅被称为母亲Dione is called mother

    《伊利亚特》第5卷写阿佛洛狄忒来到母亲狄俄涅身边。这是亲属称谓见证，不是完整诞生场景。

    Iliad Book 5 presents Aphrodite coming to her mother Dione. This is a kinship witness, not a complete birth scene.

    Homer, Iliad · Book 5, lines 370–372; Book 14, line 193 · 《伊利亚特》第5卷370–372行。不要从一处称谓推演未叙述的出生过程。
  2. 02

    宙斯之女Daughter of Zeus

    《伊利亚特》第14卷又把阿佛洛狄忒称为宙斯之女，形成与《神谱》海中诞生不同的家谱层。

    Iliad Book 14 also calls Aphrodite a daughter of Zeus, creating a genealogical layer different from the sea-birth account in the Theogony.

    Homer, Iliad · Book 5, lines 370–372; Book 14, line 193 · 《伊利亚特》第14卷193行。“女儿”是文本称谓；本页不补写其出生情节。
  3. 03

    两个古代版本共存Two ancient versions coexist

    数据库保留《神谱》与《伊利亚特》的差异，并通过冲突档案让读者并排查看，而不是按名称或现代常识合并。

    The database preserves the difference between the Theogony and the Iliad and exposes it through a conflict record instead of merging the accounts by name or modern convention.

    Homer, Iliad · Book 5, lines 370–372; Book 14, line 193 · 两个古代文本见证及显式冲突档案。冲突状态保持开放。

珀耳塞斯（赫利俄斯之子） Perses (son of Helios)

狄奥多罗斯与《书库》科尔喀斯叙事中的珀耳塞斯，赫利俄斯之子、埃厄忒斯之兄弟；不是赫卡忒之父。

Colchian Perses, son of Helios and brother of Aeetes; not the father of Hecate.

  1. 01

    回到科尔喀斯Return to Colchis

    《书库》1.9.28把美狄亚写成返回科尔喀斯并采取行动的人物。

    Library 1.9.28 presents Medea as the figure who returns to Colchis and acts.

    Pseudo-Apollodorus, Library · Library 1.9.28 · 《书库》1.9.28。这是汇编中的短序列，不扩写对话或动机。
  2. 02

    同名者的结局The fate of a namesake

    在这一段中，被杀并被废黜的是赫利俄斯之子、埃厄忒斯兄弟的珀耳塞斯，而不是赫卡忒之父的泰坦珀耳塞斯。

    The Perses killed and deposed here is the son of Helios and brother of Aeetes, not the Titan Perses who is father of Hecate.

    Pseudo-Apollodorus, Library · Library 1.9.28 · 《书库》1.9.28与同名实体审计。严格防止同名人物合并。
  3. 03

    王位恢复Restoration of the throne

    短篇以埃厄忒斯恢复王位结束；当前证据没有为这一结局补写更长的政治过程。

    The short sequence ends with Aeetes restored to the kingdom; the current evidence does not invent a longer political process.

    Pseudo-Apollodorus, Library · Library 1.9.28 · 《书库》1.9.28。当前证据未说明的过程保持空缺。

Daughter of Demeter in the registered Theogony and Hymn to Demeter witnesses.

    女儿被带走The daughter is taken

  2. 02

    Demeter leaves the gods and searches for her daughter. Hecate and Helios appear in the search passage, with their roles limited to the registered hymn lines.

    Homeric Hymn 2 to Demeter, Evelyn-White English text · Hymn 2, lines 1–495 · 赞歌1–87行及25–62行的已登记Claims。未把其他作者的寻找情节混入。

盖亚 Gaia

《神谱》宇宙起源与家谱中的大地神；本版本连接乌拉诺斯与克洛诺斯谱系。

Earth deity in Hesiodic cosmogony and genealogy.

  1. 01

    提丰出现Typhon appears

    赫西俄德的段落把提丰置于宙斯的对手位置，并以一场神圣冲突展开。

    The Hesiodic passage places Typhon as the opponent of Zeus and unfolds as a divine conflict.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 820–885 · 《神谱》820–885行。提丰的出身与形貌只在未来逐行版本中扩充。
  2. 02

    宙斯发动雷霆Zeus unleashes thunder

    事件档案记录宙斯以雷、闪电和雷电武器进攻；此处没有把后世宙斯武器造型反投到赫西俄德文本。

    The event profile records Zeus attacking with thunder, lightning, and the thunderbolt; later visual forms of the weapon are not projected back into Hesiod.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 820–885 · 《神谱》820–885行。武器名与图像史须另行建模。
  3. 03

    本见证的结局Outcome in this witness

    提丰在赫西俄德的战斗段落中被击败；数据库把这个结局限定在该文本见证。

    Typhon is defeated in the Hesiodic combat passage; the database scopes this outcome to that textual witness.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 820–885 · 《神谱》820–885行。其他作者的结局版本未在本页合并。

阿佛洛狄忒 Aphrodite

爱欲女神；赫西俄德起源叙事与《伊利亚特》的宙斯—狄俄涅谱系作为冲突版本并存。

Goddess of desire; the Hesiodic origin and Iliadic Zeus–Dione genealogy remain coexisting variants.

  1. 01

    乌拉诺斯之后After Uranus

    《神谱》的出身叙事把阿佛洛狄忒的出现连接到乌拉诺斯受创后落入海中的身体部分。

    The Theogony connects the appearance of Aphrodite to what falls into the sea after the wounding of Uranus.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 188–206 · 《神谱》188–206行。本站以简洁概述处理古代文本中的身体暴力。
  2. 02

    海中诞生Birth from the sea

    这一见证叙述她从海中形成并显现，因此不使用“宙斯之女”作为此版本的出生框架。

    This witness narrates her formation and appearance from the sea and therefore does not use the daughter-of-Zeus genealogy as its birth frame.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 188–206 · 《神谱》188–206行。只说明此版本，不否定其他古代见证。
  3. 03

    版本边界Version boundary

    海中诞生是《神谱》的故事版本；《伊利亚特》的家谱称谓在下一版本单独阅读。

    The sea birth is the Theogony version; the genealogy terms in the Iliad are read separately in the next version.

    Hesiod, Theogony, Evelyn-White English text · lines 188–206 · 冲突档案 conflict.greek.aphrodite_parentage_v050。系统不裁定唯一正确版本。

雅典娜 Athena

吠陀传统 / Vedic · 有完整故事Full story

因陀罗 Indra

《梨俱吠陀》中的因陀罗：金刚杵持有者，并在 1.32 与 2.12 的具体见证中与蛇敌、受阻水流、大气和战斗相连。

Indra in registered Rigvedic witnesses, wielder of the vajra and linked to serpent combat, released waters, atmosphere and battle.

  1. 01

    颂歌中的对手Opponents in the hymn

    《梨俱吠陀》1.32把因陀罗列为主要行动者，把弗栗多列为他的对手。

    Rigveda 1.32 identifies Indra as the principal agent and Vrtra as his opponent.

    Rigveda Shakala Samhita, Mandala 1, Sukta 32 · Mandala 1, Sukta 32, verses 1–15 · RV 1.32.1–15。本页只代表此颂歌见证。
  2. 02

    金刚杵The vajra

    同一颂歌的登记Claims把金刚杵连接到因陀罗，并把其制造连接到陀湿多。

    Claims registered from the same hymn connect the vajra to Indra and its making to Tvastr.

    Rigveda Shakala Samhita, Mandala 1, Sukta 32 · Mandala 1, Sukta 32, verses 1–15 · RV 1.32金刚杵见证。不把后期佛教或图像版本反投到吠陀颂歌。
  3. 03

    水流释放Release of the waters

    事件档案以对手被击杀、水得以流出作为颂歌叙事结果；它不是现代自然科学陈述。

    The event profile records the opponent struck down and the waters flowing out as the narrative result of the hymn; it is not a modern scientific statement.

    Rigveda Shakala Samhita, Mandala 1, Sukta 32 · Mandala 1, Sukta 32, verses 1–15 · RV 1.32事件索引。知识层保持为神话叙事／文本见证。

墨西卡 / Mexica · 有完整故事Full story

特拉洛克 Tlaloc

与农业相关的雨神；大神庙北侧是他的神庙（墨西哥国家人类学与历史研究所）。

Rain god linked to agriculture whose temple occupied the north side of the Templo Mayor (INAH).

  1. 01

    南边的战神The war god on the south

    大神庙南侧的神殿属于维齐洛波奇特利——墨西加人的太阳战神，也是他们的守护神。

    The southern chapel of the Templo Mayor belongs to Huitzilopochtli, the Mexica's solar god of war and their patron.

    Museo de Sitio del Templo Mayor · Museum description · INAH 大神庙遗址博物馆说明。
  2. 02

    北边的雨神The rain god on the north

    北侧的神殿属于特拉洛克——掌管雨水、与农业紧密相连的神。战争与农业，两位神并立在同一座金字塔的顶端。

    The northern temple belongs to Tlaloc, god of rain and closely tied to agriculture. War and farming stand side by side at the top of one pyramid.

    Museo de Sitio del Templo Mayor · Museum description · INAH 大神庙遗址博物馆说明。
  3. 03

    一层层长高的神庙A temple that grew in layers

    据 INAH 介绍，墨西加人在1325年建立特诺奇蒂特兰时修了一座小神庙，此后经过多次重建和扩建，越修越高。

    According to INAH, the Mexica built a small temple when they founded Tenochtitlan in 1325, and it was rebuilt and enlarged many times over.

    Museo de Sitio del Templo Mayor · Museum description · INAH 大神庙遗址博物馆说明。各建造阶段的具体年代仍有不确定之处。
墨西卡 / Mexica · 有完整故事Full story

特斯卡特利波卡 Tezcatlipoca

又称蒂特拉卡瓦；《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷中令羽蛇神在图拉衰落的巫师之一。

Also called Titlacauan; among the sorcerers who bring Quetzalcoatl's glory at Tollan to an end (Florentine Codex Book 3).

  1. 01

    被当作神的蒂特拉卡瓦Titlacauan, held to be a god

    第三卷第2章讲人们怎样把一位叫蒂特拉卡瓦、也叫特斯卡特利波卡的人物当作神来敬奉。

    Chapter 2 of Book 3 tells how one called Titlacauan, or Tezcatlipoca, came to be regarded as a god.

    Digital Florentine Codex · Book 3, chapters 2–5 and 14 · 《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第2章。
  2. 02

    图拉的大法师The great wizard of Tollan

    第3章讲羽蛇神——图拉的一位大法师。第4章说，当巫师们来到他身边，他的荣光便走到了尽头；第5章又记下蒂特拉卡瓦带来的另一个异兆。

    Chapter 3 tells of Quetzalcoatl, a great wizard at Tollan. Chapter 4 relates how his glory came to an end when the sorcerers came to him, and chapter 5 records another portent brought about by Titlacauan.

    Digital Florentine Codex · Book 3, chapters 2–5 and 14 · 《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第3–5章。本段依据章节标题与概要，具体情节尚未逐句核对。
  3. 03

    雪山之间Between the snow mountains

    第14章记载，羽蛇神离去时，追随他的人经过伊斯塔克特佩特尔与波波卡特佩特尔两座雪山之间，被冻死在冰雪里。

    Chapter 14 records that as Quetzalcoatl departs, his vassals freeze to death in the ice as they pass between Iztactepetl and Popocatepetl.

    Digital Florentine Codex · Book 3, chapters 2–5 and 14 · 《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第14章。
墨西卡 / Mexica · 有完整故事Full story

科约尔沙乌基 Coyolxauhqui

Daughter of Coatlicue who leads her brothers against their mother and is killed by Huitzilopochtli at Coatepec.

  1. 01

    一团羽毛A ball of feathers

    科阿特利库埃住在蛇山上。一天她打扫时，一团羽毛从天上飘下来。她把羽毛捡起来，塞在腰间——就这样怀孕了。

    Coatlicue lives on Coatepec. One day as she sweeps, a ball of feathers drifts down. She picks it up and tucks it at her waist — and so she becomes pregnant.

    Digital Florentine Codex · Book 3, chapter 1 · 《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章。
  2. 02

    兄妹要杀母亲Her children turn against her

    她的女儿科约尔沙乌基对兄弟们——四百个南方人——说：母亲让我们蒙羞，我们必须杀了她。于是他们全副武装，朝蛇山进发。

    Her daughter Coyolxauhqui tells her brothers, the four hundred Centzon Huitznahua, that their mother has shamed them and must be killed. Armed, they set out for Coatepec.

    Digital Florentine Codex · Book 3, chapter 1 · 《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章。
  3. 03

    全副武装地出生Born fully armed

    腹中的维齐洛波奇特利安慰母亲：不要怕，我知道该怎么做。敌人冲上山顶的那一刻，他出生了，手持盾牌、投枪和投枪器。

    From the womb Huitzilopochtli comforts his mother: do not be afraid, I know what to do. The moment the attackers reach the summit he is born, carrying shield, darts and dart-thrower.

    Digital Florentine Codex · Book 3, chapter 1 · 《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章。
  4. 04

    山顶的胜利Victory on the summit

    维齐洛波奇特利砍下科约尔沙乌基的头，她的身体沿着蛇山滚落下去。四百个兄弟也被他从山顶追得四散奔逃。

    Huitzilopochtli cuts off Coyolxauhqui's head, and her body tumbles down the side of Coatepec. He drives the four hundred brothers from the summit, scattering them in every direction.

    Digital Florentine Codex · Book 3, chapter 1 · 《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷第1章。学者认为这段叙事与大神庙的布局相呼应，属于解释，不写成事实。

维齐洛波奇特利 Huitzilopochtli

墨西加人的太阳战神与守护神；《佛罗伦萨手抄本》第三卷说他在蛇山全副武装地诞生；大神庙南侧是他的神殿。

夏威夷 / Hawaiian · 有完整故事Full story

佩蕾 Pele

夏威夷火山之神；据美国国家公园管理局对口述传统的概述，她从卡希基远航而来，定居基拉韦厄的哈雷茂茂火山口。

Hawaiian volcano deity who, in NPS's summary of oral history, voyaged from Kahiki and made her home in Halemaʻumaʻu crater at Kīlauea.

  1. 01

    生在卡希基Born in Kahiki

    国家公园管理局介绍说，按照口述传统，佩蕾是夏威夷的火山之神，是一股自然之力，也是这些火山地貌的创造者。她出生在遥远的卡希基，为了给自己的火和家人寻找合适的家，乘船来到夏威夷群岛。

    The National Park Service explains that, in oral history, Pele is the Hawaiian volcano deity, an elemental force and the creator of these volcanic landscapes. Born in the distant land of Kahiki, she voyaged to the Hawaiian Islands to find a suitable home for her fire and family.

    Pele · Pele; Holo Mai Pele · NPS“Pele”与“Holo Mai Pele”页面。
  2. 02

    一路向东南Island by island to the southeast

    她一座岛一座岛地向东南迁移，寻找能安顿下来的地方。

    She moved southeast through the islands, one after another, looking for a place to settle.

    Pele · Pele; Holo Mai Pele · NPS“Pele”页面。各岛上的具体经历因版本而异，本站不补写。
  3. 03

    哈雷茂茂的家A home in Halemaʻumaʻu

    最后她来到基拉韦厄山顶的哈雷茂茂火山口，把这里定为永久的家。据说她至今仍住在那里。

    At last she came to Halemaʻumaʻu crater at the summit of Kīlauea and made it her permanent home. She is said to live there still.

    Pele · Pele; Holo Mai Pele · NPS 两页介绍。
  4. 04

    经过她的土地Passing through her land

    在夏威夷传统中，经过佩蕾的土地要先请求她的许可。国家公园管理局也请访客在这片神圣的地景中保持尊重。公园还提醒：不同的 moʻolelo 版本可能差别很大。

    In Hawaiian tradition it is customary to ask Pele's permission to travel through her land, and the park asks visitors to behave respectfully in this sacred landscape. It also notes that versions of the moʻolelo may differ, sometimes greatly.

    Pele · Pele; Holo Mai Pele · NPS“Pele”页面。这里只讲公开概述；家族与地方传统不在收集范围。
巴比伦 / Babylonian · 有完整故事Full story

提亚马特 Tiamat

  1. 01

    创世战斗的两方The two sides of the creation combat

    登记的《埃努玛·埃利什》Claims确认马尔杜克与提亚马特都参与这场创世战斗。

    Registered Enuma elish claims confirm that both Marduk and Tiamat participate in the creation combat.

    Poem of Creation (Enūma eliš) · Poem of Creation corpus L/1/2 · 电子巴比伦图书馆语料L/1/2。战斗逐步过程尚未入库。
  2. 02

    数字校勘语料A digital critical corpus

    故事页直接连接电子巴比伦图书馆的数字语料，而不是引用无出处的现代故事改写。

    The story page links directly to the electronic Babylonian Library corpus instead of relying on an unsourced modern retelling.

    Poem of Creation (Enūma eliš) · Poem of Creation corpus L/1/2 · 电子巴比伦图书馆语料登记。语料界面与翻译权利按来源条款。
  3. 03

    当前版本的边界Boundary of the current version

    胜负、世界形成与王权确认的详细次序将在泥板逐行建模后加入；当前不把常见概述冒充已验证Claims。

    The detailed order of victory, world formation, and kingship will be added after tablet-level modeling; common summaries are not presented as verified claims here.

    Poem of Creation (Enūma eliš) · Poem of Creation corpus L/1/2 · 当前Claim层与故事版本状态PARTIAL。当前证据未细分这些阶段。

斯拉夫 / Slavic · 有完整故事Full story

佩伦 Perun

《往年纪事》所见东斯拉夫佩伦：条约誓言、980 年基辅神像与 988 年毁像分别登记；“全斯拉夫唯一主神”与命名神器不作无证断言。

Perun in East Slavic chronicle witnesses: treaty oaths, the 980 Kyiv idol and its 988 removal are separate claims; no unsupported universal pantheon or named weapon is asserted.

  1. 01

    山岗上的神像Idols on the hill

    《往年纪事》980 年条记载：弗拉基米尔开始在基辅独自为王，就在宫殿庭院外的山岗上立起一排神像。

    The Primary Chronicle's annal for 980 records that when Volodimer began to reign alone in Kyiv, he set up a row of idols on the hill outside the palace courtyard.

    Tale of Bygone Years digital critical edition · annals 980 and 988 · 《往年纪事》980 年条。
  2. 02

    银头金须的佩伦Perun with a silver head

    最前面的是佩伦：木头雕成，头是银的，胡须是金的。

    First stood Perun, carved of wood, with a silver head and a golden moustache.

    Tale of Bygone Years digital critical edition · annals 980 and 988 · 《往年纪事》980 年条。
  3. 03

    名单最后的莫科什Mokosh at the end of the list

    接下来是霍尔斯、达日博格、斯特里博格、西马尔格尔，名单的最后一位是莫科什。人们向这些神像献上祭品。编年史只记下了莫科什的名字，没有说她掌管什么。

    Then came Khors, Dazhbog, Stribog and Simargl, and last on the list, Mokosh. People brought offerings to the idols. The chronicle records only Mokosh's name, not what she governed.

    Tale of Bygone Years digital critical edition · annals 980 and 988 · 《往年纪事》980 年条神名名单。关于莫科什职能的说法来自其他材料与现代研究，本故事不补写。
  4. 04

    988 年：神像倒下988: the idols fall

    八年后，弗拉基米尔受洗，下令推倒这些神像：有的砍碎，有的烧掉。佩伦被绑在马尾上拖下山，有人用棍子抽打，最后被扔进第聂伯河。

    Eight years later Volodimer was baptized and ordered the idols overthrown — some cut to pieces, others burned. Perun was tied to a horse's tail and dragged down the hill, beaten with sticks, and thrown into the Dnieper.

    Tale of Bygone Years digital critical edition · annals 980 and 988 · 《往年纪事》988 年条。这是皈依一方的记载，不是对斯拉夫宗教的中立描述。
斯拉夫 / Slavic · 证据卡Evidence card

维列斯 Veles

  1. 01

    佩伦与维列斯一同被呼告。

    The 907 and 971 treaty-oath traditions invoke Perun and Veles or Volos together.

    Tale of Bygone Years digital critical edition · annals 907 and 971
斯拉夫 / Slavic · 有完整故事Full story

莫科什 Mokosh

《往年纪事》980 年条中弗拉基米尔在基辅山岗所立神像名单的最后一位；该处未说明其职能。

Named last among the idols Volodimer set up on the Kyiv hill in the Primary Chronicle's annal for 980; no function is stated there.

  1. 01

    山岗上的神像Idols on the hill

日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

伊邪那岐 Izanagi

《古事记》中的男性创世神：与伊邪那美造出淤能碁吕岛，追往黄泉，归来禊祓时生出天照大神、月读与须佐之男。

Male creator deity of the Kojiki: with Izanami he forms Onogoro Island, follows her to Yomi, and on returning gives birth to Amaterasu, Tsukuyomi and Susanoo as he purifies himself.

  1. 01

    天浮桥上的长矛A spear from the Floating Bridge

    天上的众神命令伊邪那岐和伊邪那美去巩固还在漂浮的大地，并交给他们一支镶玉的天沼矛。两神站在天浮桥上，把矛伸进海里搅动；提起矛时，矛尖滴下的盐水层层堆积，成了淤能碁吕岛。

    The heavenly deities command Izanagi and Izanami to make firm the drifting land and give them a jewelled spear. Standing on the Floating Bridge of Heaven, they stir the brine; when they lift the spear, the drops from its tip pile up into Onogoro Island.

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第三节（kj010）。《日本书纪》说两神是自己决定动手，本版不合并。
  2. 02

    火神出生The birth of the fire deity

    两神结合，生出许多岛屿和神灵。可是伊邪那美生下火神时被烧伤，病倒在床，最终“神去”——也就是死去。

    Joined together, the pair bring forth islands and deities. But when Izanami gives birth to the Deity-of-Fire she is burned, falls sick, and at length 'divinely retires' — she dies.

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第七节（kj014）。
  3. 03

    追到黄泉Following her to Yomi

    伊邪那岐想再见到妻子，便追到黄泉，请她回去：“我们造的国土还没有完成。”伊邪那美回答：你来得太晚了，我已经吃了黄泉灶火煮的东西——不过她答应去和黄泉的神商量。

    Longing to see her, Izanagi follows Izanami to Yomi and asks her to come back, for the lands they made are not yet finished. She answers that he has come too late: she has already eaten from the hearth of Yomi — yet she agrees to consult the deities there.

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第九节（kj016）。之后的逃离情节可参看本库“黄泉中的八雷神”故事。
  4. 04

    洗出三位贵神Three deities born of washing

    伊邪那岐回来后在水中洗净自己，身上不断化生出神。最后，他洗左眼时生出天照大神，洗右眼时生出月读，洗鼻子时生出须佐之男。

    Back from Yomi, Izanagi bathes to purify himself, and deities are born as he washes. Last of all, washing his left eye brings forth Amaterasu, his right eye Tsukuyomi, and his nose Susanoo.

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第十节（kj017）及译注。三神的通行名称来自译者注释。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

伊邪那美 Izanami

《古事记》中的女性创世神：与伊邪那岐一同造地，生火神后被灼伤而死，留在黄泉。

  4. 04

    洗出三位贵神Three deities born of washing

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第十节（kj017）及译注。三神的通行名称来自译者注释。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

伏雷 Fusuikazuchi

《古事记》黄泉段八雷神之一。

One of the eight thunder kami in the Kojiki Yomi episode.

  1. 01

    黄泉段落The Yomi passage

    学术神名索引把八位雷神定位到《古事记》上卷的黄泉段落，并说他们出现在伊邪那美身上。

    The scholarly divine-name index locates the eight thunder kami in the Yomi episode of the upper Kojiki scroll and places them on Izanami.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 《古事记》上卷黄泉段落索引。只采用公开学术索引范围。
  2. 02

    八个独立名称Eight distinct names

    数据库为大雷、火雷、黑雷、拆雷、若雷、土雷、鸣雷与伏雷分别建档，不把八名压成一个泛称。

    The database keeps Ooikazuchi, Honoikazuchi, Kuroikazuchi, Sakuikazuchi, Wakaikazuchi, Tsuchiikazuchi, Naruikazuchi, and Fusuikazuchi as separate records rather than one generic name.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 八个成员Claims及神名索引。部分名称的精细语义仍待校勘。
  3. 03

    不自动等同地方神No automatic local identification

    鸣雷、若雷等相近名称在后世神社或地方传统中可能出现，但系统不会仅凭名字相似就认定为《古事记》中的同一神。

    Similar names such as Naruikazuchi or Wakaikazuchi may occur in later shrine or local traditions, but name similarity alone does not establish identity with the Kojiki figures.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 成员Claim及身份冲突档案。身份关系保留为争议或未知。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 证据卡Evidence card

加雷神／地方雷神 Karaijin

國學院资料列为地方神社所奉雷神名之一；本档与 Raijin 泛称及贺茂别雷大神分开。

Name used for thunder kami at local shrines in the Kokugakuin overview; distinct from the generic Raijin dossier and Kamo Wakeikazuchi.

  1. 01

    加雷神／地方雷神——关联：must not be merged with Kamo Wakeikazuchi solely because both are thunder kami

    Kokugakuin lists Karaijin and Kamo Wakeikazuchi as separate local shrine examples.

    Raijin · Authority record and scoped summary
  2. 02

    加雷神／地方雷神与雷神相关。

    Karaijin is registered under the broader thunder-kami research umbrella but remains a distinct regional dossier.

    Raijin · Authority record and scoped summary

日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

天照大神 Amaterasu

  1. 01

    须佐之男与八岐大蛇Susanoo and Yamata no Orochi

    國學院学术条目概述须佐之男击败八岐大蛇。

    The Kokugakuin academic entry summarizes Susanoo defeating Yamata no Orochi.

    Yamatanoorochi · Yamatanoorochi — Kojiki/Nihongi narrative summary · 國學院大学 Encyclopedia of Shinto 的 Yamatanoorochi 条目。条目列出的象征解释仍属于学术解释，不写入叙事事实。
  2. 02

    在蛇尾中发现神剑The sword found in the serpent's tail

    同一学术综述把草薙剑的发现放在大蛇尾部。

    The same academic summary places the discovery of Kusanagi in the serpent's tail.

    Yamatanoorochi · Yamatanoorochi — Kojiki/Nihongi narrative summary · Yamatanoorochi 条目的神剑发现段。这是文本叙事，不是考古出土记录。
  3. 03

    呈给天照大神Presented to Amaterasu

    该条目说明须佐之男把剑呈给天照大神；后续奉祀另由不同来源版本叙述。

    The entry says Susanoo presented the sword to Amaterasu; later enshrinement is handled in a different source version.

    Yamatanoorochi · Yamatanoorochi — Kojiki/Nihongi narrative summary · Yamatanoorochi 条目的呈递段。不以呈递动作自动证明所有后续持有链。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 证据卡Evidence card

建御雷 Takemikazuchi

古典叙事主要登记为武神／天神使者；雷电关联为次级且不与“雷神”自动合并。

Primarily a martial heavenly envoy in registered classical narratives; thunder association remains secondary and does not merge the deity with Raijin.

  1. 01

    建御雷出现在古事记中。

    Kojiki traditions place Takemikazuchi in divine-generation, land-transfer and imperial-expedition narrative layers.

    Takemikazuchi · Upper and Middle Scroll locations indexed by Kokugakuin
  2. 02

    建御雷与雷相关。

    Takemikazuchi’s name and reception support a secondary thunder association, while the registered classical actions are primarily martial and emissarial.

    Takemikazuchi · Kokugakuin entry ID 9145
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

月读 Tsukuyomi

月神；《古事记》中伊邪那岐洗右眼时所生，张伯伦译作“掌管月夜者”。

Moon deity born when Izanagi washes his right eye (Kojiki); Chamberlain renders the name 'Moon-Night-Possessor'.

  1. 01

    天浮桥上的长矛A spear from the Floating Bridge

    天上的众神命令伊邪那岐和伊邪那美去巩固还在漂浮的大地，并交给他们一支镶玉的天沼矛。两神站在天浮桥上，把矛伸进海里搅动；提起矛时，矛尖滴下的盐水层层堆积，成了淤能碁吕岛。

    The heavenly deities command Izanagi and Izanami to make firm the drifting land and give them a jewelled spear. Standing on the Floating Bridge of Heaven, they stir the brine; when they lift the spear, the drops from its tip pile up into Onogoro Island.

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第三节（kj010）。《日本书纪》说两神是自己决定动手，本版不合并。
  2. 02

    火神出生The birth of the fire deity

    两神结合，生出许多岛屿和神灵。可是伊邪那美生下火神时被烧伤，病倒在床，最终“神去”——也就是死去。

    Joined together, the pair bring forth islands and deities. But when Izanami gives birth to the Deity-of-Fire she is burned, falls sick, and at length 'divinely retires' — she dies.

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第七节（kj014）。
  3. 03

    追到黄泉Following her to Yomi

    伊邪那岐想再见到妻子，便追到黄泉，请她回去：“我们造的国土还没有完成。”伊邪那美回答：你来得太晚了，我已经吃了黄泉灶火煮的东西——不过她答应去和黄泉的神商量。

    Longing to see her, Izanagi follows Izanami to Yomi and asks her to come back, for the lands they made are not yet finished. She answers that he has come too late: she has already eaten from the hearth of Yomi — yet she agrees to consult the deities there.

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第九节（kj016）。之后的逃离情节可参看本库“黄泉中的八雷神”故事。
  4. 04

    洗出三位贵神Three deities born of washing

    伊邪那岐回来后在水中洗净自己，身上不断化生出神。最后，他洗左眼时生出天照大神，洗右眼时生出月读，洗鼻子时生出须佐之男。

    Back from Yomi, Izanagi bathes to purify himself, and deities are born as he washes. Last of all, washing his left eye brings forth Amaterasu, his right eye Tsukuyomi, and his nose Susanoo.

    The Kojiki: Records of Ancient Matters (tr. Basil Hall Chamberlain) · Sections III–X (kj010–kj017) · 张伯伦译本第十节（kj017）及译注。三神的通行名称来自译者注释。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

析雷 Sakuikazuchi

《古事记》黄泉段八雷神之一。

One of the eight thunder kami in the Kojiki Yomi episode.

  1. 01

    黄泉段落The Yomi passage

    学术神名索引把八位雷神定位到《古事记》上卷的黄泉段落，并说他们出现在伊邪那美身上。

    The scholarly divine-name index locates the eight thunder kami in the Yomi episode of the upper Kojiki scroll and places them on Izanami.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 《古事记》上卷黄泉段落索引。只采用公开学术索引范围。
  2. 02

    八个独立名称Eight distinct names

    数据库为大雷、火雷、黑雷、拆雷、若雷、土雷、鸣雷与伏雷分别建档，不把八名压成一个泛称。

    The database keeps Ooikazuchi, Honoikazuchi, Kuroikazuchi, Sakuikazuchi, Wakaikazuchi, Tsuchiikazuchi, Naruikazuchi, and Fusuikazuchi as separate records rather than one generic name.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 八个成员Claims及神名索引。部分名称的精细语义仍待校勘。
  3. 03

    不自动等同地方神No automatic local identification

    鸣雷、若雷等相近名称在后世神社或地方传统中可能出现，但系统不会仅凭名字相似就认定为《古事记》中的同一神。

    Similar names such as Naruikazuchi or Wakaikazuchi may occur in later shrine or local traditions, but name similarity alone does not establish identity with the Kojiki figures.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 成员Claim及身份冲突档案。身份关系保留为争议或未知。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

火之迦具土神 Kagutsuchi (Deity of Fire)

Fire deity whose birth burns Izanami in the Kojiki; Chamberlain calls him the Deity-of-Fire and names him Kagu-tsu-chi in his notes.

  1. 01

    天浮桥上的长矛A spear from the Floating Bridge

    天上的众神命令伊邪那岐和伊邪那美去巩固还在漂浮的大地，并交给他们一支镶玉的天沼矛。两神站在天浮桥上，把矛伸进海里搅动；提起矛时，矛尖滴下的盐水层层堆积，成了淤能碁吕岛。

    洗出三位贵神Three deities born of washing
