火雷神（乙训传统） Honoikazuchi (Otokuni tradition)

学术条目所述乙训郡火雷神／红箭谱系人物；不与《古事记》黄泉八雷中的火雷自动合并。

Honoikazuchi of the Otokuni red-arrow genealogy; not automatically merged with the Kojiki Yomi thunder kami.

  1. 01

    逸文而非完整原书A fragment, not the complete work

    资料来自《山城国风土记》逸文的学术说明；原书没有完整保存，因此系统不重建一部想象中的完整《风土记》。

    The evidence comes from an academic account of a surviving Yamashiro Fudoki fragment; the full work is not extant, so the system does not reconstruct an imagined complete text.

    Boshijin · Fragmentary Yamashiro no kuni fudoki genealogy discussion · 國學院大学公开学术条目。文本状态明确为fragment。
  2. 02

    玉依姬与红箭Tamayorihime and the red arrow

    逸文谱系点名玉依姬，并把红箭解释为乙训地区所祭火雷神的变化形。

    The fragmentary genealogy names Tamayorihime and explains the red arrow as a transformed form of Honoikazuchi worshipped in Otokuni.

    Boshijin · Fragmentary Yamashiro no kuni fudoki genealogy discussion · 逸文谱系学术说明。独立概述，不复制现代条目原文。
  3. 03

    贺茂别雷大神的出生谱系Birth genealogy of Kamo Wakeikazuchi

    现有Claims把玉依姬登记为母亲，把红箭所对应的火雷神登记为父系角色，并把结果连接到贺茂别雷大神。

    Existing claims register Tamayorihime as mother, the thunder deity associated with the red arrow as the paternal figure, and Kamo Wakeikazuchi as the resulting child.

    Boshijin · Fragmentary Yamashiro no kuni fudoki genealogy discussion · 逸文谱系的三个参与者Claims。地方传统与《古事记》八雷神仍保持分离。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

玉依姬（贺茂传统） Tamayorihime (Kamo tradition)

贺茂谱系中的玉依姬；“玉依姬”也可指其他人物，因此使用限定实体。

Kamo-genealogy Tamayorihime; the title/name can denote other figures, so this record is scoped.

  1. 01

    逸文而非完整原书A fragment, not the complete work

    资料来自《山城国风土记》逸文的学术说明；原书没有完整保存，因此系统不重建一部想象中的完整《风土记》。

    Boshijin · Fragmentary Yamashiro no kuni fudoki genealogy discussion · 國學院大学公开学术条目。文本状态明确为fragment。
  2. 02

    玉依姬与红箭Tamayorihime and the red arrow

    逸文谱系点名玉依姬，并把红箭解释为乙训地区所祭火雷神的变化形。

    The fragmentary genealogy names Tamayorihime and explains the red arrow as a transformed form of Honoikazuchi worshipped in Otokuni.

    Boshijin · Fragmentary Yamashiro no kuni fudoki genealogy discussion · 逸文谱系学术说明。独立概述，不复制现代条目原文。
  3. 03

    贺茂别雷大神的出生谱系Birth genealogy of Kamo Wakeikazuchi

    现有Claims把玉依姬登记为母亲，把红箭所对应的火雷神登记为父系角色，并把结果连接到贺茂别雷大神。

    Existing claims register Tamayorihime as mother, the thunder deity associated with the red arrow as the paternal figure, and Kamo Wakeikazuchi as the resulting child.

    Boshijin · Fragmentary Yamashiro no kuni fudoki genealogy discussion · 逸文谱系的三个参与者Claims。地方传统与《古事记》八雷神仍保持分离。

雷神 Raijin

“雷神”作为跨时期的雷神称谓／复合入口；地方雷神与古典八雷分别保存。

Umbrella entry for Japanese thunder-kami traditions; regional deities and the Kojiki eight thunder kami remain distinct.

  1. 01

    贺茂别雷大神与雷神相关。

    Kamo Wakeikazuchi is connected to the broader Japanese thunder-kami research umbrella while remaining a distinct regional dossier.

    Boshijin · Official catalogue or public institutional description
  2. 02

    俵屋宗达《风神雷神图屏风》描绘了雷神。

    Kyoto National Museum identifies the paired composition as Wind God and Thunder God, providing a seventeenth-century Raijin visual witness.

    Wind God and Thunder God screens · Official catalogue or public institutional description

    加雷神／地方雷神与雷神相关。

日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

须佐之男 Susanoo

  1. 01

    须佐之男与八岐大蛇Susanoo and Yamata no Orochi

    國學院学术条目概述须佐之男击败八岐大蛇。

    The Kokugakuin academic entry summarizes Susanoo defeating Yamata no Orochi.

    Yamatanoorochi · Yamatanoorochi — Kojiki/Nihongi narrative summary · 國學院大学 Encyclopedia of Shinto 的 Yamatanoorochi 条目。条目列出的象征解释仍属于学术解释，不写入叙事事实。
  2. 02

    在蛇尾中发现神剑The sword found in the serpent's tail

    同一学术综述把草薙剑的发现放在大蛇尾部。

    The same academic summary places the discovery of Kusanagi in the serpent's tail.

    Yamatanoorochi · Yamatanoorochi — Kojiki/Nihongi narrative summary · Yamatanoorochi 条目的神剑发现段。这是文本叙事，不是考古出土记录。
  3. 03

    呈给天照大神Presented to Amaterasu

    该条目说明须佐之男把剑呈给天照大神；后续奉祀另由不同来源版本叙述。

    The entry says Susanoo presented the sword to Amaterasu; later enshrinement is handled in a different source version.

    Yamatanoorochi · Yamatanoorochi — Kojiki/Nihongi narrative summary · Yamatanoorochi 条目的呈递段。不以呈递动作自动证明所有后续持有链。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 证据卡Evidence card

风神 Fujin

日本风神入口；本批仅登记宗达屏风中的后世图像见证。

Japanese wind-deity entry currently limited to the later Sotatsu screen witness.

日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

鸣雷 Naruikazuchi

《古事记》黄泉段八雷神之一；与后世同名神社传统的关系未知。

One of the Kojiki eight thunder kami; identity with later similarly named shrine traditions is unknown.

  1. 01

    黄泉段落The Yomi passage

    学术神名索引把八位雷神定位到《古事记》上卷的黄泉段落，并说他们出现在伊邪那美身上。

    The scholarly divine-name index locates the eight thunder kami in the Yomi episode of the upper Kojiki scroll and places them on Izanami.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 《古事记》上卷黄泉段落索引。只采用公开学术索引范围。
  2. 02

    八个独立名称Eight distinct names

    数据库为大雷、火雷、黑雷、拆雷、若雷、土雷、鸣雷与伏雷分别建档，不把八名压成一个泛称。

    The database keeps Ooikazuchi, Honoikazuchi, Kuroikazuchi, Sakuikazuchi, Wakaikazuchi, Tsuchiikazuchi, Naruikazuchi, and Fusuikazuchi as separate records rather than one generic name.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 八个成员Claims及神名索引。部分名称的精细语义仍待校勘。
  3. 03

    不自动等同地方神No automatic local identification

    鸣雷、若雷等相近名称在后世神社或地方传统中可能出现，但系统不会仅凭名字相似就认定为《古事记》中的同一神。

    Similar names such as Naruikazuchi or Wakaikazuchi may occur in later shrine or local traditions, but name similarity alone does not establish identity with the Kojiki figures.

    Kojiki divine-name index · Upper Scroll, Yomi episode · 成员Claim及身份冲突档案。身份关系保留为争议或未知。
日本神道 / Japanese Shinto · 有完整故事Full story

黑雷 Kuroikazuchi

《古事记》黄泉段八雷神之一。

One of the eight thunder kami in the Kojiki Yomi episode.

  1. 01

毛利 / Māori · 有完整故事Full story

兰吉努伊 Ranginui

天父；与帕帕图阿努库从黑暗中出现，孩子们把他们分开，光明由此进入世界（Te Ara）。

Sky father who, with Papatūānuku, emerges from darkness; their children separate them and let in the light (Te Ara).

  1. 01

    从黑暗中出现Out of darkness

    Te Ara 写道：在毛利传统中，创世的核心行动是天与地的分离。故事从黑暗与虚无开始，天父兰吉努伊与地母帕帕图阿努库从中出现。起初天地紧紧相连，他们的孩子就出生在两者之间。

    Te Ara writes that in Māori tradition the central act of creation is the separation of earth and sky. The story begins in darkness and nothingness, from which Ranginui the sky father and Papatūānuku the earth mother emerge. At first earth and sky are joined, and their children are born between them.

    Māori creation traditions · Page 1 — Common threads in creation stories · Te Ara“毛利创世传统”第1页。
  2. 02

    孩子们分开父母The children part their parents

    孩子们决定把父母分开。天与地一旦分离，光就照进了世界——从黑暗走向光明，正是由孩子们的这一行动完成的。

    The children decide to separate their parents. Once earth and sky are apart, light enters the world: the move from darkness to light is achieved by the children's act.

    Māori creation traditions · Page 1 — Common threads in creation stories · 同一页面。Te Ara 说明：在部分部族版本中，塔内在分开天地时起重要作用；本站不选定某一部族的说法。
  3. 03

    自然的众神Atua of the natural world

    分离之后，孩子们成为自然各部分的神（atua）：塔内是森林之神，塔加罗阿是海洋之神，还有地震之神鲁奥莫科、风与天气之神塔维里马特阿。把这些神编进同一个谱系，是毛利人解释自然世界的传统方式。

    After the separation the children become atua of different parts of nature: Tāne of the forests, Tangaroa of the sea, Rūaumoko of earthquakes, Tāwhirimātea of winds and weather. Weaving these atua into one genealogy is the traditional Māori way of explaining the natural world.

    Māori creation traditions · Page 1 — Common threads in creation stories · 同一页面。鲁奥莫科与塔维里马特阿尚未建立独立实体。
  4. 04

    不是只有一个版本Not a single version

    Te Ara 也提醒，各部族的创世故事差异很大，特别是关于至高存在的角色。这里只讲公开页面所总结的共同线索，具名部族的版本须经相应权威授权。

    Te Ara also cautions that tribal versions vary considerably, especially on the role of a supreme being. This retelling covers only the common threads the public page summarizes; named iwi versions require permission from the appropriate authority.

    Māori creation traditions · Page 1 — Common threads in creation stories · Te Ara 故事摘要。具名 iwi／hapū 版本仍为权限阻塞。

地母；与兰吉努伊相连，直到孩子们把他们分开（Te Ara 对共同线索的概述）。

Earth mother joined to Ranginui until their children part them (Te Ara's summary of common threads).

波利尼西亚 / Polynesian · 有完整故事Full story

塔加罗阿 Tangaroa

Te Ara 所说的海洋之神，兰吉努伊与帕帕图阿努库的孩子之一。

Named by Te Ara as atua of the sea, one of the children of Ranginui and Papatūānuku.

爱尔兰 / Irish · 有完整故事Full story

布里吉德 Brigid

《第二次马格图雷德之战》中达格达之女布里格，为儿子鲁阿丹哀哭，是爱尔兰第一次听到的哀歌。

Bríg, daughter of the Dagda in Cath Maige Tuired, whose keening for her son Rúadán is the first heard in Ireland.

  1. 01

    达格达的大锅The Dagda's cauldron

    达努神族带来了四件宝物，其中一件是从穆里亚斯城带来的达格达的大锅：凡是围着它吃饭的人，从没有一个是不饱足地离开的。

    The Túatha Dé Danann brought four treasures. One was the Dagda's cauldron from the city of Murias: no company that ate from it ever went away unsatisfied.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §6。
  2. 02

    百艺之主来到塔拉The master of all arts at Tara

    鲁格来到塔拉的大门前，守门人问他会什么手艺。他报出一门又一门，而宫里每门手艺都已有人；最后他问：有没有一个人能同时精通所有这些？于是他以“百艺之主”的身份被请了进去。

    Lug comes to the door of Tara, and the doorkeeper asks what art he brings. He names one after another, and each already has a master within — until he asks whether anyone there has all of them at once. So he is admitted as the Samildánach, master of all arts.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §§53–72。
  3. 03

    萨温节的河边By the river at Samain

    萨温节前后，达格达在乌恩辛河边遇见一个女人正在洗东西，一只脚踩在南岸，一只脚踩在北岸。她就是莫莉甘。两人结合之后，她告诉达格达弗魔族将在哪里登陆，并许诺要取弗魔王因德赫的心血。

    Around Samain the Dagda meets a woman washing at the river Unshin, one foot on the southern bank and one on the northern. She is the Morrígan. After they unite she tells him where the Fomoire will land and promises to take the heart's blood of their king Indech.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §84。
  4. 04

    第一次哀歌The first keening

    布里格是达格达的女儿，她和布雷斯的儿子鲁阿丹被派去刺杀铁匠戈伊布纽，却被戈伊布纽杀死。布里格赶来为儿子哀哭，先是尖声恸哭，最后低声啜泣——这是爱尔兰第一次听到哀歌与哭泣。

    Rúadán, son of Bres and of Bríg the Dagda's daughter, is sent to kill the smith Goibniu and is killed instead. Bríg comes and keens for her son, shrieking first and weeping at the end — the first time keening and weeping were heard in Ireland.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §§123–125。文本写作 Bríg；与数据库中“布里吉德”实体的对应依学界通行用法。
  5. 05

    巴洛尔的毒眼Balor's deadly eye

    弗魔族勇士巴洛尔有一只毒眼，只在战场上睁开，要四个人用把手才能抬起眼皮。鲁格用投石一击，石头把那只眼睛从巴洛尔脑后打了出去，结果看见它的反而是他自己的军队。

    The Fomorian champion Balor has a deadly eye, opened only on the battlefield, its lid lifted by four men with a handle. Lug casts a sling stone that drives the eye out through the back of Balor's head, so that it is his own host that looks upon it.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §§133–135。
  6. 06

    莫莉甘宣告胜利The Morrígan proclaims the victory

    战斗结束后，恩玛斯的女儿莫莉甘把这场大胜宣告给爱尔兰的王者高地、神丘里的众灵、各条大河与河口，然后又说出关于未来的预言。

    When the battle is won, the Morrígan, daughter of Ernmas, proclaims the great victory to the royal heights of Ireland, its síd-hosts, its chief waters and river-mouths — and then speaks prophecy.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §§166–167。预言诗的内容未逐句建模。
爱尔兰 / Irish · 有完整故事Full story

莫莉甘 The Morrígan

恩玛斯之女；萨温节与达格达相会，许诺击杀因德赫，并在第二次马格图雷德之战后宣告胜利。

Daughter of Ernmas who meets the Dagda at Samain, promises Indech's death and proclaims the victory after the second battle of Mag Tuired.

  1. 01

    达格达的大锅The Dagda's cauldron

    达努神族带来了四件宝物，其中一件是从穆里亚斯城带来的达格达的大锅：凡是围着它吃饭的人，从没有一个是不饱足地离开的。

    The Túatha Dé Danann brought four treasures. One was the Dagda's cauldron from the city of Murias: no company that ate from it ever went away unsatisfied.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §6。
  2. 02

    百艺之主来到塔拉The master of all arts at Tara

    鲁格来到塔拉的大门前，守门人问他会什么手艺。他报出一门又一门，而宫里每门手艺都已有人；最后他问：有没有一个人能同时精通所有这些？于是他以“百艺之主”的身份被请了进去。

    Lug comes to the door of Tara, and the doorkeeper asks what art he brings. He names one after another, and each already has a master within — until he asks whether anyone there has all of them at once. So he is admitted as the Samildánach, master of all arts.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §§53–72。
  3. 03

    萨温节的河边By the river at Samain

    萨温节前后，达格达在乌恩辛河边遇见一个女人正在洗东西，一只脚踩在南岸，一只脚踩在北岸。她就是莫莉甘。两人结合之后，她告诉达格达弗魔族将在哪里登陆，并许诺要取弗魔王因德赫的心血。

    Around Samain the Dagda meets a woman washing at the river Unshin, one foot on the southern bank and one on the northern. She is the Morrígan. After they unite she tells him where the Fomoire will land and promises to take the heart's blood of their king Indech.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §84。
  4. 04

    第一次哀歌The first keening

    布里格是达格达的女儿，她和布雷斯的儿子鲁阿丹被派去刺杀铁匠戈伊布纽，却被戈伊布纽杀死。布里格赶来为儿子哀哭，先是尖声恸哭，最后低声啜泣——这是爱尔兰第一次听到哀歌与哭泣。

    Rúadán, son of Bres and of Bríg the Dagda's daughter, is sent to kill the smith Goibniu and is killed instead. Bríg comes and keens for her son, shrieking first and weeping at the end — the first time keening and weeping were heard in Ireland.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §§123–125。文本写作 Bríg；与数据库中“布里吉德”实体的对应依学界通行用法。
  5. 05

    巴洛尔的毒眼Balor's deadly eye

    弗魔族勇士巴洛尔有一只毒眼，只在战场上睁开，要四个人用把手才能抬起眼皮。鲁格用投石一击，石头把那只眼睛从巴洛尔脑后打了出去，结果看见它的反而是他自己的军队。

    The Fomorian champion Balor has a deadly eye, opened only on the battlefield, its lid lifted by four men with a handle. Lug casts a sling stone that drives the eye out through the back of Balor's head, so that it is his own host that looks upon it.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §§133–135。
  6. 06

    莫莉甘宣告胜利The Morrígan proclaims the victory

    战斗结束后，恩玛斯的女儿莫莉甘把这场大胜宣告给爱尔兰的王者高地、神丘里的众灵、各条大河与河口，然后又说出关于未来的预言。

    When the battle is won, the Morrígan, daughter of Ernmas, proclaims the great victory to the royal heights of Ireland, its síd-hosts, its chief waters and river-mouths — and then speaks prophecy.

    Cath Maige Tuired: The Second Battle of Mag Tuired (English translation) · §§6–167 · Gray 本 §§166–167。预言诗的内容未逐句建模。

达格达 The Dagda

《第二次马格图雷德之战》中拥有“无人离去不饱足”之大锅的神；战前在乌恩辛河边与莫莉甘相会。

In Cath Maige Tuired, owner of a cauldron from which no company left unsatisfied; meets the Morrígan at the Unshin before the battle.

    达格达的大锅The Dagda's cauldron

鲁格 Lugh

“百艺之主”，在《第二次马格图雷德之战》中用投石击杀巴洛尔。

The Samildánach, master of all arts, who in Cath Maige Tuired kills Balor with a sling stone.

    达格达的大锅The Dagda's cauldron

玛雅 / Maya · 有完整故事Full story

伊察姆纳 Itzamna

年老的天神伊察姆纳（Itzamnaaj），有一个鸟形化身，在玛雅太初神话中地位重要（大都会博物馆，2022）。

Aged celestial god Itzamnaaj with an avian avatar, important in Maya primordial myths (Met, 2022).

  1. 01

    年老的天神The aged god

    2022 年的展览按“众神的一生”展开：从婴儿、壮年到老年。其中的老年之神叫伊察姆纳（Itzamnaaj），他还有一个鸟形化身，两者在太初神话中都很重要。

    The 2022 exhibition followed the gods through life — infancy, prime and old age. The aged god is Itzamnaaj, who has a bird avatar; both play important roles in primordial myths.

    Lives of the Gods: Divinity in Maya Art — exhibition announcement · 2022 exhibition description · 大都会艺术博物馆 2022 年展览说明。
  2. 02

    名字仍有疑问A name still in question

    “Itzamnaaj”是殖民时期尤卡坦一位重要神祇的名字。博物馆提醒，古典期这位老神的名字符号该怎样读，学界还没有定论。

    'Itzamnaaj' is the name of a major deity in colonial Yucatán. The museum notes that how to read the Classic-period name glyph of this elderly god is still uncertain.

    Lives of the Gods: Divinity in Maya Art — exhibition announcement · 2022 exhibition description · 大都会艺术博物馆展览说明。名字读法未定，本站同时保留“伊察姆纳”与“Itzamnaaj”。
  3. 03

    故事还要等更多见证The story awaits more witnesses

    这里依据的是一份现代展览介绍，而不是古代文本。展览说伊察姆纳在太初神话中很重要，但没有在说明里讲出那些神话；本站因此不替它补写情节。

    This version rests on a modern exhibition text, not an ancient one. The exhibition says Itzamnaaj mattered in primordial myths but does not retell them, so the site does not fill in a plot.

    Lives of the Gods: Divinity in Maya Art — exhibition announcement · 2022 exhibition description · 大都会艺术博物馆展览说明。待找到可定位的古代或殖民时期文本见证后再扩充。
玛雅 / Maya · 有完整故事Full story

库库尔坎 Kukulkan

与奇琴伊察“库库尔坎神庙”相连的羽蛇之名；联合国教科文组织称其为图拉统治者“羽蛇”名号的玛雅译名（通行说法）。

Feathered-serpent name linked to El Castillo, the 'Temple of Kukulkan' at Chichen-Itza; UNESCO reports it as the Maya rendering of a Tula ruler's Quetzalcoatl name.

  1. 01

    奇琴伊察的金字塔A pyramid at Chichen-Itza

    奇琴伊察是尤卡坦半岛上的前西班牙时期古城，已列入世界遗产名录。联合国教科文组织的记录把卡斯蒂略金字塔列为城市北部建筑群中的主要建筑。

    Chichen-Itza is a pre-Hispanic city on the Yucatán peninsula, inscribed on the World Heritage List. UNESCO's record names El Castillo among the chief buildings of the city's northern group.

    Pre-Hispanic City of Chichen-Itza · World Heritage property 483 — brief synthesis · 联合国教科文组织世界遗产 483 号记录。
  2. 02

    库库尔坎神庙The Temple of Kukulkan

    奇琴伊察北区最重要的建筑是卡斯蒂略金字塔，联合国教科文组织的记录说，它又被称为“库库尔坎神庙”。

    The chief monument of Chichen-Itza's northern group is El Castillo, which UNESCO's record says is also known as the Temple of Kukulkan.

    Pre-Hispanic City of Chichen-Itza · World Heritage property 483 — brief synthesis · 联合国教科文组织世界遗产 483 号记录。
  3. 03

    一个名字的翻译A name in translation

    同一记录提到一种最常见的说法：图拉之王“一芦苇·托皮尔钦·羽蛇”占领了这座城市，而“库库尔坎”正是玛雅人对“羽蛇”这个名字的翻译。

    The same record cites the most common version: the King of Tula, Ce Acatl Topiltzin Quetzalcoatl, took the city — and 'Kukulkan' is how the Maya translated his name.

    Pre-Hispanic City of Chichen-Itza · World Heritage property 483 — brief synthesis · 联合国教科文组织世界遗产 483 号记录。这是关于统治者名号的传统说法，不把玛雅与墨西加的神合并为同一实体。
玛雅 / Maya · 有完整故事Full story

恰克 Chaac

玛雅雨神，古典期象形文字写作 Chahk；大都会博物馆的陶盘上他半身浸在水中。

Maya rain god, written Chahk in Classic hieroglyphs; shown waist-deep in water on a Met plate.

  1. 01

    水中的雨神The rain god in the water

    这只古典期的玛雅陶盘上，雨神恰克半身浸在水波纹里，四周写着表示“海”的象形文字。

    On this Classic Maya plate the rain god Chahk stands waist-deep in watery motifs, surrounded by hieroglyphs for 'sea'.

    Plate with the god Chahk · 2021.320 — catalogue description · 大都会艺术博物馆 2021.320 号。
  2. 02

    Chahk 这个名字The name Chahk

    在古典期的玛雅象形文字里，这位雨神的名字写作 Chahk；人们常写的“恰克”就是这个名字。

    In Classic-period Maya hieroglyphs the rain god's name is written Chahk — the familiar 'Chaac'.

    Plate with the god Chahk · 2021.320 — catalogue description · 大都会艺术博物馆藏品说明。
  3. 03

    进入博物馆Into the museum

    这只陶盘在 2021 年由 Mol 收藏捐赠给大都会艺术博物馆。博物馆的说明只描述画面本身，并没有讲述一个完整的雨神故事。

    The plate entered The Metropolitan Museum of Art in 2021 as a gift of the Mol Collection. The museum's entry describes the image itself; it does not narrate a full rain-god myth.

    Plate with the god Chahk · 2021.320 — catalogue description · 大都会艺术博物馆藏品出处说明。这是物质见证，本站不补写神话情节。
琐罗亚斯德传统 / Zoroastrian · 有完整故事Full story

安格拉·曼纽 Angra Mainyu

《万迪达德》第一章中“全然是死亡”的存在，对阿胡拉·马兹达创造的每一片良地施以反创造的灾祸。

In Videvdad 1, 'all death', who answers each good land made by Ahura Mazda with a counter-created plague.

  1. 01

    第一片良地The first good land

    阿胡拉·马兹达说：我所创造的良地中，第一片是雅利安瓦伊贾，在善河岱提亚旁边。

    Ahura Mazda says: the first of the good lands I created was Airyana Vaejah, by the good river Daitya.

    Vendidad, Fargard 1, translated by James Darmesteter · Fargard 1 · 第一章第一片良地。雅利安瓦伊贾不映射为现代坐标。
  2. 02

    全然是死亡的对手The one who is all death

    随后，“全然是死亡”的安格拉·曼纽来了，他针对这片良地造出了河中之蛇和寒冬，那是恶魔们的作品。

    Then came Angra Mainyu, who is all death, and against it he counter-created the serpent in the river and winter, a work of the daevas.

    Vendidad, Fargard 1, translated by James Darmesteter · Fargard 1 · 第一章第一片良地。
  3. 03

    十六地，十六灾Sixteen lands, sixteen plagues

    名单一片接一片地继续：第二片是粟特人居住的平原，安格拉·曼纽就造出蝗虫。如此一共列出十六片良地，每一片都对应一种反创造的灾祸。

    The list goes on land by land: the second is the plain where the Sughdhas dwell, and Angra Mainyu makes the locust. In all, sixteen good lands are named, each answered by a counter-created plague.

    Vendidad, Fargard 1, translated by James Darmesteter · Fargard 1 · 第一章第二片良地及以下。译者认为这是宗教地理，混合了神话之地与真实国家；只有部分地名可以确认。
琐罗亚斯德传统 / Zoroastrian · 有完整故事Full story

密特拉 Mithra

《密赫尔颂》中的“广阔牧场之主”：阿胡拉·马兹达所造、与其同受崇拜，守护契约，有千耳万眼。

Lord of wide pastures in the Mihr Yasht: created by Ahura Mazda as worthy of worship as himself, guardian of the contract, with a thousand ears and ten thousand eyes.

  1. 01

    与造物主同样尊贵As worthy as his maker

    颂歌一开头，阿胡拉·马兹达对查拉图斯特拉说：我创造广阔牧场之主密特拉时，使他与我自己一样，值得献祭、值得祈祷。

    The hymn opens with Ahura Mazda telling Zarathustra: when I created Mithra, lord of wide pastures, I made him as worthy of sacrifice and as worthy of prayer as myself.

    Mihr Yasht (Yasht 10), translated by James Darmesteter · Yt 10.1–13 · Yt 10.1。
  2. 02

    不可背弃契约Never break the contract

    对密特拉说谎、背弃契约的恶人，会给整个国家带来死亡。所以契约绝不可背弃——不论是与信徒订立的，还是与不信者订立的。

    The wretch who lies to Mithra, breaking the contract, brings death upon the whole land. So the contract must never be broken, whether made with one of the faithful or with an unbeliever.

    Mihr Yasht (Yasht 10), translated by James Darmesteter · Yt 10.1–13 · Yt 10.2。“契约”是译者对 miθra 一词的理解，学界另有讨论。
  3. 03

    千耳万眼A thousand ears, ten thousand eyes

    颂歌赞美密特拉说真话，是集会中的首领；他有一千只耳朵，一万只眼睛，什么都瞒不过他。

    The hymn praises Mithra as truth-speaking, a chief in assemblies, with a thousand ears and ten thousand eyes — nothing escapes him.

    Mihr Yasht (Yasht 10), translated by James Darmesteter · Yt 10.1–13 · Yt 10.7。
  4. 04

    越过哈拉山Over Mount Hara

    每天清晨，他是天上诸神中第一个越过哈拉山的，比不朽的、驾着快马的太阳还要早。

    Each morning he is the first of the heavenly gods to reach over Mount Hara, ahead of the undying, swift-horsed sun.

    Mihr Yasht (Yasht 10), translated by James Darmesteter · Yt 10.1–13 · Yt 10.13。

阿胡拉·马兹达 Ahura Mazda

  1. 01

    与造物主同样尊贵As worthy as his maker

    颂歌一开头，阿胡拉·马兹达对查拉图斯特拉说：我创造广阔牧场之主密特拉时，使他与我自己一样，值得献祭、值得祈祷。

  2. 02

    不可背弃契约Never break the contract

    对密特拉说谎、背弃契约的恶人，会给整个国家带来死亡。所以契约绝不可背弃——不论是与信徒订立的，还是与不信者订立的。

    The wretch who lies to Mithra, breaking the contract, brings death upon the whole land. So the contract must never be broken, whether made with one of the faithful or with an unbeliever.

    Mihr Yasht (Yasht 10), translated by James Darmesteter · Yt 10.1–13 · Yt 10.2。“契约”是译者对 miθra 一词的理解，学界另有讨论。
  3. 03

    千耳万眼A thousand ears, ten thousand eyes

    颂歌赞美密特拉说真话，是集会中的首领；他有一千只耳朵，一万只眼睛，什么都瞒不过他。

    The hymn praises Mithra as truth-speaking, a chief in assemblies, with a thousand ears and ten thousand eyes — nothing escapes him.

    Mihr Yasht (Yasht 10), translated by James Darmesteter · Yt 10.1–13 · Yt 10.7。
  4. 04

    越过哈拉山Over Mount Hara

    每天清晨，他是天上诸神中第一个越过哈拉山的，比不朽的、驾着快马的太阳还要早。

    Each morning he is the first of the heavenly gods to reach over Mount Hara, ahead of the undying, swift-horsed sun.

    Mihr Yasht (Yasht 10), translated by James Darmesteter · Yt 10.1–13 · Yt 10.13。

奥洛杜马雷 Olodumare

活态约鲁巴传统中的至高存在；在具名社区权威授权之前，本站不发布故事。

Supreme being in living Yorùbá tradition; no public story is published here until a named community authority grants scope.

    这位神祇属于活态传统。在具名社区权威授权并写明可用范围之前，本站不发布故事，也不替社区讲述。Living tradition. Permitted: Name, civilization link and a note that no public story has been published here. Not collected: Oríkì, Ifá verses, ritual and lineage narratives without named community authorization.

    奥舒恩 Oshun

    约鲁巴女神；联合国教科文组织记录把她与奥孙-奥索博神树林相连；活态信仰中的叙事需社区许可。

    Yoruba goddess whom UNESCO's record links to the Osun-Osogbo Sacred Grove; living-practice narratives require community permission.

    1. 01

      奥舒恩——职掌：fertility

      UNESCO's description calls Osun a goddess of fertility.

      Osun-Osogbo Sacred Grove · Brief synthesis
    2. 02

      奥舒恩在奥孙-奥索博神树林受到崇拜。

      UNESCO describes the Osun Sacred Grove as the abode of Osun, a Yoruba goddess of fertility, its landscape and river dotted with sanctuaries, shrines, sculptures and artworks in her honour.

      Osun-Osogbo Sacred Grove · Brief synthesis
    此处只使用公开记录；活态传统中的叙事、祷词与仪式须经社区授权。Not collected without permission: Narratives, praise poetry, ritual and festival detail drawn from living practice.
    约鲁巴 / Yoruba · 需社区授权Permission required

      尚戈 Shango

      奥约约鲁巴活态传统中的 Ṣàngó：雷、闪电、正义、王权与祖先记忆公共层；受限知识只登记访问边界，不收集秘密内容。

      Sango in the living Oyo Yoruba tradition, with public thunder, justice, kingship and ancestor-memory layers; restricted knowledge is represented only by access metadata.

      1. 01

        尚戈与奥约尚戈节相关。

        The annual Oyo Sango Festival is a public living-tradition expression centered on Sango.

        Sango Festival, Oyo · ICH 01974
      2. 02

        尚戈与尚戈双斧祭仪杖相关。

        Museum records identify Ose Sango as a double-axe-topped sacred or devotional staff associated with Sango.

        Ose Sango staff · Met object 1983.603.5
      3. 03

        尚戈与雷相关。

        The community-backed UNESCO Sango Festival dossier publicly identifies Sango with thunder, lightning, justice and Oyo kingship/ancestor memory.

        Sango Festival, Oyo · ICH 01974 public dossier
      苏美尔 / Sumerian · 有完整故事Full story

      乌图 Utu

      太阳神；吉尔伽美什向他求助，他赐下勇士为远征胡瓦瓦引路（ETCSL 1.8.1.5）。

      Sun god whom Gilgameš asks for help; he grants warriors to guide the expedition against Ḫuwawa (ETCSL 1.8.1.5).

      1. 01

        要进山去To the mountains

        吉尔伽美什决定动身进山。

        Gilgameš resolves to set off for the mountains.

        Gilgameš and Ḫuwawa (Version A) · ETCSL 1.8.1.5 · ETCSL 1.8.1.5 开篇。
      2. 02

        先告诉乌图First tell Utu

        恩奇都说：山是乌图管的，这件事应该先让乌图知道。

        Enkidu says the mountains are Utu's concern, so Utu should be told of the plan.

        Gilgameš and Ḫuwawa (Version A) · ETCSL 1.8.1.5 · ETCSL 1.8.1.5 开篇。
      3. 03

        眼泪换来的向导Guides granted for tears

        吉尔伽美什向乌图祈求。乌图接受他的眼泪作为礼物，赐给他七位勇士为他引路，一同去面对胡瓦瓦。

        Gilgameš prays to Utu, who accepts his tears as a gift and grants him seven warriors to guide him toward Ḫuwawa.

        Gilgameš and Ḫuwawa (Version A) · ETCSL 1.8.1.5 · ETCSL 1.8.1.5 乌图赐助段落。七位勇士的具体身份描述尚未逐句建模。
      苏美尔 / Sumerian · 有完整故事Full story

      伊南娜 Inanna

      1. 01

        把心意转向地下Turning toward the world below

        作品1.4.1开篇说伊南娜把心意转向地下世界，并离开天与地展开下降。

        Composition 1.4.1 opens with Inana setting her mind on the world below and leaving heaven and earth to descend.

        Inana's Descent to the Netherworld · Composition 1.4.1 · ETCSL作品1.4.1开篇及事件参与Claim。中文为独立转述，不复制ETCSL现代译文。
      2. 02

        冥界事件The netherworld event

        数据库目前确认伊南娜是下降事件的参与者，并将其归为冥界旅程；门、裁决与复生阶段仍待逐行Claims。

        The database currently confirms Inana as the participant in a descent event classified as an underworld journey; the gates, judgment, and revival still need line-level claims.

        Inana's Descent to the Netherworld · Composition 1.4.1 · ETCSL作品1.4.1与现有事件档案。细节未完成结构化。
      3. 03

        替代者段落仍待扩张The substitute sequence remains open

        作品后段与伊南娜返回及替代者有关，但v0.25不在没有逐行证据链接时把整段写成已完成版本。

        The later composition concerns Inanas return and a substitute, but v0.25 does not present the whole sequence as complete without line-level evidence links.

        Inana's Descent to the Netherworld · Composition 1.4.1 · ETCSL完整作品链接已登记。明确显示为资料缺口。

      南纳 Nanna

      月神；带着礼物乘船前往父亲恩利尔的城市尼普尔，带回给乌尔的祝福（ETCSL 1.5.1）。

      Moon god who sails with gifts to Nibru, the city of his father Enlil, and returns with blessings for Urim (ETCSL 1.5.1).

      1. 01

        想念父亲的城市Longing for his father's city

        英勇的南纳一心想着父亲的城市——尼普尔。

        Heroic Nanna-Suen sets his mind on his father's city, Nibru.

        Nanna-Suen's journey to Nibru · ETCSL 1.5.1 · ETCSL 1.5.1 开篇。
      2. 02

        满船的礼物A boat full of gifts

        他准备了一条船，装满各种礼物，顺着河道一路航行，前往尼普尔。

        He prepares a boat, loads it with gifts and sails along the waterways toward Nibru.

        Nanna-Suen's journey to Nibru · ETCSL 1.5.1 · ETCSL 1.5.1。沿途停靠的城市尚未逐项登记。
      3. 03

        带回乌尔的祝福Blessings for Urim

        恩利尔欢迎儿子的到来，赐给他丰饶的物产，让他带回自己的城市乌尔。

        Enlil welcomes his son and grants him abundance to take back to his own city, Urim.

        Nanna-Suen's journey to Nibru · ETCSL 1.5.1 · ETCSL 1.5.1 结尾。学者把这首诗与城市间运送供品的仪式相联系，属于解释层。

      埃列什基伽勒 Ereshkigal

      1. 01

      苏美尔 / Sumerian · 有完整故事Full story

      宁胡尔萨格 Ninhursag

      恩基吃下迪尔蒙的植物后诅咒他、之后又生出众神为他治病的女神（ETCSL 1.1.1）。

      Goddess who curses Enki after he eats the plants of Dilmun and later brings forth deities to heal him (ETCSL 1.1.1).

      1. 01

        纯净的迪尔蒙Pure Dilmun

        故事开头描写迪尔蒙：一片纯净、安宁的土地，一切都还处在最初的状态。

        The composition opens with Dilmun, a pure and peaceful land where everything is still in its first state.

        Enki and Ninhursaĝa · ETCSL 1.1.1 · ETCSL 1.1.1 开篇。神话中的迪尔蒙不等同于历史上的同名地区。
      2. 02

        吃下植物，招来诅咒Eating the plants brings a curse

        恩基把那里长出的植物一一吃掉。宁胡尔萨格大怒，诅咒他，恩基于是病倒了。

        Enki eats the plants that have grown there, one after another. Ninhursaĝa, enraged, curses him, and he falls ill.

        Enki and Ninhursaĝa · ETCSL 1.1.1 · ETCSL 1.1.1 植物与诅咒段落。具体植物名单尚未逐项建模。
      3. 03

        为每一处病痛生出神A deity for every ailment

        后来宁胡尔萨格回到恩基身边。他哪里疼，她就为那里生出一位神灵，就这样把他治好了。

        Later Ninhursaĝa returns to Enki. For each part of his body that aches, she brings forth a deity, and so he is healed.

        Enki and Ninhursaĝa · ETCSL 1.1.1 · ETCSL 1.1.1 疗愈段落。
      苏美尔 / Sumerian · 证据卡Evidence card

      2. 02

      阿卡德 / Akkadian · 有完整故事Full story

      阿卡德《安祖神话》中的宁努尔塔 Ninurta in the Akkadian Anzu myth

      本档聚焦《安祖神话》中击败安祖并收回命运泥板的角色。

      Dossier scoped to Ninurta’s role in the Akkadian Anzu myth.

      1. 01

        命运泥板被夺The Tablet is stolen

        ORACC的学术纲要把安祖写成夺走命运泥板的对手。

        The ORACC scholarly synopsis presents Anzu as the antagonist who steals the Tablet of Destinies.

        Ninurta, god of victory — Anzu narrative · Anzu narrative synopsis · ORACC Anzu narrative synopsis。本站不是泥板全文译本。
      2. 02

        宁努尔塔出战Ninurta fights

        宁努尔塔在登记的叙事纲要中成为获胜的神圣英雄，并击败安祖。

        Ninurta becomes the victorious divine hero in the registered synopsis and defeats Anzu.

        Ninurta, god of victory — Anzu narrative · Anzu narrative synopsis · ORACC Anzu narrative synopsis。战斗动作的逐行细节未在当前层展开。
      3. 03

        泥板被取回The Tablet is recovered

        故事以宁努尔塔取回命运泥板作为当前证据层的结果。

        The current evidence layer ends with Ninurta recovering the Tablet of Destinies.

        Ninurta, god of victory — Anzu narrative · Anzu narrative synopsis · ORACC Anzu narrative synopsis。不同安祖文本版本将在后续分别建档。
